Chapter 1151: 1150 The Worldly Venerable Observes the Future

"What, you were constables this whole time?" Yue Changhe was taken aback.

"I always thought you were hunting me down because I carry the bloodlines of both the Human race and the Demon race. The two powerful forces you represent must have valued the rarity of my bloodline so much that you were sent to capture me and subject me to inhuman experiments, or to use me as a pivot in the relationship between the Human race and the Demon race."

Originally, when Yue Changhe saw the man with the eagle eyes and the hulking monk approaching fiercely, demanding to know whether he was from the Human race or the Demon race and insisting he make a choice, he knew they meant business, so he ran for his life.

The man with the eagle eyes hissed, "It seems your understanding isn’t exactly wrong."

Lu Yang nodded secretly; he had thought the same and had stepped forward intending to say a few words to smooth things over.

But there was no need for Yue Changhe with his small frame to be the pivot between the two races; the pivot now was the "East Sea Economic Alliance" between Great Xia, Buddha Country, Demon Territory, and the East Sea.

Back when he was still recovering in bed, he had been a witness to the alliance treaty discussed by Emperor Xia, Zhu Tian, and Ao Ling.

After learning of the treaty, the Buddha Country took the initiative to join.

"Nonsense, I explained the reason when I first saw you; you’re evading arrest!" the big monk shouted angrily. He had crossed nearly half of the Buddha Country in pursuit of Yue Changhe and had even worn through two pairs of cloth shoes. Even he was losing his temper.

"You clearly said you were taking me to Western Heaven!"

"What I said was that I would take you to the Western Paradise Temple. It was you who ran off before I could finish explaining!"

Yue Changhe scratched his head awkwardly; it wasn’t his fault they had chosen such a misleading name for their temple.

"Wait, that’s still not right. What crime did I commit for you to capture me?"

"You kidnapped the little princess of the Nine-tailed Fox Clan, and you still deny it?"

"I kidnapped that little girl?" Yue Changhe’s eyes widened, his face expressing an "I knew you bald-headed monks had it in for me" look.

He wasn’t afraid to confront the big monk since the living Buddha was present.

"It was that little girl who snuck out of her clan herself, ran all the way from the Demon Territory to the Buddha Country. When she left, she didn’t even carry a single Spirit Stone. If I hadn’t found her, she might have starved to death."

"That little girl is really hard to please; she eats nothing but delicacies from the mountains and the sea. She nearly made me go broke."

"Later, she told me she was the little princess of the Nine-tailed Fox Clan and asked me to take her back. She said she would pay me back tenfold or a hundredfold."

"The thing is, my parents secretly gave birth to me, so I’m not officially registered. I couldn’t leave the Buddha Country through proper channels. So, I had the little girl write to her family herself, asking them to send someone to pick her up quickly."

The man with the eagle eyes showed an intrigued expression: "Did you see what the Nine-tailed Fox Clan’s little princess wrote in her letter?"

"No, why? As long as the Nine-tailed Fox people took her away, that’s all that mattered."

"Mommy and Daddy, I’m living at a big brother’s home in Buddha Country, and I am safe. The big brother said he’s ’black-something,’ has a special identity, and can’t leave Buddha Country. He asked me to write to you to take me home. Oh, and when you come, bring lots and lots of Spirit Stones."

"...wait, so the Nine-tailed Fox Clan thought I had kidnapped that little girl? Why didn’t they do anything when they came to pick her up?!"

Yue Changhe was astonished; the Nine-tailed Foxes were a major clan in the Demon Territory, they could crush him with a single fingertip.

The eagle-eyed man put away the letter and seriously said, "Initially, when the Nine-tailed Fox Clan took in the little princess, they thought about taking action against you since your strength was too low. But upon reflection, considering your low cultivation level, you surely weren’t the main culprit. Perhaps there was an ambush around, so they took the money and hurriedly concluded the affair."

"Didn’t that little girl properly explain to her family?" Yue Changhe gritted his teeth in anger, resolving to thoroughly discipline that little wench when he saw her again!

"It is said that the little princess awakened her second tail on the way back and has been in a deep slumber ever since, yet to awaken," the eagle-eyed man’s tone grew stranger as he spoke, suggesting that what Yue Changhe said might not be false.

Lu Yang had heard of the Nine-tailed Fox Clan. The foxes in the clan were not born with nine tails; rather, they awakened them gradually. The more tails one awakened, the higher their status in the Fox Clan.

There were three methods of awakening: one was innate talent, where one would naturally awaken once they reached a certain age; another was external interference, such as consuming Heaven and Earth Treasures or undergoing the Fox Clan’s trials; and the third was a change in one’s mind state, such as experiencing the blossoming of first love.

"Little Changhe, so you are here."

An old, decrepit voice reached everyone’s ears. A hunched-old man appeared in the temple, leaning on a cane. Aside from Immortal Fairy, Lu Yang, Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, Qinghe, Jin Caiwei, and Yun Mengmeng, no one knew how the hunched elder had appeared.

The elder ignored the crowd, strolling leisurely in the Ancestral Temple as he circled around Yue Changhe, sighing wistfully, "The resemblance is uncanny. He looks just like young Yue Fang did back in the day."

Yue Changhe was on high alert, staring fixedly at the old man, poised to attack at any moment as he slowly uttered two words, "Grandfather."

"If you hand over the jade pendant your father left you, considering you have called me ’Grandfather’, I might overlook the filthy human blood flowing in your veins and spare your life."

"Spare my life?" Yue Changhe scoffed.

"You killed my father over that jade pendant, and now you think you can let me live!"

The hunched elder shook his head in disappointment, "You’re as stubborn as your father. Having had a streak of fortune, you no longer put our clan in your eyes. You fell in love with a human woman and sullied the bloodline of our mighty race."

"Humans are the leaders of all spirits, and the Demon Race also has its True Immortals. There is no superiority or inferiority in bloodline..."

The echo of Lu Yang’s previous words reverberated in the temple, startling the eagle-eyed man and the Great Monk.

It turned out that when the Worldly Venerable uttered those words, he wasn’t addressing them, but rather the hunched elder.

To overlook the past and predict the future—is this a glimpse of the Venerate’s extraordinary power?

"Who is speaking!" The hunched elder’s skin crawled as he could not pinpoint the source of the voice.

"Daring to fool around before me, utterly ignorant of life and death!" The elder erupted in rage, unleashing the imposing pressure of Combined Great Ability. His race was born with immense strength; few cultivators of the same level could match his power!

"Goodness, Benefactor, to kill your own offspring is truly devoid of all conscience."

Lu Yang extended a finger, placing it on the hunched elder’s forehead. The elder remained immovable.

In the elder’s eyes, that was not merely a finger, but rather the sky collapsing, smashing the temple and ruthlessly crushing him.

"My dear grandson, so you were hiding here. You really gave your grandfather quite the search."

A ghastly voice drifted in as another figure appeared in the Ancestral Temple.

"Hand over the cultivation technique your mother left you, and I can leave you with an intact corpse to avoid ending up with no remains, just like your mother."

The eagle-eyed man and the Great Monk simultaneously recalled the first thing Lu Yang said when he appeared:

The monk was unwilling to reveal himself to the world, but seeing as three benefactors have entered the temple with a dispute, it was only right for the monk to intervene.

Could it be that the Worldly Venerable was not referring to the three of them, but rather to Yue Changhe and his paternal and maternal grandfathers?!

Chapter 1152: 1151: Buddha Says It’s Ineffable

Yue Changhe’s grandfather soon followed in his father’s footsteps; the two men stood side by side, motionless, like a pair of statues.

These two had blood on their hands, and the monk and the eagle-eyed man, receiving one each, captured them to settle the matters, making the journey worthwhile.

Considering how ruthless these two elderly men appeared, it was likely they had committed more than one or two crimes.

At first, when the monk and the eagle-eyed man met Lu Yang, calling him Worldly Venerable was just politeness, but as things developed, they truly believed Lu Yang was the Worldly Venerable.

With an ability to predict the future and never denying the title of Worldly Venerable, it seemed he might indeed be the true Worldly Venerable!

Even if he wasn’t, he must be a supreme being closely related to the Worldly Venerable.

Thinking of this, the monk trembled with excitement, never dreaming he would one day meet the Worldly Venerable.

Lu Yang breathed a sigh of relief, thankful that Yue Changhe’s relatives kept coming one after another to cause trouble—such good people.

Had he inadvertently grown to the extent that he could solve problems without the help of an Immortal?

Kneeling on the ground, Yue Changhe bowed reverently to the Worldly Venerable and asked sincerely, "Worldly Venerable, what exactly is inside this jade pendant?"

The Worldly Venerable smiled inscrutably and replied, "Indescribable."

Yue Changhe looked up and met the Worldly Venerable’s weathered gaze. His heart trembled, feeling as if he had grasped the meaning, yet understood nothing at all.

In that moment, Yue Changhe felt profoundly fortunate; he understood that his fate with the Worldly Venerable was complete. If the Worldly Venerable were to reveal anything further, it might damage his own destiny.

Having the Worldly Venerable enlighten him and handle his two major enemies was already an enormous stroke of fate; he dared not ask for more.

In fact, Lu Yang had recognized at a glance that the jade pendant was a legacy left by a Crossing Tribulation Cultivator—it was quite a decent artifact.

However, stating some things too clearly would strip them of their mystique.

...

"Brother Eagle, when you’re free, come to Buddha Country. I’ll treat you to mush," the monk said as he bid farewell to the eagle-eyed man at the border.

Thinking of the taste of mush, the eagle-eyed man hastily waved his hand, "No thanks, no thanks. Better you come to Demon Nation, and I’ll treat you."

The monk took Yue Changhe’s grandfather to Western Paradise Temple, confirmed his identity, and then immediately reported the matter concerning Soybean Temple, until even Abbot Ming Yu was informed.

"What, the Worldly Venerable has appeared and is well-versed in time?"

Master Ming Yu’s first thought was that this was the act of Sui Yue Venerable; however, he quickly realized that was unlikely since the Venerable acted discretely and wouldn’t let news spread.

In Mahavira Hall, Sui Yue Venerable listened to Master Ming Yu’s report, his expression strange, and after holding back for a while, eight words burst forth.

"An old acquaintance’s deed, let him be."

After all, Lu Yang could be considered a Future Buddha, so calling him the Worldly Venerable wasn’t wrong.

Originally, the incident of Soybean Temple only spread among a few nearby cities, but ever since Western Paradise Temple received the news, the legend of Soybean Temple silently spread throughout Buddha Country.

As more and more people learned of the existence of Soybean Temple, Lu Yang was pleased to set a rule that allowed Soybean Temple to appear randomly anywhere in Buddha Country, thus adding an extra layer of mystery. It was all up to chance whether people would encounter Soybean Temple.

Although only a very few had ever entered Soybean Temple, everyone who came out had gained a tremendous opportunity—even Integration Period Cultivators benefited greatly, saying they had glimpsed the path to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

People said that anyone who entered Soybean Temple was recognized by the Worldly Venerable, who could grant any of their wishes.

Lu Yang hadn’t bestowed the opportunity for Soybean Temple to gain fame; rather, it was to train his skill in using rules. Fame was an unexpected result.

"But I see you smiling quite happily after you became famous." Immortal Fairy was puzzled. Ever since Soybean Temple had become well-known, Lu Yang would occasionally head out to a teahouse, order a pot of tea, and quietly listen to the discussions of worldly people.

For example, Lu Yang was currently sitting in a teahouse, sipping tea that had been brewed several times and had lost its flavor.

"This is a smile of joy from helping others," Lu Yang earnestly explained.

"Have you heard? Soybean Temple can also help people conceive. Mr. Song’s wife has a connection with Buddha, and after visiting Soybean Temple, her belly grew quite quickly."

Lu Yang’s smile froze on his face. Spreading rumors, huh? Who exactly was Mr. Song’s wife, and since when did Soybean Temple deal with fertility matters?!

Although he occasionally heard things in the teahouse that were not related to Soybean Temple, those were minor issues that Lu Yang could selectively forget.

"Here, I lay down the [Rule: In the teahouse, I cannot hear any rumors about Soybean Temple]."

Now he only heard good things.

"What a wonderful place Buddha Country is."

Lu Yang stretched lazily. The teahouse wasn’t a particularly interesting place, and after sitting there for a day, he felt a bit bored.

It was only while in the teahouse that he would casually stop rampaging carriages, teach lessons to arrogant young ladies, watch the top two warriors on the martial leaderboard duel, catch thieves trying to plant stolen goods in his hands and falsely accuse him, and reveal his immortal powers to clear his name, among other things.

Upon returning to Soybean Temple, he saw Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi eagerly inviting him to join them in plotting something significant.

"Senior Brother Lu, seeing that you’ve almost mastered your rule cultivation, should the three of us join forces to practice some techniques to deal with my husband?"

"Ah?"

Lu Yang broke out in a cold sweat. He was all for teaching Qilin Immortal a lesson, but please don’t drag him into it!

He quietly stepped back, offering an apologetic smile, "Perhaps it’s best if I don’t get involved in family matters?"

"How can you consider yourself an outsider, Little Yang? You are Xiao Ling and Lianyi’s senior brother. You’re definitely one of us!" Immortal Fairy felt Little Yang was being too modest.

Lu Yang shook his head regretfully, "Honestly, although I now possess an immortal body, I still can’t control its power well. If the three of us were to practice together, I might accidentally hurt someone."

"For example, look at this Buddha’s Handprint!"

Lu Yang shouted loudly, and a massive Buddha’s hand descended from the sky, vast and boundless, with countless temples sitting in its palm, and the sound of Buddha echoing endlessly.

Then, the Buddha’s handprint disintegrated in mid-air, breaking into pieces that fell onto the desert causing the earth to tremble.

A small fragment falling on a city could destroy it.

"Or take the Three Flavored True Fire!"

Lu Yang exhaled the Three Flavored True Fire, scorching the desert into a glassy state. Anyone tasting it could discern different flavors.

"Originally, I only intended to scorch a hundred miles of desert, but I accidentally ended up burning a thousand miles," Lu Yang shook his head, feeling quite dejected.

"Or like Heaven and Earth in an Inch."

Lu Yang took a step forward, seizing the opportunity to use Heaven and Earth in an Inch to disappear for a while. After a few days, when he returned dusty and worn, Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi would see it hadn’t been easy for him and realize his lack of familiarity with controlling his immortal body, so they would no longer invite him.

The next moment, Lu Yang appeared on a cliff in a frozen tundra.

He fell into deep thought. Was there such a cold place in Buddha Country?

Chapter 1153: 1152: The Good Days of Buddha Country Are Over

"Chief Housekeeper, Second-in-command, have you returned... Is anyone there?"

Yun Mengmeng ran out from the Ancestral Temple and saw only Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi standing outside.

"How strange, I clearly heard the Second-in-command’s voice. Sister Ao Ling, Sister Lianyi, have you seen the Chief Housekeeper and Second-in-command?"

Jiang Lianyi’s face showed a peculiar expression, somewhat at a loss for words. After pondering for a while, she slowly said, "It seems they’ve eloped."

"?"

...

"Is there such a cold place in Buddha Country?" Just as Lu Yang wondered, he suddenly realized the ground beneath his feet was gone—it was an icy abyss with sharp ice edges stretching down for miles.

Instinctively, he wanted to fly up but found that his Immortal Body had disappeared, making it impossible to fly.

"Mm-hm, it looks like the distance you can traverse with ’Heaven and Earth in an Inch’ has increased after your ascension, and you’ve stepped right out of Buddha Country," explained Immortal Fairy unhurriedly.

"Your person has left Buddha Country, but your Immortal Body cannot leave Buddha Country, so now you’re stuck like this."

This was bad. He had just lied to Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi, saying that he was not proficient with his magic skills after ascending, and now the lie had turned into reality. He had directly come outside Buddha Country, and Lu Yang regretted it deeply. Truly, lying always comes back to bite you.

Before, when he told only partial truths, nothing ever went wrong.

All around him were icy skies and snow-covered lands, with the abyss stretching down thousands of feet below—a likely arrival at the Extreme Northern Lands.

This was a famously desolate place in the Cultivation World.

"I haven’t even enjoyed a few good days in Buddha Country, and now I’ve left," Lu Yang said with regret.

Immortal Fairy comfortingly reassured her subordinate, "Little Yang Zi, cheer up. Every day is a good day with this immortal by your side."

After hearing this, Lu Yang felt even colder in his heart.

Forget it, let’s not worry about these things for now and just try to fly up.

With a bottomless cliff below, Lu Yang kept falling as the two spoke.

But he wasn’t panicking. His Realm fluctuated to the Foundation Establishment Stage, which should allow him to fly.

"That’s not right, why can’t I fly?"

Immortal Fairy stroked her chin, "It seems there is an Array Method at the bottom of the cliff restricting flight."

Lu Yang’s eyes twitched. Misfortunes never come singly, and he immediately resorted to a Forbidden Technique, "Deceiving Heaven and Earth Technique!"

He entered a state of death, landing rigidly on the ground.

When Lu Yang dispelled the Deceiving Heaven and Earth Technique, he was already at the bottom of the cliff, feeling sore all over, but otherwise, he was okay and his movement was not impeded.

A yellow-orange lantern swayed in front of Lu Yang. A middle-aged man with a goatee beard had appeared from nowhere, looking at him with concern.

The goatee-bearded man jumped when Lu Yang woke up, "Young man, you’re still alive after falling from such a height?"

Lu Yang was also shocked. There were people at the bottom of the cliff—was this a famous local landmark?

"I am Murong You, young man, what’s your name?"

Feeling it was embarrassing to have fallen like that, Lu Yang gave a false name, "I’m Meng Jingzhou."

Murong You rolled his eyes, not buying Lu Yang’s lie. Meng Jingzhou was a big-name; how could someone like that come to such a place? "Why don’t you say your name is Lu Yang?"

"...My name is Lu Yang."

Murong You looked at Lu Yang with a weird expression, "Young man, how did you fall down?"

Lu Yang considered the retribution for his earlier lies and decided to tell the truth, "After becoming immortal in Buddha Country, I was demonstrating magic skills to both the Dragon Clan Ancestor and the Phoenix Ancestor. I was careless and didn’t control the power of the skills properly, and I ended up transporting myself here. By the way, where is ’here’?"

Murong You stroked his goatee, looking at Lu Yang with increasing pity. To fall from such a height, he must have some kind of life-saving magic artifact that saved his life.

What a pity, though his life was saved, his mind seems to have taken a blow. Talking about Buddha Country, about becoming immortal.

Was this a case of infighting within a large family, pushing the family heir off a cliff?

"This place is Soul-forgotten Cliff."

"Soul-forgotten Cliff? Is it part of the Extreme Northern Lands?" Lu Yang had never heard of this place.

"Of course, it’s the Extreme Northern Lands."

Lu Yang thought to himself how desolate the Extreme Northern Lands must be. He was quite famous, yet the other person only knew his name but not his appearance.

"Young man... it’s not good to keep calling you ’young man.’ Do you remember what you’re called?"

"I really am called Lu Yang," Lu Yang said helplessly, even pulling out his Seek Dao Sect Identity Jade to show Murong You.

But Murong You had never seen an identity token of such caliber and assumed it was something Lu Yang had created out of admiration for Lu Yang, just a trinket.

Murong You shook his head, "You’ve got quite the amnesia. How about we call you Little Lu for now?"

"Alright."

Lu Yang wasn’t someone who fusses over his identity.

"Little Lu, do you want to come with me? Don’t look at me like this, but I am the Sect Leader of the Tiger Roar Sect, and we have eight hundred members," Murong You boasted proudly.

A sect with eight hundred members seemed quite influential, it’s just that Murong You’s cultivation level wasn’t very high, only in the early stages of Golden Core.

"Okay."

Lu Yang pondered; he was completely unfamiliar with the Extreme Northern Lands, so it seemed like a good idea to follow a local to learn more about the area.

Murong You extinguished the lantern and walked ahead, with Lu Yang following behind.

According to Murong You, Demon Beasts roamed everywhere around the Soul-forgotten Cliff, and keeping the lantern lit would attract them, which would not bode well even for him.

Due to the unique environment of the Soul-forgotten Cliff, many priceless herbs were grown here. Murong You had come to harvest a type of Spirit Grass called Forget Sorrow Grass.

Flight was impossible at the Soul-forgotten Cliff, so he was lowered down by his disciples with ropes, and after finding the herbs, the disciples would pull him back up.

The disciples were too weak in cultivation and would only meet their demise if they encountered Demon Beasts, leaving him no choice but to come down and harvest the herbs himself.

Lu Yang vaguely remembered seeing Forget Sorrow Grass in the medicinal garden of Seek Dao Sect, growing in large patches and as rampant as weeds under the care of the Little Medicinal Kings.

"I found them!"

Murong You exclaimed excitedly. Not far away, several sprouts of Spirit Grass emitted a shimmering white glow— the very Forget Sorrow Grass he was looking for.

"To think there are three plants, what a stroke of luck!"

Just as Murong You was about to go and harvest them, he heard footsteps from the Forget Sorrow Grass, and pairs of emerald-green eyes shone strikingly in the darkness, with a sapphire-colored ice crystal on the forehead.

"It’s an Ice Plain Wolf!"

Murong You retreated in terror. Even a Late Stage Golden Core cultivator would face life-or-death against a pack of Ice Plain Wolves.

Just as Murong You braced himself for a desperate struggle, the snarling Ice Plain Wolves suddenly showed fear, whimpering lowly and steadily backing away.

As Demon Beasts, their senses were sharper; they sensed two incredibly terrifying presences in the serene youth who stood behind Murong You.

Those two terrifying presences were entities their ancient ancestors would tremble before, supreme beings of dread!

Unable to suppress their fear, the Ice Plain Wolves turned tail and fled, leaving Murong You utterly stunned.

Lu Yang pondered. Before coming here, he had encountered Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi. Could the pack of wolves have sensed their aura?

Chapter 1154: 1153: The 800 Disciples of Tiger Roar Sect

Although Murong You didn’t know why the Ice Plain Wolf ran away, being fortunate enough to survive meant he wasn’t in the mood to delve into the reasons.

"Maybe I scared it off; I’ve always been lucky." Lu Yang took credit for the action, believing that good deeds should be acknowledged.

Murong You looked at Little Lu in surprise, but on second thought, he realized that maybe Little Lu was right. Under normal circumstances, he would have certainly been dead.

If there was a difference from the usual situation, it was indeed the presence of Little Lu.

And the fact that Little Lu had fallen from such a high place was enough to prove that he was indeed lucky.

"Alright, alright, we’ll rely on your luck from now on."

Murong You knew that the longer they stayed at Soul-forgotten Cliff, the more likely they were to encounter danger. This time it was the Ice Plain Wolf; next time, it might be the Moon Shadow Sky Lion that could tear a Nascent Soul cultivator to shreds.

He quickly picked some Forget Sorrow Grass and took Lu Yang to the agreed meeting spot.

A rope dropped vertically from above, lowered by Murong You’s disciples.

"I’ll go up first, then you follow my lead and climb up," instructed Murong You. If both tried to climb the rope at the same time and something went wrong, they would both fall.

Murong You pulled himself up by the rope, carefully climbing the cliff face, while Lu Yang quickly followed, his actions even swifter than Murong You’s.

Although Lu Yang’s realm had dropped to the Foundation Establishment Stage, his control over his body was on a completely different level from Murong You.

Even if Lu Yang were to battle against his past self at the Foundation Establishment Stage, he could win with ease. This was why high level cultivators who suppressed their realms could be invincible among their peers.

"Master, are you alright?" At the cliff edge, three disciples showed concern for Murong You.

"That was a close call." Only after truly leaving Soul-forgotten Cliff did Murong You breathe a sigh of relief, producing three stalks of Forget Sorrow Grass.

"There are actually three stalks!" The three disciples rejoiced.

"Keep holding the rope; there’s still someone else coming up."

"There’s someone else down there?"

"Yes, a poor kid named Little Lu who also damaged his head."

When Lu Yang climbed up, he felt the pitying gazes of Murong You’s three disciples looking at him.

Wait, can you please stop spreading the word that I’ve damaged my head?

Lu Yang glanced at the three disciples, one at the Foundation Establishment Stage and two at the Qi Cultivation Stage.

Murong You entered Muxue City with his four disciples. The city walls were covered with a thick layer of ice, which Murong You explained had been there since the day Muxue City was founded, never melting over a thousand years.

In the Extreme Northern Lands, things like ten-thousand-year-old mysterious ice weren’t uncommon.

The population in the Extreme Northern Lands was sparse, with no unified government. The cities had little communication with one another, and what had happened in the Cultivation World years ago could still be considered news here.

"What, a Semi-Immortal Battle took place at the Western Paradise Temple, and an Immortal Battle occurred at Demon City?"

Murong You was shocked to hear people discussing this by the roadside. Both the Western Paradise Temple and Demon City were centers of major powers, supposedly safe places. Why did it sound more dangerous than their Muxue City?

Given the situation, Lu Yang suspected the people of the Extreme Northern Lands were unaware of his advancement to the Refining the Void Stage.

This place was indeed remote; he feared that not even his master’s enemies would be found here.

"What is this?"

Murong You whispered, "It’s said that the City Lord has a grudge against Daoist Buyu."

"I know you admire Lu Yang, but I’ll pretend I didn’t hear that. If the City Lord’s people find out, you’re done!"

Lu Yang held his forehead. "Master, can you stay honest for once? How do you even have enemies in the Extreme Northern Lands?"

"Why can’t you be more like me? I may roam everywhere, but I am kind to people and make no enemies."

"Forget it, I won’t go by Lu Yang anymore."

"What will you call yourself then?"

"Lu Doudou."

"We’re here," Murong You led Lu Yang to a building.

It had a signboard that read: Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall.

There was a recruitment offer at the entrance: sign up for a three-month course and get half a month free, or sign up for one year and get three months free.

Entering the martial arts hall, Lu Yang saw a group of children earnestly practicing their punches, their voices young but in unison.

Every so often, someone would come in leading a child and inquire, "Is it true that you offer a free seven-day trial before deciding whether to sign up?"

"...Murong Sir, didn’t you say your Tiger Roar Sect has eight hundred disciples?"

Murong You appeared somewhat embarrassed: "We do have quite a lot of outer gate disciples, and the entry standards are indeed a bit low."

"It’s just that, considering how dangerous the Extreme Northern Lands are, we thought it’d be good for children to learn some martial arts for survival. If we’re lucky enough to find one with a Spirit Root, then we’ll make them inner sect disciples."

"If you’re temporarily without a place to stay, why not come teach martial arts at our hall?" Murong You suggested. Seeing that Lu Yang was at the Foundation Establishment Stage, he would be considered the second strongest in their Tiger Roar Sect.

However, since he was an outsider, it wouldn’t be prudent to let him access the core of the sect right away, but letting such a fine solitary cultivator slip away also pained him, so he came up with the idea of having him teach martial arts.

"Sure."

After observing the children’s Tiger Roar Fist for a while, which seemed similar to moves like the Imitation Fist and the Lion Roar Skill, Lu Yang thought he should have no problem teaching it—especially since he knew Jin Caiwei, the most formidable tiger in the world.

Murong You arranged for Lu Yang a place to stay where the concentration of Spiritual Energy was quite decent; although it was of no use to the current Lu Yang, it showed Murong You’s regard for him.

Lu Yang lay down and feigned death, reporting his situation to the sect.

Asking for help was a bit embarrassing, so he didn’t mention it proactively, hoping the sect would get the hint and send someone to take him back.

His third senior sister was at the Void Refinement Stage and practiced in the Demon Territory with several magic artifacts in hand. She could face Body Integration Realm without defeat, while he had nothing, which was quite dangerous.

...

"Elder Brother Dai, the Xuanyuan Sword Master—no, I mean Elder Brother Lu Yang has ’died’ again."

Gu Junye found Dai Bufan, who was taken aback.

Didn’t that kid Lu Yang ascend to become a Semi-Immortal in Buddha Country? What could have happened that he needed to play dead again?

"Junior Brother Lu said he accidentally arrived at the Extreme Northern Lands."

"What’s he doing running off to the Extreme Northern Lands?" Dai Bufan wondered. Did Lu Yang think he’d stirred up too much trouble and went seeking ten-thousand-year-old Mysterious Ice in the Extreme Northern Lands to freeze himself and become a scourge on the Cultivation World in tens of thousands of years?

Dai Bufan immediately reported Lu Yang’s situation to Yun Zhi.

"Junior Brother went to the Extreme Northern Lands, that’s a good mindset."

"A good mindset?"

"Junior Brother must feel that he’s too comfortable in Buddha Country and lacking in hardship. He went to the Extreme Northern Lands to challenge and temper himself."

"But Junior Brother Lu said he went to the Extreme Northern Lands by accident."

Yun Zhi waved her hand, unconcerned. "Junior Brother has always been modest; that’s just a humble way for him to express it."

Chapter 1155: 1154: Teacher Lu Yang

"Elder Brother Dai should be sending someone to pick me up, right?"

Lu Yang said, confident that the kind-hearted Elder Brother Dai would not leave him in the perilous Extreme Northern Lands without care.

"Keep a low profile before the Sect sends someone. Pretend to be an ordinary person swept up in the mundane world."

"Is Little Lu there?" A voice came from outside the door, it was Murong You’s.

"Please come in."

Murong You entered with a cheerful concern, "Are you getting used to living here? If you encounter any difficulties, just let me know. My name still carries some weight on this Rain Street."

Lu Yang took the initiative to pour tea for Murong You. Unlike the delicate fairies, he was not fussy about where he lived; as long as he had a place to stay, he was content.

Murong You was taken aback as he accepted the tea; he used to be able to discern that Lu Yang was at the Foundation Establishment Stage. Why couldn’t he see through him now?

Was it some magic artifact used to conceal cultivation level?

Surely being from an influential family meant having plenty of life-saving treasures.

"Little Lu, this is the material you will be teaching," Murong You said, handing over a finely decorated book of Fist, the fundamental Tiger Roar Fist from the Tiger Roar Sect.

Lu Yang flipped through the book and immediately knew it was akin to the Lion Roar Skill, but much simpler and easier, with a lower threshold. This kind of thing, if placed in the Seek Dao Sect’s Scripture Pavilion, would have to be shelved alongside the "Encyclopedia of Crosstalk," as part of the folk arts.

"Sir, this Fist manual isn’t complete, is it?"

"Sharp eyes. This manual is only for mortals and Qi Cultivation Stage cultivators to practice. To access the versions for Foundation Establishment Stage and Golden Core Stage, one must become an inner sect disciple of our Tiger Roar Sect."

"In fact, our Sect doesn’t possess the complete manual either. According to our ancestors, this Tiger Roar Fist originated from an Ancient Great Power, who observed the supreme roar of the Qiongqi Clan that shook the heavens and the earth, and composed this extraordinary Fist. However, what has been passed down to this day covers only the first three realms, and we don’t know when we’ll be able to complete the entire manual."

As Murong You spoke, he sighed with a tinge of pride, his words subtly boasting the long history and profound heritage of the Tiger Roar Sect, intending to make Lu Yang feel inclined to join them.

Lu Yang caught the reference to a familiar figure, "An Ancient Times Qiongqi Clan’s Venerate? Little Tiger?"

"Little Tiger’s father. Back then, Little Tiger hadn’t even become a Semi-Immortal yet."

"Ah, right, I remember now."

The first time Lu Yang visited the Qiongqi Clan, he had seen the Ancient Qiongqi’s Illusory Shadow in the ancestral tombs, confronting Zhu Tian’s Illusion and scaring it into conceding, indicating the formidable presence it once had in the Ancient Times.

...

"Hello, everyone. My name is Lu Doudou, and I will be responsible for teaching you the Tiger Roar Fist. You can call me Mr. Lu." Lu Yang, dressed in the uniform of the Tiger Roar Sect with an embroidered roaring tiger head on the chest and a large "Tiger" character on the back, greeted the class.

"Hello, Mr. Lu."

There were not only children learning Fist; the group Lu Yang was teaching now consisted of teenagers around fourteen or fifteen years old.

"Show me your Fist one by one, so I can see how you’ve all been practicing."

The teenagers executed their Fist with earnest movements, showing that they were sincerely practicing.

This was understandable; in the Extreme Northern Lands, having martial skills meant not being bullied and was a means to improve one’s living conditions.

Although the teenagers were earnest, their movements were generally stiff and rigid. Considering that their previous teacher was only at the Qi Cultivation Stage, such an outcome was forgivable.

Lu Yang observed each person’s movements carefully, noting their shortcomings for future targeted guidance. Since he had become a teacher, he couldn’t afford to be negligent.

"Not bad movements, but lacking in momentum," Lu Yang commented calmly.

Seeing that the young men were somewhat unconvinced, Lu Yang didn’t say much, simply taking out a Recording Orb from his Identity Jade.

He infused Spiritual Energy into the Recording Orb, and an intimidating Little Tiger appeared out of nowhere, startling the young men quite a bit.

"Howl—"

This was a Dream Mirage produced by Jin Caiwei for the promotion of their Qiongqi Clan; the Qiongqi featured were not fictitious but played by members of the Qiongqi Clan themselves.

"First, watch the Dream Mirage for a few days, then practice your fists."

"Is this the Dream Mirage from the Central Continent?"

"But I heard that the Dream Mirage from over there isn’t played using a Recording Orb."

The young men had no chance to see a Dream Mirage, let alone the latest version, so the discussion quickly died down as they focused intently on the legendary path of the Qiongqi Clan.

Although in Lu Yang’s view, the legendary path of the Qiongqi Clan in the Dream Mirage was ridiculously exaggerated, it didn’t matter as long as it was entertaining.

"Who wants to watch this stuff? It’s a waste of time. I’d rather practice my fists than spend this much time on it!" A frustrated youth stood up, unable to comprehend Lu Yang’s actions, and stormed out, slamming the door behind him.

Lu Yang remembered the leaving youth seemed to be named Gong Che, who was the best at fist techniques among this group of people.

...

"That’s it for today, class dismissed," Lu Yang announced, clapping his hands to disperse the students.

At that moment, Murong You came over and whispered to him, "Little Lu, these past few days there’ve been parents complaining to us, saying you’re not seriously teaching their kids fist techniques, being lazy and taking it easy, showing the Dream Mirage for half of the time."

"Who, Gong Che’s parents?"

Lu Yang remembered that Gong Che had been present these days, but every time he started the Dream Mirage, Gong Che would turn around and leave. Although Lu Yang had repeatedly suggested he stay and watch for a while to help with his fists practice, Gong Che obviously had an aversion to Lu Yang and was unwilling to listen.

"Don’t worry about who it was, it’s not just one person saying this. Little Lu, I’m telling you, as Cultivators we must do good deeds and make good connections; when teaching students, we can’t be lazy."

Lu Yang laughed, "Gong Che’s just outside, isn’t he? Have him pick a student to practice with and you’ll see."

Lu Yang had already noticed with his Spiritual Sense that Gong Che’s parents were also outside, and it seemed they were both Cultivators in the Qi Cultivation Stage.

"Alright."

Murong You called Gong Che and his family in. Although Gong Che’s parents held a sense of awe for Lu Yang, a high-level practitioner in the Foundation Establishment Stage, Gong Che, young and brash, did not care about this.

The students who were about to leave stayed for the excitement, and those who had left the martial arts hall were called back by their peers.

"What’s the matter, do you have a problem with my teaching methods?" Lu Yang looked at Gong Che with a half-mocking smile.

"You’re wasting our time!" Gong Che said angrily, but Lu Yang was not annoyed.

"Then why don’t you pick one of your classmates and exchange a few moves with him?"

"Fine! Shi Yi, let’s practice!"

Shi Yi was not surprised to be called upon; after all, his fist techniques were second only to Gong Che.

The two took their stances, and Gong Che made the first move, his fists moving like the wind, with a faint sound.

"Howl!"

What surprised everyone was that Shi Yi responded faster even though he struck later, directly letting out a tiger’s roar that suggested he had improved leaps and bounds compared to just a few days ago!

Ten days ago, before Lu Yang had arrived, they had sparred, and Gong Che was clearly superior to Shi Yi, but in just a short ten days, the positions were reversed; Shi Yi was now the one pressing Gong Che continuously!

If anyone was most shocked, it was Murong You. Shi Yi’s tiger roar indicated that he had mastered Tiger Roar Fist—a rarity among many batches of students.

The only issue was that the tiger’s roar produced by Shi Yi sounded a bit immature, not quite like that of a grown fierce tiger.

Chapter 1156: 1555: Drive the Tiger to Swallow the Wolf

Gong Che was no match for Shi Yi and was quickly defeated.

The students were in uproar, the ever-progressive Gong Che had actually lost to Shi Yi?

"Little Lu, did you teach this?!" Murong You’s eyes were wide with shock as she stared at Lu Yang. It had only been a few days, and even if she herself had taught the class, she wouldn’t have been able to achieve such results.

"The main thing is that I taught well. Of course, the children also have decent comprehension abilities. It’s not just Shi Yi; the other children are also advancing rapidly in their cultivation."

"How did you teach them?" Murong You suddenly realized something and excitedly grabbed Lu Yang’s shoulders. "Dream Mirage, you showed them the Dream Mirage, didn’t you?"

"Alright, stop shaking me, just take a look and you’ll understand." Lu Yang chuckled and picked up the Recording Orb from the ground; class had just ended and this incident had already occurred. He hadn’t had time to put away the Recording Orb yet.

The majestic Ancient Qiongqi appeared before Murong You, roaring and demonstrating a ruler’s demeanor.

"This, this is a Qiongqi?!"

Murong You didn’t recognize Jin Caiwei and could only identify this as one of the strongest races among the legendary Tiger Clan, the Qiongqi Clan.

"Where did this Recording Orb come from?"

"It was a gift from the junior ancestor of the Qiongqi Clan."

"I’m serious."

"I’m serious too."

Seeing that Lu Yang was unwilling to tell the truth, Murong You let it be, as she couldn’t stop admiring the Recording Orb.

Not only was the Recording Orb beneficial for Shi Yi, but it was also very useful to her.

She had never seen a living Qiongqi in her lifetime.

"Little Lu, ah no, Daoist Lu, are you selling this Recording Orb?"

"Whether it’s for sale or not, it’s not something valuable. I’ll give it to you all." This Recording Orb that Lu Yang had was the original version, different from those available on the market, and best reflected the divine might of the Qiongqi Clan.

"This, how could I accept this... What if I give you the fist manuals for the Foundation Establishment Stage and the Golden Core Stage?"

Lu Yang felt that if he simply gave it to Elder Murong, Elder Murong might be restless. It was better to accept something in return to put his mind at ease: "That’s fine, but I’ll still need this Recording Orb for teaching. You can use it after my lessons are finished."

"Of course, of course."

Murong You was overjoyed.

"How about it, little Gong? Will you still come to class?"

By this point, Gong Che knew that Shi Yi’s progress was thanks to Lu Yang. He had missed a great opportunity and knelt on the ground to apologize: "I’m sorry, Mr. Lu. I will definitely attend class on time and not leave early."

"That’s enough, no need to kneel." Lu Yang lifted him up with mana.

"Mr. Lu, what about the child in the future..." Gong Che’s parents came forward with apologetic smiles, worried that Lu Yang would refuse to teach Gong Che.

"You’ve paid the tuition, if little Gong comes to learn, I will teach."

"He will definitely come. If he doesn’t, I’ll spank him!" Gong Che’s father declared in a gruff voice.

"Right, the two of you, stay back for a moment after class. I have something to ask you."

After the students and Murong You had left, Gong Che’s parents came to Lu Yang’s room feeling uneasy, leaving Gong Che outside.

Nervously, Gong Che’s parents took out several Spirit Stones from their Storage Ring, intending to hand them to Lu Yang.

"You’ve misunderstood; that’s not what I meant. I wanted to ask if Gong Che has had any accidents at birth since there seems to be an excess of cold Qi in his body?"

Gong Che’s parents didn’t expect Lu Yang to ask this question, but ultimately, Gong Che’s father decided to reveal the truth.

"Ah, we might as well tell the truth; actually, Gong Che isn’t our biological child. We found him when we were hunting Demon Beasts outside the city."

"When we found Gong Che, the child’s Little Face was purple. We couldn’t bear to let the child freeze to death outside, so we brought him back."

"We fed the child ginger soup and herbs, but his complexion didn’t improve. We searched for all the doctors in the city to save him."

"But the doctors told us that the child’s body was afflicted with cold Qi, a sign of an early death, not expected to live past sixteen."

"Then we always looked for various ways to keep little Che alive. We happened to hear a folk remedy saying that practicing a yang-filled fist could drive out the cold Qi, so we had him practice fist here."

"Does little Gong know about this?"

"We didn’t dare tell him; we only said his body was weak and he needed to take more medicine and practice more fist. However, the child is sensitive, he may have noticed something."

"He feels that your classes are a waste of time without teaching fist; perhaps that’s also why."

When it came to the sad part, Gong Che’s mother cried, sobbing: "I don’t know which heartless person left little Che in the snow to let the cold Qi enter his body."

Lu Yang shook his head: "It’s not because he was left in the snow; rather, he is afflicted with Xuantian cold Qi, which is not something commonly found around here."

"But it’s not a big problem; continue to follow me and practice fist, and it will eliminate the cold Qi from his body."

"Then thank you, Mr. Lu!" The couple hurriedly knelt and kowtowed; although Gong Che was not their biological child, after raising him for fifteen years, it felt like he was their own.

"That’s enough; I’ve already said I do this for money, it’s a fair trade, there’s no need to thank me." Lu Yang never expected that after helping Gong Che up, he would have to help his parents too. Truly like family.

With the original method, Gong Che would definitely not live past sixteen, but with Lu Yang around, this kind of cold Qi wasn’t a problem.

Actually, the best solution would be to cut Old Meng, get a drop of his blood, dilute it in water for Gong Che to soak in once, and guarantee a cure. But Old Meng was far away, and it was a bit troublesome to look for him; practicing fist was the most trouble-free option.

After sending Gong Che’s family away, Lu Yang focused on dealing with his own affairs.

"Fairy, do you know any life-saving techniques?"

Several days passed, and Elder Brother Dai hadn’t sent anyone. It looked like he had to rely on himself.

"Life-saving techniques? The Playing Dead Technique."

"...Do you have any life-saving techniques with an offensive nature?"

If the enemy was slow-witted, that’s one thing; but if he encountered enemies smarter than Immortal Fairy Eternity, the Playing Dead Technique was likely not going to work.

"How about I perform the Playing Dead Technique, and this Immortal possesses your body to help you fight?"

"Anything else?"

Immortal Fairy Eternity clenched her teeth, as if making a significant decision: "If need be, I’ll risk damage to my essence and forcefully condense an Immortal body to save you!"

This gave Lu Yang a big scare, and he hurriedly said it wasn’t necessary.

"Hmm, when in danger, just shout that you are Lu Yang; the City Lord will surely save you."

Lu Yang was silent. The Fairy had even learned tactics like driving the tiger to swallow the wolf; she didn’t seem like someone who would use such tactics when they first met, right?

Had following him for so long subtly made the Fairy smarter?

"What if the danger comes from the City Lord?"

"Then you say you have so many enemies that they need to take a number to seek revenge on you."

"And if the other enemies come?"

"Then you curse at Yingtian Immortal; Yingtian Immortal will have a response and will definitely bring down Immortal Thunder to save you."

"What if Yingtian Immortal’s action attracts even stronger enemies, like from the Great Qian Dynasty, for example?"

"If need be, I’ll risk damage to my essence..."

Chapter 1157: 1156: Life-saving Means

Lu Yang was convinced that Fairy Eternity came up with this tiger-driving and wolf-swallowing scheme purely by blind luck, like a blind cat running into a dead mouse.

"Maybe I can learn from Third Senior Sister and safely get through the Refining the Void Stage with a magic artifact?"

Lu Yang still remembered the scene where, in the Demon Territory, Third Senior Sister didn’t have to lift a finger herself, frightening the Demon Race into a hasty retreat with her magic artifact alone.

"Using a magic artifact, indeed, that’s a good idea," Fairy Eternity nodded in agreement.

"Right? Like refining Junior Brother Li Haoran’s type of war armor magic artifact. The war armor automatically counters the enemy, its power nearly indistinguishable from my own attacks," Lu Yang said excitedly.

Fairy Eternity also got excited by Lu Yang’s words and continued, "Or maybe refining different functional magic artifacts, a gourd that breathes in and out Yin and Yang, a jade bottle that spouts water, a medicinal cauldron that spits fire, a fan for riding the wind... Yin Yang Five Elements Spells ready for anything, capable of handling whatever enemy we encounter!"

The two of them got more excited the more they talked, feeling that using magic artifacts was indeed a great idea.

Lu Yang looked at Fairy Eternity excitedly, "I don’t know Artifact Refining Technique, do you, Fairy?"

Fairy Eternity excitedly looked back at Lu Yang, "I don’t either."

"..."

The two looked at each other, quickly regaining their composure.

Lu Yang regretted not preparing himself. If he had known he would come to the Extreme Northern Lands, he should have asked Junior Brother Li for a few handy life-saving magic artifacts before leaving.

It was unlikely he would find an Artifact Refiner in a remote place like Muxue City who could help him with refining.

"Little Yang, you could scold Ying Tian a few times. That way, if Ying Tian has a reaction, he will seek us out, and we’ll have an Artifact Refiner then."

Lu Yang walked outside, casually chopped down a tree from the yard, and carried it back into the room.

Although he didn’t know Artifact Refining Technique, he could use a similar method.

Lu Yang took out his Green Peak Sword and whittled the tree into several wooden sword shapes, then injected them with Sword Qi.

"Done."

His current Realm fluctuation was at the Body Integration Realm level, and the Sword Qi he injected was also of the Body Integration Realm grade. He had made wooden swords for the Fu Ling Sisters as amulets when he was at the Nascent Soul Stage. Now that he was cultivating to the Void Refinement Stage, making these wooden swords was even easier for him.

Lu Yang kept injecting the wooden swords with Sword Qi, mass-producing these incredibly powerful protective talisman wooden swords. Soon, he started to find it a bit troublesome.

He used the Tree Planting Spell, allowing his Wooden Avatar to hold the Green Peak Sword and carve and inject Sword Qi into the wood, while he had other matters to attend to.

"To be on the safe side, let’s add another life-saving measure."

...

"Have you heard? There’s a new reward posted on the street," the students of the martial arts hall gathered in groups before class, discussing the fresh news on the streets.

"Ah, did the City Lord update the bounty again? Where’s the reward notice? It would be just right to tear a few off to patch up our windows. Last time when they were looking for Daoist Buyu, we missed out on getting any."

"What reward, can’t you read? That’s called a missing person notice!" Gong Che corrected.

"A missing person notice?"

"Yes, it says that Meng Family’s Young Master, Meng Jingzhou, has wandered off sleepwalking. The Seek Dao Sect and the Meng Family jointly issued the missing person notice, offering a billion Spirit Stones to anyone who can safely return Meng Jingzhou to the Seek Dao Sect."

"A billion Spirit Stones, how much money is that?" the students exclaimed in surprise, as they had never seen nor heard of anyone possessing such an amount of Spirit Stones.

Gong Che’s family was considered wealthy among them, but even his family couldn’t produce a hundred Spirit Stones in total.

"Okay, enough talking, let’s start the class," Lu Yang clapped his hands, noticing the message had spread quickly.

The missing person notice posted on the street had been put up by him. He had decided that if he encountered danger, like facing more enemies than the number of wooden swords in a sack, he would disguise himself as Old Meng. His transformation mask could fool even those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

Although the Meng Family’s reputation in the Imperial City was somewhat poor, just a bit below that of the Seek Dao Sect, outside the city, who didn’t have financial dealings with the Meng Family?

Lu Yang counted the number of people and noticed two were missing, "Why haven’t Fang Zhige and Liu Yu come? Does anyone know what’s going on with them?"

Lu Yang had been teaching these classes for so many days, and except for Gong Che skipping in the first few, no one else had missed a class.

Most of these students weren’t very wealthy at home, and missing a day of class would be a loss to them.

The students shook their heads one after another, all clueless about why both were absent.

"Mr. Lu, it’s possible that something has happened at Liu Yu’s house. I heard that Fang Zhige has been living with Liu Yu," Gong Che said.

Shi Yi also said, "Right, I remember now, Liu Yu mentioned that Fang Zhige didn’t initially want to learn to fist fight. It was Liu Yu who dragged him to learn it."

The students’ deepest impression of the two was that they were inseparable, often joking that they were a couple. Every time Liu Yu just blushed and kept silent while Fang Zhige coldly denied it.

As for their progress in learning fist techniques, Fang Zhige was only mediocre, not even as good as Liu Yu.

Lu Yang nodded and continued to play the Recording Orb, "You guys watch this, Gong Che, keep an eye on them for me; I’m going to check out Liu Yu’s house."

Lu Yang had a deep impression of Fang Zhige. Fang Zhige was intentionally staying at the middle level; his real strength was in the Late Stage of Qi Cultivation. He stood out among the ordinary student crowd and always carried a Qi Concealing Talisman.

...

At Liu Yu’s house.

"Sister Yu, I’ve let you down!" Fang Zhige, who usually exuded an icy demeanor, was now in utter disarray. He had tried every means to handle the situation himself, but in the end, he’d still dragged Liu Yu into his troubles.

"It’s alright, I don’t blame you, but after this is over, you have to tell me everything about you!" Liu Yu, holding Fang Zhige’s face, said earnestly.

Fang Zhige gave a bitter smile. This was over? Could they even stay alive after it ended?

"Promise me!"

Looking into Liu Yu’s pond-like eyes, Fang Zhige seemed to make a certain resolution, nodding solemnly, "Alright, I’ll tell you everything about my past!"

Fang Zhige positioned Liu Yu behind him, vigilant of attacks from all around.

"Master Luo, I didn’t expect the organization to send you."

Master Luo’s chilly voice, carried by the cold wind, reached their ears, his tone mocking, "Fang Sha, you thought you could escape the Ghost Killing Alliance and live?"

"Hard to believe you, Fang Sha, who passed the final test by killing your best friend, have fallen so low as to rename yourself Zhige, find a charming partner, and join common folk to learn the silly Tiger Roar Fist. What a farce."

"If I hadn’t seen it with my own eyes, I would find it hard to imagine that Fang Sha, who went through that, could fall to such a state!"

"Shut up!" Master Luo struck a nerve, and Fang Zhige couldn’t help but shout out!

"Fang Sha, given your outstanding talent in assassination techniques, I can give you a chance. Just kill that girl next to you, kill the students and teachers of the Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall, prove you’re still the same Fang Sha, and I’ll spare your life, allowing you to rejoin the Ghost Killing Alliance."

"You’re dreaming!"

Fang Zhige yelled angrily. It was Liu Yu, it was everyone at the martial arts hall who had shown him what emotions were. How could he possibly bring himself to harm them.

"Can’t do it? I taught you the assassination techniques myself. It seems today I’ll have to take back your skills..."

Master Luo sighed, and then his presence vanished, leaving only the relentless northern winds howling.

Fang Zhige kept a wary eye on his surroundings, a bead of cold sweat running down his forehead, quickly dried by the northern wind.

Although Master Luo was at the Foundation Establishment Stage and he was at Great Perfection of Qi Cultivation, if he found the right opportunity, there was a chance he could turn the tables and kill Master Luo through assassination!

Where would Master Luo appear from?

But was there something unfinished about Master Luo’s last words?

Liu Yu could feel the grip on her wrist tightening, but she didn’t dare to speak, afraid of distracting Fang Zhige.

With a creak, the wooden door pushed open, and Lu Yang walked in, looking at the couple.

"Did you run into some trouble? Why didn’t you go to class?"

Chapter 1158: 1157: How Did the Ghost Killing Alliance Become Connected with Young Master Lu?

Lu Yang noticed Fang Zhige clutching Liu Yu’s wrist with a playful look in his eyes and kindly reminded them,

"Having your first romantic feelings is good, but I should remind you that you are still young. Whether you are learning martial arts or cultivating, you need to maintain ample kidney essence to ensure your cultivation speed."

"I have a friend who, in order to cultivate, abstained from the company of women, and his cultivation progress was so rapid that anyone who saw him had to praise him as a genius of the Great Era. I’ve heard his worth has even increased by a billion recently."

Fang Zhige, upon hearing Lu Yang’s reminder, realized he had been impolite and quickly let go of Liu Yu’s wrist.

That’s not right, things aren’t over yet!

"Mr. Lu, be careful, there are assassins nearby!"

Although Mr. Lu is also at the Foundation Establishment Stage, facing an assassin at the same level he would be hard-pressed to escape death.

"Assassin? Where?" Lu Yang was startled. Where did this assassin come from? He was only at the Unification realm, and if he encountered an assassin above the Pass Tribulation, his chances were slim.

The three of them stood in the courtyard for a while, but still saw no sign of Master Luo.

Fang Zhige was puzzled. This wasn’t right. Why hadn’t Master Luo made a move? Did Master Luo see that Mr. Lu was also at the Foundation Establishment Stage and didn’t dare to act?

This was not Master Luo’s style. Master Luo had always completed his tasks perfectly, having killed more than a handful of cultivators at the Foundation Establishment Stage; he wouldn’t likely retreat just because it was difficult.

"All right, all right, go back to class. It’s not easy for your parents to earn money; you can’t use this as a way to skip class." Lu Yang pretended to be angry and pushed the two toward the martial arts school.

When the three of them returned to the martial arts school, the segment about the Qiongqi Clan had just finished, and the first half of the class had ended.

Recently, Lu Yang’s classes consisted of watching Dream Mirage for the first half and teaching Fist techniques for the second half.

Fang Zhige muddled through the class, then muddled out of class, still not understanding where Master Luo had gone or why he hadn’t taken action.

Back at Liu Yu’s home, in accordance with the agreement, he honestly confessed his past: "Actually, I was raised by the Ghost Killing Alliance from infancy. We’ve been training in assassination techniques since we were young..."

"I always thought our Ghost Killing Alliance was righteous, that those we killed deserved to die. But in the ultimate test, I came into contact with the true nature of the Ghost Killing Alliance and made up my mind to escape from them..."

While telling his own past, Fang Zhige was still thinking about Master Luo.

At this moment, Master Luo, whom Fang Zhige had been thinking about, was hanging from a tree. In the Extreme Northern Lands, where it was snowy year-round, a thick layer of snow had accumulated on top of Master Luo’s head.

"Ready to make a clean breast of it?" Lu Yang took down Master Luo and dragged him into the cabin.

Master Luo’s eyes were filled with fear as he looked at Lu Yang. As a seasoned assassin, he had thoroughly investigated Fang Zhige’s social relationships before the assassination, including the change of his fist teaching teacher.

Lu Doudou, at the Foundation Establishment Stage, with unknown origins, knowledgeable and well-read.

"Kid, if you let me go now, I can pretend nothing happened. Since you’re so well-informed, you must have heard of our Ghost Killing Alliance’s reputation..."

"Wait, what is the Ghost Killing Alliance?" Lu Yang thought through all the forces he knew of, but there was none called the Ghost Killing Alliance.

"Not knowing the Ghost Killing Alliance, such an ignorant guy. Others say you’re knowledgeable and well-read, but it seems to be nothing more than that. Our Ghost Killing Alliance is backed by the Jiuyou Sect. Everyone who dares to oppose our Ghost Killing Alliance is bound to end up with no bones left."

"You are from the Jiuyou Sect?" Lu Yang was surprised. They had already found proper jobs for the Jiuyou Sect; why were they still doing things like this?

With that in mind, Lu Yang didn’t bother wasting more words with Master Luo and directly used the Soul Search to understand the origin of the Ghost Killing Alliance.

It really wasn’t just an empty threat; it indeed belonged to the Jiuyou Sect.

Lu Yang took out the Jiuyou Order and sent a message to the Jiuyou Sect: The assassin from the Ghost Killing Alliance just tried to kill me and claimed that the Ghost Killing Alliance is under your Jiuyou Sect. You should investigate this, I hope there’s no misunderstanding between us.

Then, Lu Yang looked at Master Luo’s corpse, pondering what to do with it.

"Forget it, use it as fertilizer."

Lu Yang dug a hole in the yard. Previously, he had dug up the tree there to make wooden swords, and now it was just right to bury Master Luo underneath and plant a tree sapling on top.

His Tree Planting Spell couldn’t make trees grow rapidly, but with a Foundation Establishment Stage cultivator as nourishment, the sapling should thrive.

...

"Is the Ghost Killing Alliance part of our Jiuyou Sect’s power?" Sect Leader Mu Baiyi, upon seeing the words on the Jiuyou Order, broke out in a cold sweat on his back.

Although he had recently broken through to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, he was still terrified by the lightly phrased sentence on the Jiuyou Order.

Young Sect Master Lu was calling him out.

The problem was that he had no recollection of this Ghost Killing Alliance.

Soon Vice Sect Hierarch Shi was summoned by Mu Baiyi, who also had never heard of this Ghost Killing Alliance appearing out of nowhere.

Seeing the situation, Mu Baiyi simply called over all three Vice Sect Hierarchs to investigate the origin of the Ghost Killing Alliance.

"We’ve found it, we’ve found it," another Vice Sect Hierarch exclaimed.

"What exactly is the origin of this Ghost Killing Alliance?" everyone asked eagerly.

"It originated from a rudder master of our sect from three hundred years ago, whose son-in-law had some cultivation talent. Feeling restrained in Great Xia, he went to the Extreme Northern Lands and established an organization called the Nine Ghosts Alliance there."

"Under the Nine Ghosts Alliance, there are nine smaller factions including Ghost, Fierce Ghosts, Ghost Killing, and Ghost Death, with the Ghost Killing Alliance being one of them."

"The leader of the Nine Ghosts Alliance’s cultivation level is probably at Mid-stage Nascent Soul, maybe even at Nascent Soul Great Perfection."

"Every year, the Nine Ghosts Alliance offers tribute to us, so there’s some incense relationship."

Mu Baiyi’s expression was bizarre: "Are you saying that an unknown assassin from an assassination organization, which is a subordinate of an organization with cultivation highest at Nascent Soul, wants to assassinate Young Master Lu who has the support of two Immortals and four Semi-Immortals?!"

What kind of connection was this?

"Master, what should we do?"

Mu Baiyi glared: "What to do? We need to obliterate this Nine Ghosts Alliance immediately, what nonsense incense relationship. What does this minor power have to do with us!"

"Vice Sect Hierarch Shi, you go."

"Yes."

Vice Sect Hierarch Shi immediately arranged the task.

"Huo Huashen, you pick two more clever Nascent Souls to take a trip to the Extreme Northern Lands, and yes, don’t kill indiscriminately, Young Sect Master Lu probably doesn’t like senseless killing of the innocent."

"Yes."

"Little Liu, take twenty Nascent Souls with me to the Extreme Northern Lands."

"Yes."

...

"Yo, Master Huo, what wind has blown you here?" Upon hearing that Huo Huashen was visiting, the Nine Ghosts Alliance Leader, so frightened that he couldn’t even dress properly, scrambled out of bed and rushed to greet him.

Facing Huo Huashen, the Nine Ghosts Alliance Leader was trembling with fear, not daring to show the slightest negligence, anxiously and fearfully unaware of why this esteemed individual had come to the Extreme Northern Lands.

This Master Huo Huashen was none other than the favored figure of Vice Sect Hierarch Shi, the general manager of a chain barbecue restaurant, which demonstrated how much Vice Sect Hierarch Shi valued him.

Huo Huashen did not reply, simply looking at the Nine Ghosts Alliance Leader with a cold gaze, frightening him so much that he immediately knelt down, and the nine small Alliance Leaders of the Nine Ghosts Alliance followed suit, prostrating themselves on the ground.

"Master Huo, what... what exactly is this..."

"In your next life, be more discerning of the eyes, and don’t provoke those you cannot afford to provoke."

...

As Lu Yang lay in bed, pondering over the future lesson plans, a response from Mu Baiyi came through the Jiuyou Order: The Ghost Killing Alliance is under the power of the Ruthless Sect, falsely framing our sect with deliberate malice. Our sect has already eradicated it thoroughly.

Chapter 1159: 1158: In a Tight Spot but Resolute

"You guys think, why have the numbers of Demon Beasts outside the city reduced so much recently? Could this be a sign of impending trouble?" Lu Yang asked.

"If we knew why, would the City Lord have sent us to investigate?" another team member responded.

A seven-person scouting team made their way through a world of ice and snow. Each of them was a battle-hardened Cultivator with experience in defeating Demon Beasts of their level. They had been dispatched by the City Lord to investigate the reason behind the changing numbers of the beasts.

They glided close to the ground, dressed in white robes, with a High-level Concealing Talisman affixed inside their clothing. Magic artifacts hung at their waists, and their compasses spun in their hands.

As they moved further away from Muxue City, they finally encountered a higher number of Demon Beasts.

Their Concealing Skill was excellent; they flew past the Demon Beasts without getting noticed, even those beasts that were a whole Realm higher in Cultivation Level.

Suddenly, everything before them went dark, and a mountainous silhouette appeared, exhaling a frosty breath. One Cultivator failed to react in time and got touched by the cold air, instantly freezing into an ice sculpture.

The team leader looked up and made eye contact with the mountainous figure, finally recognizing its identity. "It’s the Frost Elephant Demon King, run!"

Upon seeing the Frost Elephant Demon King, fear engulfed the others. Their rich experience living on the icy plains and fighting Demon Beasts suddenly became useless!

The shout from their leader finally snapped them out of their fear. They didn’t care about concealing their presence anymore and started to flee immediately.

Suddenly, a snake’s tongue appeared and swept another one of their companions away.

The White Snake hid within the ice and snow, almost invisible. They hadn’t noticed its presence all along.

"It’s the White Snake Demon King!"

One after another, creatures from the legends of the icy plains appeared, all of them Great Demon Kings. The people completely lost their ability to think, their gazes blank.

...

"Stick close together, don’t fall behind." Teacher Lu Doudou waved a small red flag, organizing a class outing.

"That’s odd, aren’t those Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall members? Why have they left the city?" two guards from Muxue City discussed quietly.

"No idea."

Shortly after the martial arts hall students left, the heavily injured team leader of the scouting team staggered back and collapsed at the city gate with a thud, startling the two guards who quickly went to check on him.

The team leader, lying in the arms of a guard, shakily raised his hand, pointing in the direction he came from.

"Quickly inform the City Lord, a beast tide is coming..."

"What, a beast tide?" The guards trembled. Everyone said beast tides were terrifying, but they had never heard of Muxue City encountering one in their lifetimes.

"Wait, didn’t the Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall members go in the direction of the beast tide?"

Outside Muxue City, Lu Yang led thirty-plus students, all bundled up in thick coats like little snowmen, except for Lu Yang, who wore a long jacket emblazoned with the Tiger Roar insignia, standing out from the rest.

"Teacher Lu, isn’t real-life training a bit too dangerous?" A student asked, shivering as he raised his hand. They had come enthusiastically for class, only to be told by Teacher Lu to go kill Demon Beasts in the wild?

These were Demon Beasts! Even their parents would run upon seeing one, how could they possibly stand a chance?

"You’ve trained in Tiger Roar Fist for so long, it’s time to test your Cultivation achievements. Don’t worry, I won’t pick too strong opponents for you."

The students groaned, although there was the powerful Teacher Lu watching over them, what if something uncontrollable happened?

Teacher Lu didn’t care much about what the students were thinking; he continued walking ahead with the flag, and the students had no choice but to follow.

"We have wolf tracks here; it should be this direction!" Lu Yang crouched and confidently pointed at the blank snowfield, like a seasoned hunter.

"Teacher, there are no footprints here?"

"The footprints have been covered by the heavy snow, You’re still young, you can’t see them, but the teacher can see the footprints beneath the snow."

The students looked up to Lu Yang with admiration.

Lu Yang was pleased with the students’ reactions and led them in the direction of the Wolf Demon his Spiritual Sense had detected.

After walking an unknown distance, Lu Yang raised his hand in a fist to signal everyone to stop.

He pointed behind a huge rock nearby and said, "See that big rock? There’s a Wolf Demon behind it. According to my experience, it’s a second-level Qi Refinement Wolf Demon, one-eyed and lame on the left leg, abandoned by its pack."

Fang Zhige looked bewildered, Can experience really tell all that?

"Gong Che and Shi Yi, you two command the students to encircle and suppress this Wolf Demon."

"Yes!"

Gong Che and Shi Yi shouted simultaneously, a mix of fear and excitement.

Among everyone, only Fang Zhige was composed; being at Great Perfection of Qi Refinement, dealing with these Qi Cultivation Stage Demon Beasts was a piece of cake.

"Little Fang, come here," Lu Yang gestured, calling Fang Zhige under a pine tree.

"You don’t have to fight with them, I’ll find you a Demon Beast from the Foundation Establishment Stage to deal with."

Fang Zhige took a few steps back in disbelief, staring at Lu Yang, who looked indifferent.

How could this be, how could Teacher Lu see through his true Cultivation Level, even the Golden Core Stage Master Murong couldn’t!

Could it be that Master Luo hadn’t been troubling him because Teacher Lu had dealt with Master Luo?

Thinking of this, Fang Zhige lowered his voice and sternly warned, "Teacher Lu, you shouldn’t stir this muddy water. Perhaps Master Luo isn’t a threat to you, but behind him is the endless bounty of the Ghost Killing Alliance, and above that is the Nine Ghosts Alliance. You’ll be facing relentless pursuit!"

Thinking that such a good teacher as Lu Doudou might face a life-threatening situation because of him made Fang Zhige extremely remorseful.

"Alright kid, don’t worry about it too much, go and fight your Demon Beast."

It was then that Fang Zhige suddenly realized his greatest problem, he was only at Great Perfection of Qi Refinement and had never fought a Demon Beast from the Foundation Establishment Stage.

His learned assassination techniques were useless against Demon Beasts.

"Walk north for about a mile, there you’ll find a snow fox at the Early Stage of Foundation Establishment, go fight it."

Lu Yang patted Fang Zhige on the back, urging him to hurry.

After Fang Zhige left, Lu Yang touched his chin and said, "Speaking of which, is the environment around Muxue City really that good? It took us so long to find a Demon Beast, and their realms aren’t even high."

"How do you see it, Fairy..."

"Awoo, awoo— I am currently a wolf, awoo—"

In the Spiritual Space, huge snowflakes fell like goose feathers, burying the Imperial Palace of the Great Bean Dynasty. The roof of the palace was indistinguishable, and it was snowing inside the palace too, covering everything from the ground to the throne.

Immortal Fairy, wearing a white wolf skin, leapt up and down in the snow, howling and waving its wolf claws. As soon as Lu Yang turned to ask a question, she pounced on him.

"What are you doing, Fairy?"

After Lu Yang staggered to his feet, he realized Immortal Fairy had draped him in an unknown Demon Beast’s fur while he wasn’t looking.

"This is Bei Skin."

"Isn’t there a proverb ’a collusion between wolves,’ which describes a very close relationship? Now, I am the wolf, and you are the Bei; our relationship is unbreakable!"

Chapter 1160: 1159: Making Up for the Regret of Never Having Battled a Demon Beast at the Qi Cultivation Stage

"His Majesty’s natural talent is as pure as it was at the beginning. With towering talents, he has created idioms based on the work of his predecessors—an exemplary model for all scholars in the land."

"Upon my return, I will ask Senior Qinghe to inscribe His Majesty’s newly minted idioms into the annals of history, so that they may be passed down through the ages, and let our names as ruler and subject be celebrated for a thousand years."

As the Emperor’s Master, Lu Yang had initially intended to correct Fairy Eternity’s misuse of idioms; however, upon reflection, he considered how could His Majesty make a mistake—these were not errors, these were idioms His Majesty had created himself.

They were indeed worthy of being recorded in the history books.

"Hehehe, feel free to lavish more praise on me."

Being praised so much by Little Yang, Fairy Eternity felt somewhat embarrassed and chuckled awkwardly while scratching the back of her head.

...

"Tiger Roar Fist!"

The immature roar of the tiger echoed, soft and feeble, lacking any intimidation.

Initially, the one-eyed, limping lone wolf faced the students’ siege with indifference—mere mortals delivering themselves as a meal—until, after receiving a couple of hits from Tiger Roar Fist, it realized something was amiss.

Though one or two punches did nothing to it, thirty-two students and sixty-four fists were unbearable.

The lone wolf went mad, trying to break through the encirclement, while Gong Che and Shi Yi stood ready, anticipating this very move.

Both Gong Che’s parents were cultivators, and Shi Yi came from a family of hunters; they had the experience to fend off both wild beasts and Demon Beasts.

"Tiger Roar!"

The roar of the tiger continued without cease; everyone’s fist techniques improved significantly.

Now, Gong Che finally understood the profundity of Teacher Lu’s painstaking efforts. Teacher Lu had intentionally made their Tiger Roar Fist sound immature. The enemy was less alert from the start—a forewarned strike against an unprepared foe, victorious even before the fight began!

The lone wolf howled mournfully and its soul departed to the Nether. The students were initially stunned, scarcely believing they had accomplished what even their parents could not, and soon erupted in cheers.

And this was a Wolf Demon at the second layer of Qi Refinement! Its fur was tattered from the battle, but it was still that of a Wolf Demon—valuable when sold.

"Skin the wolf along its grain," Shi Yi, coming from a family of hunters, most experienced in this area, instructed as he worked.

Then Fang Zhige returned, bloodied in face and body.

"Successful?" asked Lu Yang with a smile, legs crossed.

"Successful!"

While everyone was immersed in joy, Lu Yang’s expression suddenly shifted, and he struck out fiercely.

A snow-white object burst from the ground and leaped towards the students, who screamed instinctively at the imminent danger.

Bang—

Lu Yang appeared before them, his fist shattering the object.

It was a rabbit, at the stage of Qi Refinement Cultivators.

"It’s a Chewing Demon Rabbit!" Shi Yi cried out in shock.

One Demon Rabbit wasn’t much, but Chewing Demon Rabbits always appeared in swarms!

Fang Zhige’s expression also changed. In his studies of Demon Beasts, encountering such a rabbit meant one thing—run for your life!

Sure enough, more and more Demon Rabbits tumbled down from the snowy mountains, red-eyed, focusing on Lu Yang.

And among them, the leader was at the Foundation Establishment Stage!

Cold sweat dripped down Lu Yang’s forehead, his back chilled—a Foundation Establishment Stage Demon Rabbit, nearly a hundred Qi Refinement Stage Demon Rabbits, this array was formidable enough to defy even a Golden Core Stage cultivator.
