And now, with his own realm fluctuating towards Foundation Establishment, how could he possibly be a match for these Demon Rabbits?

He feared this would be his end.

If he perished, it was of little concern, but the children must not be harmed!

"Quick, run—I’ll hold them off!" While confronting the Demon Rabbits, Lu Yang turned with a serious shout, his eyes already filled with a resolve to die.

A Demon Rabbit sprang at him from a blind spot, only to be sent flying by Lu Yang’s punch.

"Teacher Lu!"

"Run, can’t you see? We’re just holding Teacher Lu back!"

"We’re no use here; Teacher Lu is blessed by heavens and will be safe!"

Gong Che and Shi Yi urged with tears in their eyes, choked up with emotion.

The students, knowing Gong Che and Shi Yi were right, retreated while calling out to Teacher Lu.

They had a sinking feeling that this farewell might be their last.

Only Fang Zhige looked strange—as if he had seen something amiss. Could it be that the Demon Rabbit killed by Teacher Lu’s punch was the same Foundation Establishment Stage Demon Rabbit?

Lu Yang’s eyes turned blood red as he threw himself into battle with the Demon Rabbits.

If only the auras of Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi were still present to scare off this group of Demon Rabbits, but it had been so long since he arrived in the Extreme Northern Lands that their auras had long since dissipated without a trace.

He could only rely on himself now.

"I’ll fight you with all I’ve got!"

Lu Yang dared not underestimate these Qi Cultivation Stage Demon Beasts; he had cultivated up to this point, battling demon beasts of all levels, but he had never actually fought against Qi Cultivation Stage Demon Beasts.

When facing Demon Beasts he had never crossed swords with before, one couldn’t be too careful!

Seeing their leader killed, the Demon Rabbits panicked and fled.

Lu Yang relentlessly pursued them, but a noise in the distance caught his attention.

He noticed that in the direction the Demon Rabbits had run towards, snow and fog whirled around, and a herd of Demon Beasts was running his way.

At the very front ran an Ice Plain Wolf, and behind it there seemed to be very large Demon Beasts, but their species was obscured by the snow and fog.

These Demon Rabbits couldn’t beat him and they still called for reinforcements?

"Fairy, what’s going on? Why are so many Demon Beasts running over here?" Lu Yang finally understood why he couldn’t find any Demon Beasts earlier; turns out they were all waiting for him here.

Imperial Fairy Eternity, clad in White Wolf fur, sat on the throne: "This looks like a beast tide. There are many reasons for one to form, such as a new Demon King trying to establish its authority, something in the city drawing them in, or their living environment being encroached upon."

"It was a similar chaos back when Immortal Jiuchong was capturing Golden Crows for breeding, with suns flying all over the sky."

"...I just went out for some extracurricular activities, and now they don’t even let people attend class. Forget it. First, I’ll deal with these Demon Wolves and Demon Rabbits."

The Demon Rabbits were agile, darting left and leaping right among the wolf pack, running deep into the beast tide.

Lu Yang pursued without rest. The leader of the Ice Plain Wolves, a Golden Core great demon, had its head blown apart at his first encounter.

The Ice Plain Wolves, with a lot of spine, sought revenge for their leader and were all pulverized by Lu Yang’s punches.

Then came a bear group, three zhang tall, with immense strength, thick hides, and in greater numbers than the wolf pack. There were also several Nascent Soul Stage Demon Bears, and with Lu Yang only at Foundation Establishment Stage, they were difficult to fight.

With his wooden sword activated, he swept through the bear group like a scythe through wheat, mercilessly harvesting them without even a whimper.

The wooden sword released a burst of Sword Qi and shattered, unable to be used again. Lu Yang had to continue taking out new small wooden swords from his Identity Jade.

Later, finding it too bothersome to fetch wooden swords this way, he dumped all the swords from the sack, using his intention to float them behind his back, creating a forest of wooden swords.

For the cities in the Extreme Northern Lands, a beast tide was a cataclysmic disaster. Not only because of the presence of several Great Demon Kings but also because the endless hordes of Demon Beasts made the Human race’s skin crawl.

But no matter how numerous, these Demon Beasts were no match for Lu Yang.

Lu Yang strolled leisurely through the beast tide, his fingers lightly hooking forward, sending the small wooden swords flying and felling swathes of Demon Beasts.

By the end, even the irrational Demon Beasts regained their senses, afraid to charge any further, and stared in terror at this seemingly weak member of the Human race.

The Demon Rabbits were too fast and difficult to find amidst the beast tide, especially as Lu Yang was sweeping through with wooden swords that couldn’t quite reach the smaller creatures.

"Found them."

Lu Yang shielded his brow with his palm and spotted the Demon Rabbits not far off.

Shick—

The wooden sword flew out and shattered, slaughtering the group of Demon Rabbits.

Lu Yang wiped the nonexistent sweat from his forehead, finally having killed them all. It hadn’t been easy.

Boom—

The stomp of an elephant’s foot, dozens of zhang thick, shook the earth, capturing Lu Yang’s attention.

The massive shapes emerged from the snow fog, revealing their true forms.

Mountainous Frost Elephants, White Snakes that carved rivers on the ground as they slid, Sword-toothed Tiger Kings that could shatter mountaintops with a single leap, Northern Whales treating the snow as their sea... Even the commanders of Muxue City’s guards would shiver at the sight of these Ice Plain’s infamous Great Demon Kings!

The Frost Elephant exhaled white fog, and the snow fog turned out to be the breath of the mountainous Frost Elephants!

Lu Yang subconsciously continued to gesture with his fingers, but no wooden swords flew out. Turning his head, he realized he had run out of small wooden swords.

Roar Roar—

Guffaw Guffaw—

The Great Demon Kings made various sounds, mocking Lu Yang’s predicament. The only weapon that could threaten them, the wooden swords, was gone, so what could this tiny Foundation Establishment Stage Cultivator do?

The infectious laughter of the Great Demon Kings spread among the beast pack, causing all the Demon Beasts in the beast tide to laugh along.

Seeing this, Lu Yang could only sigh in resignation and took out another sack of small wooden swords from his Identity Jade.

The previous swords had been refined during his Body Integration Realm; these were refined during his Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

The laughter of the Great Demon Kings came to an abrupt halt.

Chapter 1161: 1160 Swordsmanship Supreme (Extra - for Monthly Votes)

"City Lord, terrible news! The third scout team reports that a Great Demon King is attacking from outside the city, likely heralding a beast tide!"

"What did you say?!" City Lord Zhang’s pupils constricted sharply, and he abruptly stood up, knocking over the table with his vigorous movement.

"Quickly summon the captain of the guard, the other two vice city lords..."

The attack by the Great Demon King and the outbreak of a beast tide quickly alarmed the entire upper echelon of Muxue City.

The atmosphere in the city lord’s council hall was as heavy as leaden clouds, and the maid who was serving tea and water was extremely cautious, fearing to offend and incur the wrath of these great figures.

"According to what the captain of the third scout team said, are the scant numbers of demon beasts outside Muxue City due to them being summoned by the Great Demon King?"

"And not just one Great Demon King, but including the Frost Elephant Demon King and the White Snake Demon King, plus two other Great Demon Kings that were not clearly identified?" Vice City Lord Yao was deeply moved.

"Is the information confirmed? Why isn’t the captain of the third scout team here to report this?"

"After he reported to the gate guards, he fell into a severe coma from his injuries. What, does Vice City Lord Ma have a way to awaken him?" Commander Gongsun squinted his eyes and asked, detecting Vice City Lord Ma’s unpleasant tone.

"How could it not be possible? There are four Great Demon Kings, led by the Frost Elephant Demon King who has ravaged the ice plains for over a thousand years, trampling numerous Unification Cultivators to death. It is rumored that it has long advanced to the Integration Late Stage. Facing the Frost Elephant Demon King, how could a mere scout team manage to escape?"

"I suspect that this captain has been bought by another city and is a spy fabricating false information to deceive us into chaos!"

Commander Gongsun angrily said, "Nonsense, you even suspect that the man I chose is a fraud!"

Suddenly, the council hall became tense, with the two Unification Cultivators seeming ready to clash at a moment’s notice.

The city lord, who had been silent all this time, finally spoke. He possessed absolute authority in the city; it wouldn’t be an exaggeration to say that he built Muxue City himself: "Enough, Ma. This matter must be true. Although demon beasts lack spiritual wisdom, the Frost Elephant Demon King, being a seasoned demon of the Integration Late Stage, could be cunning, which is not strange."

"Letting the scout team captain escape was probably done on purpose to let us know of this in advance and provoke us."

Everyone fell silent. The city lord’s words were like the final verdict. Despite all five present being in the Body Integration Realm, facing four Great Demon Kings, along with the fierce and fearless beast tide, they still felt powerless.

"We can only face battle now."

The city lord stood up and spoke in a solemn voice; Muxue City was his life’s work, he couldn’t just give it up.

"Put the entire city on alert. Vice City Lord Ma, gather cultivators from the city’s sects, academies, and factions, regardless of their cultivation levels; all must join the battle!"

"Commander Gongsun, deploy the guard forces for citywide defense."

Commands were issued, and though the battle had not yet started, everyone felt the tense atmosphere, as if a storm was about to break.

"Vice City Lord Yao, take a few people to scout out the enemy situation and determine the scale of the beast tide. Remember, do not approach the Great Demon Kings!"

"Yes!"

...

Amidst a raging blizzard, Vice City Lord Yao took responsibility in a crisis, readying his gear and leading a few particularly brave confidants in silence.

The confidants were trembling with fear; though bold, they were not dead soldiers. If detected by the Great Demon Kings, not even Vice City Lord Yao could protect them, let alone fend for himself.

"Vice City Lord Yao, why don’t we send a few Nascent Soul Stage cultivators to scout, and maybe we shouldn’t go ourselves?"

"Nonsense, what could Nascent Soul Stage cultivators discern? Are you thinking of deserting in the face of battle? If we can’t withstand this beast tide, we might have a chance to escape, but what about our families—will you abandon them?"

Truthfully, Vice City Lord Yao was also afraid, but what good would being afraid do? Sooner or later, they had to face the beast tide; gathering early intelligence and preparing could offer a sliver of hope.

"But..."

"Silence, we are approaching the beast tide!"

Several confidants were so frightened that they dared not speak.

They approached quietly, their brows furrowing gradually, their hearts uneasy.

Why was this beast tide so unusually quiet, so silent that it made their hearts chill?

They summoned their courage to approach and saw an unbelievable scene, their hearts skipping several beats.

Demon Beast corpses were piled up like mountains, and the large snow, like goose feathers, almost covered the bodies and masked the smell of blood. The Demon Beasts died with their eyes wide open, as if they had encountered unspeakable terror.

"Could it be, could it be that the Great Demon Kings are fighting among themselves?" one confidant asked shakily, finding his own question somewhat amusing.

What kind of being killed these Demon Beasts, and was it still here?

Thinking of this, their hearts rose to their throats, and they dared not breathe.

They landed quietly, carefully observing their surroundings, fearing to disturb that terrifying powerful enemy.

Several huge figures blocked their way; the north wind blew, dispersing the chill enveloping these figures, and the people saw clearly what the huge figures looked like, a chill running from their feet to the top of their heads.

The corpses of the Great Demon Kings.

"Look quickly, it’s the Northern Realm Whale King, said to be a side branch of the Kunpeng Clan, swimming in the snow! It’s been split in two!"

"That’s the Sword-toothed Tiger King, which had once stirred up a beast tide that devastated Yong’an City, guarded by two Combined Great Abilities, including Vice City Lord Ma, who then escaped to our Muxue City!"

"That’s the White Snake King, reputed to have unparalleled Invisibility Technique. Even Combined Great Abilities have been assassinated to death by it!"

Suddenly a confidant fell to the ground with a thump, shivering and pointing at the huge obstacle, "The, the Frost Elephant Great Demon King has been beheaded!"

Even after death, the Frost Elephant Great Demon King was still emitting a cold air, showing how terrifying it was in life.

But no matter how terrifying its legends were, today it too lay dead outside the city in the icy snowy wilderness.

Vice City Lord Yao, emboldened, examined the wounds, his expression growing more solemn.

The wounds were as neat as a mirror, whether it was the Little Demons or the Frost Elephant Great Demon King, all were cut down by one sword stroke, no second move needed.

"Swordsmanship... supremely honored."

...

The guards exchanged wry smiles; if a cultivator at the Foundation Establishment Stage encountered a Chewing Demon Rabbit, how could he possibly survive? Probably by the time these kids got here, that Teacher Lu had already been gnawed to bones by the Demon Rabbit.

Even if they had the ability to save him, with the current beast tide, and with the strict orders from above not allowing anyone to leave the city without the commander’s token, it was impossible for them to save anyone.

Fang Zhige, who originally had great confidence in Teacher Lu, turned pale upon hearing of the beast tide.

No matter how powerful Teacher Lu was, he could not possibly survive in the beast tide; they of the Nine Ghosts Alliance also faced the beast tide like trying to stop a car with mantis arms.

Gong Che looked towards the snowy wilderness outside the city, suddenly excited, "Look, is that Teacher Lu?"

Fang Zhige shook his head, "How could it be..."

"Classmates, Teacher is back." Lu Yang, carrying a sack of spoils, all rabbits.

"Though there were quite a few enemies, luckily I have more life-saving techniques and managed to survive by luck," Lu Yang said with lingering fear.

Chapter 1162: 1161: Man-Made Beast Tide

The city gate guards looked at Lu Yang as if they had seen a ghost. The descriptions from the students suggested that Lu Yang faced a situation where death was almost certain. Could this be explained by having just enough life-saving measures?

Lu Yang returned to Muxue City, accompanied by the students, but his presence was out of sync with the tense atmosphere of the city.

As the beast tide approached, Muxue City prepared for war, all cultivators were conscripted.

Before entering the martial arts hall, Lu Yang told his classmates to go home, declaring that today’s extracurricular activity had been very successful.

The martial arts hall was empty. Lu Yang inquired nearby and learned that the City Lord had gathered all the cultivators in the city to combat the beast tide, and Murong Da Ye and the others, as cultivators, had all been summoned.

"Speaking of which, who exactly triggered this beast tide?"

Lu Yang lay on his bed, recalling the remnants of a beast taming technique within the bodies of the Four Great Demon Kings, and murmured to himself.

This meant that the beast tide was not the natural disaster outsiders believed it to be, but a genuine man-made calamity.

"Is it to overwhelm Muxue City with the beast tide?"

Controlling the Four Great Demon Kings with a beast taming technique, the practitioner must definitely be a Pass Tribulation Cultivator. For a Pass Tribulation Cultivator, destroying Muxue City would be an easy task.

"Do they not want to make a move personally, or is there another agenda?"

Suddenly, there was a commotion in the martial arts hall.

"Did you see Vice City Lord Ma’s expression? Laughable, I thought he could only scowl, turns out he has other faces too."

"Alright, alright, keep your voices down. If Vice City Lord Ma’s people hear us, we’re finished."

When Lu Yang went outside, it turned out Sect Leader Murong Da Ye and his disciples had returned.

"Little Lu, you’re okay? I heard you took the kids outside the city to hunt demons and scared me to death," Murong Da Ye said with concern.

"Just lucky, didn’t encounter any powerful demon beasts."

"Hey, that’s why I say your luck isn’t just bragging; it’s real good. When you went out, the beast tide struck; had you encountered it, it would have been the end."

"Yeah, yeah, it was a city-destroying level beast tide. Even the City Lord had no answers," the inner sect disciples chattered, having encountered the legendary beast tide for the first time in their lives.

Lu Yang remained silent with a smile.

"I heard from a neighbor that you all were called by the City Lord to deal with the beast tide?"

"Exactly, I thought I was almost gone for good this time," Murong Da Ye said, still shaken, slapping his thigh.

"Don’t worry, sit down and take it easy," Lu Yang said smilingly, getting things organized. "I caught a few wild rabbits outside the city, let’s roast and eat them together."

"Ah, that’s wonderful," Murong Da Ye beamed with joy, his disciples also thrilled. In the barren Extreme Northern Lands, having a barbecue was one of the few forms of entertainment.

Lu Yang expertly set up the grill and listened to Murong Da Ye tell stories as he roasted the meat.

"At first, when Vice City Lord Ma summoned us, we were very scared. After all, it’s a beast tide. What could we, who are just at the stages of Qi Refinement, Foundation Establishment, and Golden Core, do? We were clearly going to our deaths."

"That Ma fellow even feigned concern, saying it was all for the continuation of Muxue City, asking us to hand over our cultivation resources for centralized allocation. If he’s so capable, let him charge at the beast tide first. It’s all nonsense, I think he just wanted to embezzle things. If the city were breached, he’d be the first to grab stuff and flee!"

"After a lot of persuasion, Vice City Lord Ma, seeing that we were unwilling to hand over our cultivation resources, started getting agitated. That’s when Vice City Lord Yao arrived, saying there was no need to worry about the beast tide anymore."

"A Supreme Power slew the Four Great Demon Kings with a single sword strike, killing tens, if not hundreds of thousands of the Demon Race, directly resolving the source of the beast tide."

"At first, Ma still said Vice City Lord Yao shouldn’t spread false military information and shake the army’s morale. But once he led a team out of the city and returned, he was utterly demoralized, talking about how it couldn’t be possible, and he was also seriously injured, hurt by the residual Sword Qi."

The apprentice chimed in longingly, "I also heard that the City Lord himself inspected the situation and said that someone at the peak of Sword Dao Supreme made their move."

"Sword Dao Supreme, if I ever get the chance to meet this Sword Dao Supreme, my life would not be in vain."

Lu Yang served four grilled rabbit legs and joked, "What’s so great about a Sword Dao Supreme?"

The apprentice said with a chuckle, "Daoist Lu, you wouldn’t know, but what if the Sword Dao Supreme took a liking to me and passed me a few sword techniques? Then I could walk sideways through Muxue City."

Murong Grandpa took a bite of grilled rabbit leg, his eyes lit up, "Hey, Little Lu, your cooking skills are impressive. You could open a shop with this—In Muxue City, I’ve eaten barbecue for nearly a hundred years, and none is better than yours!"

"You’re exaggerating, exaggerating."

...

Ma Family estate.

Ever since Vice City Lord Ma returned home, he had secluded himself, not even meeting his own family.

Vice City Lord Ma sat cross-legged on a straw mat, eyes closed, gritting his teeth, his hands cupped, as beads of sweat trickled down from his forehead, across his chin, down his cheeks, and dropped onto the ground.

The Sword Qi of the Sword Dao Supreme remained in his body, and he tried various methods to expel it quickly but could only wear it down bit by bit.

But that was not his concern. He suddenly opened his eyes, murmuring to himself, "This can’t be, this can’t be..."

"What can’t be?" A weathered voice resounded in Vice City Lord Ma’s ear.

"Who is there?"

Vice City Lord Ma tensed up. This was his cultivation place, absolutely safe; even the City Lord couldn’t just barge in.

"How is it, still have a trace of my Sword Qi inside you and don’t recognize me?"

Vice City Lord Ma’s pupils dilated, his voice filled with agitation, "It’s you, no, it’s You!"

It was the Supreme Sword Master who had slain the Four Great Demon Kings with a single sword strike!

"Vice City Lord Ma thinks something is impossible? Is it that you thought the beast swarm that was supposed to attack the city was halted by me?"

Vice City Lord Ma felt the Sword Master’s oppressive presence and fell to his knees with a thud, beads of sweat the size of soybeans forming on his forehead.

"No, that’s not what I mean," Vice City Lord Ma stammered.

Sword Master withdrew his oppressive presence, noticing Vice City Lord Ma was also a Sword Cultivator. Faced with an overwhelming Sword Cultivator’s pressure, it would be impossible for him to lie.

"I... I never imagined a Sword Cultivator could cultivate to such a level," Vice City Lord Ma said bitterly. As a Sword Cultivator, he knew all too well the chasm between him and the Supreme Sword Master.

Yet he refused to accept this, recklessly touching the Sword Qi left in the Great Demon King’s body, sustaining heavy injuries.

He realized that there was an insurmountable chasm between himself and a true Sword Cultivator, and he would never be able to reach across it in this lifetime, plunging into despair.

Sword Master sneered, "As a Sword Cultivator, yet impure in thought, facing the beast swarm you only thought of abandoning the city, spineless. Yet you aspire to climb to the pinnacle of Sword Dao, laughable."

"In your life, have you ever wielded your sword against someone more powerful than yourself?"

Vice City Lord Ma felt as if struck by lightning.

The Sword Master withdrew the Sword Qi from Vice City Lord Ma’s body and vanished.

Lu Yang appeared outside the Ma Family estate, shaking his head, "It seems he has nothing to do with the beast swarm incident."

Chapter 1163: 1162: Why Are There So Many Problematic Students?

When the people of Muxue City learned that a Supreme Expert had helped them fend off the beast tide, sparing the city from disaster without any casualties, everyone was very pleased.

But then, the more enterprising cultivators started eyeing the corpses of the demon beasts.

These demon beasts were all at least at the Foundation Establishment Stage, and selling just one could bring in a large number of spirit stones.

However, a very realistic problem soon presented itself—the demon beasts were indeed dealt with by the Supreme Expert, but the Supreme Expert’s Sword Qi also remained within the bodies of the beasts.

Touching the Sword Qi was a lesson already learned by Vice City Lord Ma.

Now, more than a month had passed since the disaster of the beast tide, and the Sword Qi still lingered in the demon beasts’ bodies, almost unchanged.

The cultivators of Muxue City sighed, it seemed that only time could wear down the Sword Qi.

At this moment, the Supreme Sword Master was methodically teaching the children the Tiger Roar Fist.

"Very good, very good, having experienced real combat really makes a difference. As usual, next we’ll start sparring in groups."

Lu Yang was very satisfied with the students’ progress in cultivating the Tiger Roar Fist. Ever since they had fought desperately against that second-level Qi Refinement lone wolf, their boldness had greatly increased.

While mastering the techniques of the Fist was important, boldness was also a crucial factor.

Take Meng Jingzhou for example, Meng Jingzhou was full of vigor, making rapid progress in Fist techniques day by day, to a point where even Lu Yang couldn’t keep up with him.

These students, if placed in the outside world, could also be revered as Great Martial Masters, standing out among ordinary people. However, the students had no concept of their own strength and didn’t know how strong they were.

Their exclamations interrupted Lu Yang’s thoughts.

"Su Yuan actually won?"

"Isn’t he the least talented among us, losing every single time?"

"This is his first win, right?"

"Although Gao Yifan’s strength is below average, he shouldn’t lose to Su Yuan."

Listening to the students’ discussions, Lu Yang roughly understood what had happened—Su Yuan, who had always been at the bottom in terms of talent, had won in this group sparring session.

He had noticed this kid Su Yuan; he was very hardworking, but his talent was indeed very poor.

It was said that he was an orphan, having survived by wandering in his early years. Perhaps it was during that time that he depleted his health, and without cultivator parents like Gong Che, naturally, his foundation was much weaker.

"Su Yuan won, not bad, not bad. It looks like the private training was not in vain." Lu Yang led the applause, always advocating for encouraging education.

"Teacher, I don’t even know what happened just now, it just happened out of the blue," Su Yuan said, feeling embarrassed.

"Bullshit, it was clearly because I was careless!" Gao Yifan angrily said. He could lose to anyone, but losing to Su Yuan, who was the least talented, was too humiliating!

Lu Yang watched the two students with interest: "Is that so? Why don’t you two spar again?"

"Old Gao, are you up for it?"

"Let’s go!"

As the roar of the Tiger sounded, Su Yuan and Gao Yifan began their second round of sparring. It was clear that Su Yuan was at a disadvantage, something all the other students could see.

Suddenly, Su Yuan changed his fist into a claw, reaching for Gao Yifan. Gao Yifan, caught off guard and unsure how to respond, could only retreat.

Su Yuan quickly advanced, launching a rapid punch that broke through Gao Yifan’s defense and knocked him to the ground.

"Su Yuan won again!"

The students exclaimed in surprise. If the first win was an accident, then the second win was a demonstration of true skill.

"Not bad, you’ve indeed improved. But how did you think of changing from fist to claw just now?" Lu Yang asked. That move was the essence of the Tiger Roar Fist, something that couldn’t be explicitly taught but had to be realized intuitively—Su Yuan had seemingly grasped it.

"I just felt it should be done that way."

"Looks like your efforts have paid off. That’s all for today’s lesson," Lu Yang clapped his hands.

...

After the class, Su Yuan looked panicked and hurried back to his thatched house to confront the other voice that had appeared in his mind.

"Who are you, and why won’t you let me tell Teacher Lu the truth!"

Su Yuan demanded. While he was sparring with Gao Yifan, a voice suddenly appeared in his mind, instructing him on his moves.

He was no match for Gao Yifan, but by following the voice’s commands, he won against Gao Yifan twice in succession.

When Lu Yang asked him why he made those moves, he had intended to tell his teacher about the voice in his mind, but the voice stopped him from doing so.

For some reason, that voice made him feel deeply convinced.

"Are you hiding something guilty, that you don’t want me to speak about it!"

"You are naive and do not understand the perils of human nature. My existence is a great secret, and also your opportunity. I have never heard of anyone revealing their opportunities."

"With my help, mastering Tiger Roar Fist and defeating Shi Yi and Gong Che will be effortless."

"But you should improve gradually; rapid changes might attract the attention of those with intentions," the voice said, sounding genuinely considerate for Su Yuan.

However, Su Yuan still didn’t trust the voice and asked cautiously, "Who are you, and why are you inside my body!"

"Who am I?" The voice paused when questioned about its identity, sounding melancholic, "I don’t know who I am either. Maybe someday, you can help me find the answer."

"Really, that sounds quite mysterious." Suddenly, a new voice chimed in, and Su Yuan was delighted.

"Teacher Lu!"

Surprisingly, Teacher Lu had appeared in his home without him knowing.

Lu Yang displayed a mentor-like smile, "I’ve come for a home visit. I hope I’m not interrupting anything?"

The voice did not appear again.

"Come on, no need to hide, I heard everything you both were talking about."

The voice still did not emerge.

Lu Yang waited patiently, gazing at Su Yuan, until finally, the voice couldn’t maintain its composure and spoke with an undertone of threat.

"Kid, though I can’t recall my past, I remember that I was a Combined Great Ability before my death. You, a mere Foundation Establishment Stage cultivator, have no business delving into it!"

Although it was a threat, the voice didn’t emit a convincing aura.

"Combined Great Ability, huh? That does sound strong." Lu Yang sat down and gestured for Su Yuan to sit as well.

Lu Yang then took out two leaves, one for each of them.

"Is this, is this Enlightenment Tea?!" the voice was a bit shocked, surprised that something like this couldn’t be offered by a Foundation Establishment Stage Cultivator.

Who exactly is he!

Lu Yang shook his head, realizing the other couldn’t even recognize that the leaves came from the Enlightenment Tea Tree King.

But it didn’t matter. A senior was still a senior, deserving of respect regardless of their awareness.

He leaned back in his chair, crossed his arms, and scrutinized Su Yuan.

"If I’m not mistaken, senior has been residing in Su Yuan for about a decade, right? Was it because I took Su Yuan out to hunt Demon Beasts that you were awoken, or was it the Yang Qi from the Tiger Roar Fist that stimulated you?"

The voice continued to be silent for a while before finally saying, "The Tiger Roar Fist you taught is not ordinary."

If there were outsiders present, they would realize that Lu Yang was not using Spiritual Sense to talk in the spiritual space; it was a very normal verbal conversation, while the voice spoke using Su Yuan’s body.

Chapter 1164: 163 The Number One Person with Swordsmanship Talent in the Extreme Northern Lands

Listening to Lu Yang speaking to the mysterious voice, Su Yuan felt uneasy. Seizing the moment when no one paid attention to him, he secretly drank a sip of Enlightenment Tea, and immediately felt his limbs and body lighten up as if he didn’t exist, making every movement exceptionally easy.

"This, this is..."

The voice was astonished; throughout his life, he had drunk Enlightenment Tea countless times, but never had a batch been so powerful.

What year’s Enlightenment Tea is this?

There shouldn’t be tea of such caliber in the Extreme Northern Lands. Moreover, what was the identity of the person who could procure such tea effortlessly?

"Exactly who are you?" the voice toned down its arrogance, realizing that Lu Yang’s origins far exceeded its imagination, most likely belonging to someone of equal stature during its life, or perhaps even a more powerful being.

The only beings more powerful would be those in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

"When asking someone’s identity, you should introduce yourself first."

The voice sighed deeply, "What you said is very true, but I really have forgotten my identity."

Lu Yang narrowed his eyes slightly, "Then what do you remember?"

"I am a cultivator from the Extreme Northern Lands at the Integration Peak, having faced a great catastrophe, I intended to possess the body of a frozen beggar in the city. Unexpectedly, after possessing it, I fell into a deep sleep, and the little beggar came back to life."

Hearing this, Su Yuan was quite shocked, "That little beggar..."

"Is you."

Su Yuan looked dazed; his childhood memories were very vague and he couldn’t remember what had happened.

"Faced a great catastrophe, what catastrophe?" Lu Yang stroked his chin, curious about the event itself.

"I can’t remember clearly, only that it was an enemy so powerful that it couldn’t be defeated."

The enemy that forced an Integration Peak cultivator to flee by possession was either on par with his own master’s capability or had to be a Pass Tribulation Cultivator.

"Su Yuan, since nothing major is happening at your home, your teacher is relieved." Lu Yang couldn’t obtain more information and prepared to leave.

Su Yuan exclaimed in surprise. Was this really considered nothing major at home?

"Let’s stop here," Lu Yang advised before leaving, "That voice won’t harm you, but don’t take everything he says at face value. He himself just said that you lack experience and don’t understand the dangers of human hearts. You should still take precautions."

"You!" The voice seemed somewhat angry.

Lu Yang pretended not to hear and left Su Yuan’s house.

"Sir, a roasted sweet potato please." On his way back, Lu Yang rubbed his ears and reddened them, pretending to look as cold as the passersby.

"I didn’t notice this before, but Su Yuan’s soul is quite strong," Lu Yang said while eating the sweet potato.

He gently peeled off the crispy skin to reveal the enticingly glossy, orange-yellow flesh inside. The steam slowly rose, condensing into tiny dewdrops in the cold air, mingling with the falling snow, covering Lu Yang’s face. Taking a bite, the soft, glutinous, sweetly fragrant texture enveloped his taste buds.

Su Yuan’s soul had reached Integration Peak, a level no cultivator in Muxue City could discern unless Lu Yang’s own soul fluctuation was at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage; otherwise, the hints were unnoticeable.

"It’s fine, it’s just at the Integration Realm." In the Spiritual Space, Immortal Fairy constructed a semicircular small house out of snow, lazily lying inside, not feeling the cold at all.

"It’s definitely a split personality."

Su Yuan didn’t know that if one were to possess another, he would, just like Lu Yang, engage in conversation with Immortal Fairy in the Spiritual Space using Su Yuan’s body the entire time.

That is to say, that voice was actually Su Yuan as well.

There was only one soul within Su Yuan’s body to corroborate this conclusion.

According to Lu Yang’s conjecture, it was ten years ago that the voice fought a great battle and, upon losing, possessed the body of a deceased beggar in Muxue City.

Whether it was because the voice was gravely injured, or because the beggar’s body was too weak, the possession encountered a mishap; the voice fell into a deep slumber and also lost its memories, splitting off a sliver of soul to become the new beggar, who was Su Yuan.

Now, Su Yuan was cultivating Tiger Roar Fist, and the masculine energy it generated had awakened that voice.

From their brief exchange, the voice had not realized that Su Yuan was another personality of his.

Perhaps the voice would continually tempt Su Yuan, persuading him to accept its power and thus take control of Su Yuan’s body, or maybe Su Yuan would resist the temptation and gain the power of the voice.

The final outcome would depend on Su Yuan’s own choices.

...

"Did you hear? Xue Shilou is coming to our Muxue City." During the break in the middle of class, students gathered together, speaking in hushed tones and gesticulating continuously, their eyebrows twitching; it was evident that Xue Shilou held much significance to them.

"That hero Xue Shilou who punishes the wicked and routes out evil?" Gong Che asked excitedly; Xue Shilou’s reputation for upholding justice was well known across the Extreme Northern Lands, and what young person wouldn’t admire him?

He was the number one in Swordmanship Talent in the Extreme Northern Lands, a title alone that could attract countless admirers.

"Yes, that’s him. It’s said he was drawn here by the Sword Qi of that unparalleled Sword Cultivator during the beast tide, wanting to observe those Sword Qi."

"I also heard he was once an opponent of Talent Lu, Lu Yang, is that true?" Shi Yi, very excited, could hardly stay seated.

"It is true. They were opponents at the Sword Inquiry Ceremony held in the Sword Tower. It’s said that among the opponents Talent Lu encountered during that ceremony, aside from Ming Lou, the successor of the Sword Tower, Xue Shilou was the strongest," Gong Che said, his family being cultivators knew more than the average person.

A duel between such formidable opponents was always a captivating topic, especially involving talents like Xue Shilou and Lu Yang, who were renowned throughout both the Extreme Northern Lands and the entire Cultivation World.

Ah, Xue Shilou, Lu Yang was briefly stunned when he heard this name; after hearing Gong Che elaborating, he then recalled who Xue Shilou was.

Indeed, Xue Shilou was a formidable opponent he encountered at the Sword Inquiry Ceremony; against other competitors he would only execute a single sword strike, but to defeat Xue Shilou, he had unleashed three full strikes.

"Alright, stop discussing, class is starting," Lu Yang shouted, the students reluctantly responded, engrossed as they were in their discussions.

The door of the Martial Arts Hall suddenly burst open with a whoosh of cold wind rushing inward, and a swordsman in black appeared, quickly shutting the door.

Xue Shilou sighed in relief; someone had inadvertently recognized him, causing the whole street to swarm with people, and unfamiliar with the place, he could only hide somewhere arbitrarily.

As Xue Shilou turned around, he noticed thirty-three students and sixty-six eyes staring directly at him, which made him somewhat embarrassed.

Suddenly, he noticed the teaching instructor, backed away several steps, nearly tripping over the threshold: "Is it you?"

He hurriedly looked up at the signboard overhead—yes, this was the Martial Arts Hall, not the City Lord’s residence.

"Focus, this gentleman is a friend of the instructor," Lu Yang said calmly, then glanced again at Xue Shilou.

Xue Shilou nodded woodenly.

Chapter 1165: 1164: A New Teacher Added

"Goodbye, Teacher Lu."

After class, the students left in small groups, walking home together. Each of them glanced at the corner, where a black-clothed swordsman with seemingly no presence sat quietly as they passed the gate.

After all the students had left, Xue Shilou finally spoke.

"You seem quite amused, Daoist Lu," Xue Shilou managed to say after holding it in for so long.

Sword cultivators typically aren’t talkative, with Daoist Buyu and Lu Yang being the two exceptions.

Xue Shilou really didn’t know what to say. Teaching a group of students fist techniques, it seemed they were unaware of Lu Yang’s identity; otherwise, their reaction wouldn’t have been so indifferent.

Why teach them? Teach me! How much does your martial arts hall charge per lesson? I want to sign up!

"Just experiencing life. I’ve heard you have a pretty good reputation in the Extreme Northern Lands, Snow Daoist," Lu Yang teased.

During the Sword Inquiry Ceremony at the Sword Tower, Xue Shilou was somewhat famous in the Extreme Northern Lands. Not seeing him for several years, Lu Yang didn’t expect his fame in the Extreme Northern Lands to have grown so much that he could carry the title of the one with the best swordmanship talent in the Extreme Northern Lands.

With cultivation level at the late stage of Spirit Transformation, his talent indeed allowed him to walk sideways in the Extreme Northern Lands. Even if he encountered a Great Demon King, he could escape unscathed.

"It’s all just hype from the people. I’m nowhere near as renowned as Daoist Lu," Xue Shilou admitted. He knew his fame only extended to the Extreme Northern Lands and beyond that, no one would recognize him.

But Lu Yang was different. It wouldn’t be an exaggeration to say that his name was known across the world.

It was as if Xue Shilou finally found a topic he could talk about and took the initiative to engage in conversation: "The Sword Qi on the Frost Elephant Demon King’s corpse is truly awe-inspiring. Even if I cultivated to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, I would have no confidence in unleashing a sword strike to match it."

"I never expected such a marvel in our Extreme Northern Lands. A single sword strike vanquished the Four Great Demon Kings so cleanly and efficiently. The Sword Qi lingers on without fading. I’ve heard that the sword cultivator Vice City Lord of Muxue City, upon witnessing that Sword Qi, was so ashamed that he nearly mutilated himself and almost gave up on swordsmanship."

"I have a premonition that the key to my breakthrough to the Void Refinement Stage lies here in Muxue City. Observing this Sword Qi and comprehending a fraction of its mysteries will surely elevate my Sword Intent to a new level."

"Speaking of which, why is Daoist Lu in Muxue City? Are you also here to observe that Sword Qi?"

"Oh, that Sword Qi was left by me."

"Cough cough—" Xue Shilou suddenly choked and coughed violently, his cheeks flushing with embarrassment, almost coughing out his lungs.

"You?!"

Lu Yang was somewhat surprised by Xue Shilou’s reaction, "Didn’t you know, Snow Daoist, I’m currently at the Void Refinement Stage, spending my days here amidst the mortal realm."

"You’re at the Void Refinement Stage?!"

Xue Shilou coughed even more violently. At the time of the Sword Inquiry Ceremony, he was at the mid-stage of Transforming Spirit while Lu Yang was at the initial stage. Then he had been forcefully taken across the border by his Senior Brother and captured by Laurel Fairy Palace, where with their help, he truly awakened his Snow Emperor Bloodline. After diving into intensive cultivation in the Extreme Northern Lands, how could it be that Lu Yang’s cultivation progress had surpassed his own?

"That’s wrong, though. Even if you’re at the Void Refinement Stage, the realm fluctuation reaching the Body Integration Realm wouldn’t be enough to deliver such a strike!"

"Oh, you’re talking about that. It happened when I accidentally triggered an unprecedented Thunder Tribulation during my breakthrough to the Void Refinement Stage, which made my Void Refinement Stage somewhat different from others. My realm fluctuation ranges from the Qi Cultivation Stage to the Pass Tribulation Stage."

Xue Shilou was completely numb by this point. He felt he shouldn’t have come to Muxue City.

Even if he thought he had a strong Dao heart, it couldn’t withstand such continual blows.

"Will Daoist Lu be staying in Muxue City for a while?"

"Given the constant fluctuation in my realm, it could be quite dangerous to go to other places. I’ll be staying at this martial arts hall for the time being."

"Is the martial arts hall still recruiting? Can I stay here?" Xue Shilou asked excitedly.

He had originally planned to stay in Muxue City for some time to learn from the Sword Qi on the Frost Elephant Demon King’s corpse. Now that Lu Yang was here, there was no need to look elsewhere.

"You’ll have to ask Master Murong about that. Oh, Master Murong is the Sect Leader of the Tiger Roar Sect."

Lu Yang realized what Xue Shilou meant, but he didn’t mind; after all, it didn’t matter whom he taught.

Xue Shilou straightened his clothes and went to visit the Sect Leader of the Tiger Roar Sect.

He wasn’t afraid of Murong You recognizing him. Although he was well-known, few people in the Extreme Northern Lands had actually seen his true face. He was once recognized by accident when someone shouted in excitement upon seeing him, causing a rumor-fueled chase after him.

Of course, the most important reason was that he did not know the Disguise Skill.

Sword Cultivators are straightforward in their actions, how could they engage in activities that require hiding and sneaking around?

Master Murong’s Abode was located in the deepest part of the Martial Arts Hall’s backyard. Lu Yang brought Xue Shilou over: "Is Master Murong here?"

"Come in."

Lu Yang and Xue Shilou entered.

"Little Lu, who is this?"

"Hello, Master Murong, my surname is Xue. I’m a friend of Teacher Lu and would like to teach at our Martial Arts Hall."

Murong You was greatly surprised: "You’re Little Lu’s friend? Little Lu, has your memory recovered?"

"...It has recovered a part."

Murong You, wise with age, instantly understood the situation. Little Lu’s memory had only partially recovered, and he couldn’t be overstimulated. He needed to recover gradually. To take care of Little Lu, his friend decided to stay at his Martial Arts Hall for a while.

"What is Teacher Xue’s Cultivation Level, and what does he want to teach?"

"Foundation...Foundation Establishment Early Stage. I have some understanding of Swordsmanship and can teach them that."

"We can’t do Swordsmanship." Master Murong shook his head, "Swords are expensive after all. Most students here come from families with not much money, they can’t even afford swords, so learning Swordsmanship would be useless to them."

Xue Shilou turned to Lu Yang for help, what else could he teach if not Swordsmanship? Was he supposed to teach the students how to maintain their composure in battle so that their clothes wouldn’t get stained with blood?

Lu Yang stepped forward to speak for Xue Shilou: "He can teach the Rake Skill, the Shovel Skill."

"Rake Skill, Shovel Skill? That could work." Murong You’s eyes brightened, not expecting Little Lu’s friend to be so versatile.

Rakes and shovels are farming tools but could also be used as weapons in critical moments. However, such weapons are quite rare, and very few in Muxue City know how to wield them.

Do I know the Rake Skill and Shovel Skill?

Xue Shilou pondered and indeed felt competent; as the saying goes, all paths lead to the same goal in the great Dao. Being devoted to the art of the sword, he had cultivated to the Spirit Transformation Stage, which allowed him to have an understanding of other weapons through analogy. Teaching a group of students would be more than manageable.

"As for the monthly salary, how about five Spirit Stones per month?"

"That’s fine." Xue Shilou didn’t care much about making money.

"Then you can live next to Little Lu."

Now officially a teacher, Xue Shilou could legitimately stay at the Martial Arts Hall and ask Lu Yang for guidance in Swordsmanship.

"Fellow Daoist Lu Yang, I have some doubts about Swordsmanship..."

Lu Yang gave Xue Shilou a look: "What Swordsmanship? Do you know the Rake Skill, the Shovel Skill? Go practice on your own first, otherwise, how will you teach the students tomorrow?"

"Oh."

On his first day at Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall, Xue Shilou learned how to use a hooked rake and a shovel.

Chapter 1166: 1165: Ice Flood Dragon Clan

In the dead of night, Xue Shilou closed his eyes and focused, treating the rake in his hand as if it were his sword. Suddenly opening his eyes, he swung it with the force of a gale, every move executed with precision and method.

Even the seasoned masters of rake skill in Muxue City, upon seeing Xue Shilou’s actions, would have to admit their respect, for Xue Shilou wielded it much stronger than they.

But Xue Shilou felt it still wasn’t enough.

Not fast enough, not fast enough, it needed to be faster, even faster!

The rake in his hand left behind trails of illusory shadows as he swung, and eventually, he was so tired he was huffing and puffing, propping himself up with the rake to barely stand, maintaining the poise of a sword cultivator.

Daoist Lu had him practice the rake skill, which must contain deeper meaning, certainly not as simple as merely teaching the students the rake skill.

Xue Shilou stared at the rake handle, now imprinted by his grip, letting his mind wander.

The rake, the rake... A flash of insight struck Xue Shilou, and he suddenly understood.

Of course, how had he not realized this point before - when swinging the rake, its nine teeth would attack the enemy simultaneously, which perfectly embodies the principle of "One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand" and "Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin".

If the rake could simultaneously encapsulate these two completely opposite sword skills, could it be that these two sword skills shared something in common?

Daoist Lu was definitely hinting at this!

With this realization, Xue Shilou’s thoughts flowed smoothly, and he was at Lu Yang’s door first thing in the morning to express his gratitude.

"Thank you, Daoist Lu, for your guidance. I’ve had an epiphany."

"..."

Last night, Lu Yang had spent a long evening accompanying Fairy Eternity, and now his mind was still in a haze, not, what epiphany did you have?

"Good, having an epiphany is what matters."

...

At Hunter Shi’s home.

Shi Yi had a very long dream, in which he saw himself falling into an ice hole and being swept away by a rapid undercurrent.

Logically speaking, falling into an undercurrent, he should have felt exceedingly cold, but instead, he felt abnormally warm, as if he was born to live there.

Shi Yi suddenly opened his eyes, only to find himself standing in the icy snow outside the house, bare-chested yet not feeling cold at all.

"Hmm? How did I end up here?"

Shi Yi felt an itch on his arm and scratched it subconsciously, only to realize something was amiss. Looking down, he saw a few ice crystal-like snake scales had grown on his arm!

"Dad, look at my arm!" Shi Yi quickly ran into the house to find his father, a renowned hunter who, despite being an ordinary human, had trapped and killed Demon Beasts of the Qi Refinement first and second layers, truly formidable.

Hunter Shi was splitting firewood when he saw the snake scales on Shi Yi’s arm; the axe dropped from his hands and thudded into the snow.

"Ah, so you’ve finally awakened?"

"Dad, what’s happening to me, you know, don’t you?" Seeing his father’s reaction, how could Shi Yi not realize that his father had long anticipated this day?

"Does this have something to do with my mother?"

Shi Yi had never met his mother, and though his father said she died in childbirth, Shi Yi always felt that his father’s tone was off, as if there was more to the story.

Hunter Shi beckoned Shi Yi inside to put on some clothes, and the father and son sat next to the table.

"I originally wanted to take this secret to my grave, but since you’ve inherited your mother’s bloodline, there’s no need to conceal it any longer." Hunter Shi took a puff of tobacco, reminiscing about the past twenty years.

"Twenty years ago, I was out hunting and came across a woman in the snow, beautiful as a fairy. Seeing that the fairy was gravely injured, I brought her back."

"When the fairy awoke, she was very hostile towards me, but gradually, as we interacted more, her attitude softened, and she no longer bore any hostility towards me."

"The fairy told me she was in the Void Refinement Stage, which made her realm very unstable, and she had been attacked while out on her practice, resulting in her severe injuries and coma."

"You must have guessed the fairy’s identity by now; she was your mother, Bing Wu."

"Later, we fell in love, and you were born, our family of three living a sweet life."

"Unfortunately, the happiness was short-lived. Your mother’s clan came for her, intending to take her away and kill both me and you."

"That was when I learned that your mother wasn’t from the Human race, but from the Ice Flood Dragon Clan."

"That legendary clan capable of creating ice with their breath, the Ice Flood Dragon Clan?" Shi Yi’s body trembled with the revelation.

"Yes, and your mother was the Holy Daughter of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan, the most likely to transform into a dragon in a thousand years."

"She married me and gave birth to you, which was a violation of our clan’s taboo, a sign of bad omen."

"It was your mother who, with death staring her in the face, forced the Ice Flood Dragon Clan to spare the lives of us both."

"The scales on your arm are the symbol of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan."

"I thought you would never awaken the Ice Flood Dragon Bloodline and would live a safe and uneventful life. That was also your mother’s wish, but alas, against all odds, you have ultimately awakened the Ice Flood Dragon Bloodline."

Shi Yi clenched his fists so tightly his nails dug into his flesh, whitening his knuckles, "Where is my mother?"

The hunter sighed, "Probably detained in the Ice Flood Dragon Clan’s forbidden grounds."

"Before she left, your mother instructed me to tell you to never entertain the thought of rescuing her. Even the high officials of Muxue City must yield three steps when they encounter the Ice Flood Dragon Clan. How can you possibly contend with them?"

Shi Yi fell silent, his heart bleeding, feeling his own weakness.

"Alright, it’s getting late. Go to class."

...

The Ice Flood Dragon Clan had an ice sculpture, a statue of an ice dragon, carved so lifelike that suddenly one of the dragon’s scales started to shimmer. The guardians of the sculpture immediately reported the situation to the Clan Leader.

The Ice Flood Dragon Clan Leader was attempting to persuade a stunning woman, "Bing Wu, just marry my son obediently. The union of the strongest bloodlines might mean that your child is born with Dragon’s Blood."

"Wishful thinking in broad daylight!" the beautiful woman flatly refused.

Just then, a subordinate came to report. The Ice Flood Dragon Clan Leader heard about the shimmering dragon scale on the sculpture and was caught by surprise, "Oh, has another clan member been born?"

He had not heard of anyone in the clan being pregnant.

He approached the sculpture and calculated with his fingers, murmuring an incantation with an annoyed expression, "Turns out it’s the bastard that Bing Wu left in the outside world."

"To think that bastard actually awakened our clan’s bloodline—ridiculous, it’s simply a desecration of our noble bloodline."

"Bing Wei, Bing Wu, where are you?"

"Present!" Two Flood Dragons appeared, kneeling before the Clan Leader.

"Go to Muxue City and kill that bastard named Shi Yi."

"Understood!"

...

Bing Wei and Bing Wu were the trusted confidantes of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan Leader, also brothers. With their Spirit Transformation Late Stage Cultivation Level, together they were unbeatable below the Body Integration Realm.

"He’s here."

Bing Wei and Bing Wu had found out the whereabouts of Shi Yi, who was in a Martial Arts Hall learning Fist Technique and Rake Skill.

"How pitiful, this is the extent of the vision of humans. They try every means to make themselves stronger, but in the end, it’s just about learning some family techniques at the Martial Arts Hall."

Bing Wei and Bing Wu, full of arrogance, sauntered into the Martial Arts Hall unconcerned about committing murder in public.

Rather, publicly executing Shi Yi would be the perfect demonstration of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan’s power.

They kicked open the doors of the Martial Arts Hall, scanning the group of students with contempt. The students, busy with their rakes laughing and practicing some unfathomable moves, included Shi Yi, sweating profusely, training with great effort.

All students turned to look at Bing Wei and Bing Wu.

Seems the teachers here are of this level...

"Xue, Xue..."

Why is the killing star Xue Shilou here?

Xue Shilou frowned as he sized up the two Ice Flood Dragons, displeased, "Who are you, and what are you doing here?"

The moment Bing Wei and Bing Wu saw Xue Shilou, they froze, taken aback. The Ice Flood Dragon Clan naturally fear no cold, but at that moment, they felt as if they had turned into mortals, drenched in a bucket of ice water from head to toe.

Smiles of flattery crept onto their faces as they gradually retreated, heading to fix the door they had kicked in, trying to restore it.

"We heard the Martial Arts Hall door was about to break down. Indeed, it’s old and falls apart upon touch. We’ll fix it right away."

Chapter 1167: 1166: This Door is Not Sturdy at All

Bing Wei and Bing Wu’s legs trembled at the sight of Xue Shilou, and they almost broke into tears.

Although they were both at the Spirit Transformation Late Stage like Xue Shilou, and together they could walk unhindered below the Body Integration Realm, Xue Shilou was a Sword Cultivator who could match those in the Body Integration Realm!

It was said that he could fight evenly with cultivators at the Early Integration Stage and even rumors persisted that he had clashed with the Demon King and, although neither had emerged victorious, he had remained uninjured!

If Xue Shilou struck with one sword, they would not die but would be gravely wounded!

Why was Xue Shilou here, and why was he holding a rake?

Bing Wei and Bing Wu took out nails and hammers from their Storage Rings and began fix the door, making clanking sounds.

As trusted followers of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan Leader, they were skilled at everything from murder and arson to mending doors and fixing shoes.

Under Xue Shilou’s supervision, they didn’t dare use regular wood and instead, heartachingly took out the Dragon Blood Wood and fashioned it into a new door.

This was Dragon Blood Wood, top-grade material for creating magic artifacts at the Spirit Transformation Level.

"Teacher Xue, please continue your lesson, we won’t disturb you anymore."

Bing Wei and Bing Wu bowed and shuffled out of the Martial Arts Hall with their heads lowered and backs bent.

After leaving the Martial Arts Hall and Muxue City, without regard for the astonished expressions of the passersby, they transformed into Flood Dragons and fled at full speed.

...

The Clan Leader of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan sat on an ice pillar, commanding and intimidating by his very presence. In the Ice Flood Dragon Clan, he was the supreme.

Hearing the report from these two good-for-nothings, the Clan Leader frowned with displeasure, "Are you suggesting that Xue Shilou was teaching that bastard child how to use a rake in the Martial Arts Hall?"

"It’s true, my lord!"

Bing Wei and Bing Wu lamented tearfully, continuously kowtowing and recounting the terrifying scene: "We had a feeling that if we lingered any longer, we would be minced by that executioner’s sword!"

The Clan Leader snorted coldly, "You two cowards, frightened out of your wits, can’t even discern fact from fiction. It must be that Xue Shilou has gone to Muxue City to observe the residual Sword Qi left by that peerless Sword Cultivator and incidentally used the Martial Arts Hall as a temporary base, merely teaching the child a few tricks in his spare time."

"Did you actually believe that mongrel was Xue Shilou’s disciple?"

Shi Yi was somehow related to the Ice Flood Dragon Clan; having someone other than his trusted followers handle exterminating Shi Yi would not look good.

"It will have to be me who takes action."

The Clan Leader was not one to procrastinate; he immediately set out for Muxue City.

As a majestic Integration Late Stage Cultivator, he feared no murderous stars like Xue Shilou.

He landed and transformed into human form, not even bothering to conceal his killing intent, which was palpable to anyone passing by, prompting them to steer clear.

He strode straight to the Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall in Muxue City and kicked the door to splinters.

"Xue, don’t think that just because my two subordinates..."

"Little Shi, you must follow your instincts when you punch, don’t be stiff." Teacher Lu pointed out the shortcomings in Shi Yi’s techniques.

Shi Yi studied diligently, doing everything Teacher Lu said, feeling that learning Tiger Roar Fist helped him awaken his Bloodline.

The students all turned their heads to look at the Ice Flood Dragon Clan Leader.

Lu Yang looked over as well.

The moment the Clan Leader got a clear look at Lu Yang, he felt as if he had fallen into an ice-cold abyss, his body stiffening like a marionette.

The Ice Flood Dragon Clan was a branch of the Dragon Clan. He had the fortune to attend the great birthday celebration of the East Sea Old Dragon King where he saw Lu Yang and the Dragon Clan Ancient Ancestor emerge from the Dragon Ball; the Dragon Clan Ancestor even thanked Lu Yang for awakening her.

He had also heard that Lu Yang was the next Sect Leader of the Seek Dao Sect, held in high regard by Celestial Master Guiyuan and Vast Sea Daoist Lord, who was a Dao-companion to the Ancient Ancestor of the Dragon Clan, Ao Ya.

Not to mention, Lu Yang was the number one Genius of his era, with an exceptionally powerful background.

Each of these was not someone he could afford to provoke.

"What are you doing?" Lu Yang frowned, his lesson having been interrupted halfway through.

The Clan Leader said furiously, "Don’t think my two subordinates are reliable in their tasks. Just look at this shoddy door they’ve repaired, it’s not at all sturdy, and it could be kicked down in one blow. This is truly an embarrassment to the extreme, thank goodness I came over to inspect!"

"Rest assured, I will definitely repair this door solidly!"

While speaking, the Ice Flood Dragon Clan Leader took out a hammer, nails, and the main trunk of the Ten-thousand-year-old Coiled Dragon Wood from his Storage Ring.

The Ten-thousand-year-old Coiled Dragon Wood is one of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan’s foundations. Any cultivator in the Body Integration Realm would covet it, and every time the Clan Leader used it, it pained him deeply.

Soon, the Martial Arts Hall had a new wooden door installed, and the Ice Flood Dragon Clan Leader left with a forced smile, retreating backward.

"I won’t disturb your class any longer, please continue," he said.

As soon as he left the Martial Arts Hall, he transformed into a Flood Dragon and flew back to the Ice Flood Dragon Clan, still shaken by his experience.

"Father, have you killed that bastard?" asked the pale-faced young son of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan Leader, his steps unsteady and his smile giving off a sinister vibe.

He had always coveted Bing Wu’s beauty, but alas, Bing Wu rejected him outright, even when his father personally tried to persuade her.

He probably had his son on her mind all along.

Now that his father had killed her son, cutting off her lingering thoughts, Bing Wu should turn to him.

Slap—

The Clan Leader slapped his son right across the face, "What bastard? Mind your language, that’s the Saint Heir our clan has been secretly cultivating!"

"Ah?" His son clutched his face, stunned.

The Clan Leader did not explain further but quickly went to the forbidden area to release Bing Wu, hoping that Lu Yang wouldn’t hold a grudge against him.

The biggest dream of their Ice Flood Dragon Clan was to give birth to a True Dragon and join the Dragon Palace. If they truly offended Lu Yang, a single word from him could prevent their Ice Flood Dragon Clan from ever joining the Dragon Palace.

"Bing Wu, do you see if we should bring your son and husband over, or would you rather go back?"

Bing Wu looked at the Clan Leader suspiciously, wondering what had happened. The change in his attitude toward her was so drastic it made her fear a conspiracy or trick.

"I’ll go back."

...

When Shi Yi returned home, he saw his father, who had already quit drinking, drown his sorrows with alcohol and pass out on the table.

He had buried the issues with his wife deep in his heart, but retelling the past to his son brought all those memories flooding back, and only alcohol could numb his pain.

Shi Yi sighed, while at the same time, an endless fighting spirit rose in his heart.

What about the Ice Flood Dragon Clan? Even if it cost him his life, he would rescue his mother!

He felt that practicing the Tiger Roar Fist could stimulate his Bloodline to awaken. Although he didn’t know why, it must become the key to rescuing his mother!

Suddenly, the door to their home opened, and the north wind howled, snow blowing backward into the house as a stunningly beautiful woman stood at the doorway, covering her tears with excitement.

The hunter Shi Yi’s vision blurred as if he saw the silhouette that had been absent for fifteen years.

"Bing’er."

...

Mr. Murong looked at the silk banner laid out on the table, deep in thought. His junior disciples claimed they knew nothing, so he summoned Teacher Lu and Teacher Xue.

Pointing to the banner, he asked, "The Shi Yiyi family sent this silk banner to our Martial Arts Hall, saying they’re grateful that we helped reunite their family. Do you know why?"

Both teachers looked blankly at Mr. Murong, shaking their heads in unison.

"Then, about the replacement of our hall’s sturdy door, was it you who arranged for someone to do this?"

Again, both teachers looked blankly at Mr. Murong, shaking their heads in unison.

"We didn’t arrange for anyone."

Mr. Murong hissed, wondering if there really were so many kindhearted people in the world.

Chapter 1168: 117 Why Does Someone Always Interrupt the Class

The morning sunlight scattered across the streets, while the city gate guards, having watched through the night, were changing shifts. Unnoticed by them, a golden Nascent Soul drifted over their heads and silently entered Muxue City.

The golden Nascent Soul appeared dim, nearly transparent, its childlike face twisted into a fierce grimace.

"Damn those from the Jiuyou Sect, so fickle and ruthless, killing at the slightest disagreement."

The mere memory of the earlier scene made the golden Nascent Soul shiver. A cultivator in the Divinity Transformation Stage had led twenty others in the Nascent Soul Stage and directly attacked the stronghold of the Nine Ghosts Alliance.

There’s a saying: you don’t strike a smiling face. The Alliance Hierarch had shown great courtesy to that envoy from the Jiuyou Sect, Huo Huashen, putting on a smiley face, but before the Alliance Hierarch could react, he was beheaded, dying with his eyes still open.

Fortunately, as the leader of the Ghost Killing Alliance, skilled in assassination and stealth techniques, he barely escaped the massacre at the cost of his lifespan.

Although he had managed to flee the scene, he was gravely wounded and beyond healing, leaving him no choice but to abandon his physical body, preserving only his Nascent Soul.

To evade the pursuit from the Jiuyou Sect, he had fled and his Nascent Soul’s power had nearly dissipated.

"I must find a target to possess!"

The Ghost Kill Alliance Leader clenched his teeth, reminiscing about his once elevated status and how today he was relegated to possessing others like a stray dog.

"This kid seems to have unusual spiritual power, and his physical fitness far exceeds his peers. You’re the one!"

The Ghost Kill Alliance Leader was overjoyed, not expecting to find such a prodigy and realizing that possessing him might even lead to further advancements, possibly reaching the Spirit Transformation and Void Refining stages one day!

At Su Yuan’s home.

"To meditate, you must calm your mind. How can you meditate with such restlessness?"

Su Yuan adopted the Five Centers Toward Heaven posture, the most orthodox meditation stance, aimed at cultivating the heart.

The only issue was the voice that kept expressing dissatisfaction with his meditative state.

...

The Ghost Kill Alliance Leader entered Su Yuan’s Spiritual Space, delighted inwardly, "I was right. This kid is naturally gifted with spiritual power. Even while a mortal, he has opened up a Spiritual Space. If he formally begins cultivation, his spiritual power could be ten times that of his peers!"

A spiritual attack tenfold stronger could instantly annihilate anyone of the same stage!

"But why is it so dark here? Could it be that this kid doesn’t know he has a Spiritual Space and has never been here?"

The Ghost Kill Alliance Leader was puzzled, as this seemed highly unlikely.

Just as he was bewildered, suddenly lamps on either side lit up, glowing orange-yellow. The dim lights were followed by numerous lamps illuminating consecutively, outlining a path.

The Ghost Kill Alliance Leader felt his hair stand on end. This was wrong; even with a natural talent for spiritual power, a mortal shouldn’t have such a vast Spiritual Space!

Clank—clank—

The sound of metal clashing rang out, a sound the Ghost Kill Alliance Leader was all too familiar with from his interrogation techniques—the sound of chains rattling.

Why would there be the sound of chains in a Spiritual Space?

At the end of the path lit by the orange-yellow lamps, a white figure appeared, constrained by dark chains on its limbs, the other ends of the chains stretching into unknown reaches.

The white figure slowly lifted its head, revealing a snow-white smile, "I thought Su Yuan would be the first visitor here."

The Ghost Kill Alliance Leader couldn’t believe his eyes, feeling a shiver run down his spine.

"Who, who are you!"

The grand voice echoed throughout the Spiritual Space, causing the Ghost Kill Alliance Leader’s Nascent Soul to turn even dimmer.

"Who am I? I too wish to know the answer. All I remember is that this body was too weak to bear my entire soul, and thus, I had no choice but to seal myself here."

The Ghost Kill Alliance Leader offered an apologetic smile and silently left the Spiritual Space, "With a senior here, I shall not intrude."

"Coming here, did you still think of leaving?"

The candle snuffed out, screams emanated from the Spiritual Space, quickly fading as if nothing had happened.

Struggling to enter a meditative state, Su Yuan shuddered, sensing unnoticed changes in his body.

"Kid, let me tell you some good news..." The voice sounded quite pleased.

Su Yuan suddenly slapped his forehead, "Damn it, it’s all because you kept teaching me meditation. Now it’s this late, if I don’t go to school soon, I’ll be late!"

"Good news..."

"Let’s talk after class, Mr. Lu said that one must not be distracted during class."

...

"Boss, the trail of the Ghost Kill Alliance Leader vanishes in a hut, empty of any people," a disciple at the Nascent Soul Stage from the Jiuyou Sect reported to Huo Huashen.

The Ghost Kill Alliance Leader had secret techniques to escape, and the Jiuyou Sect had secret techniques to track their enemies. They had been following them all the way to Muxue City, where the trail abruptly ended, as if the leader had evaporated from the Mortal World.

"A single hut?"

"Yes, the owner of that hut is an orphan named Su Yuan."

"Orphan? Su Yuan?"

Huo Huashen set down his teacup in contemplation, "Could it be that the Ghost Kill Alliance Leader possessed Su Yuan to escape?"

"Uh, it seems he didn’t escape. According to our investigation, Su Yuan went to a place called Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall early in the morning."

Huo Huashen sneered, "Maintaining habits from before possession to deceive others."

He picked up his teacup, drank the tea in one gulp, smashed the cup, and stood up to leave.

"The Ghost Kill Alliance Leader is a person personally assigned by the Master. We absolutely cannot let him escape again, follow me!"

"Yes!"

Twenty disciples at the Nascent Soul Stage shouted in unison, causing the entire room to shake.

Huo Huashen led his people directly to Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall. Allowing the Ghost Kill Alliance Leader to escape under his watch was a disgrace. If he let it happen again, how could he face the Vice Sect Hierarch and the Master?

"Boss, this is the place!" A subordinate pointed out the location to Huo Huashen.

Huo Huashen, seething with killing intent, pushed open the wooden door, and his followers poured in, forming a line to support their boss.

Such an imposing scene could not just kill the Ghost Kill Alliance Leader—it could scare him to death!

Huo Huashen’s murderous gaze swept across each student as he tried to identify Su Yuan. The students, having never seen such a spectacle, trembled in fear.

Until Huo Huashen saw Lu Yang teaching boxing, he too started to shudder.

Why was Lu Yang here?!

"What are you doing here, frightening the children?" Lu Yang frowned, annoyed that someone was disrupting his class.

And why was Huo Huashen, a member of the Jiuyou Sect, here?

Huo Huashen stepped back, hammered the wooden door twice trying to break it, "Look at your Martial Hall’s door... Damn it, Ten-thousand-year-old Coiled Dragon Wood!"

He hadn’t noticed when entering — why was the door of this shabby Martial Hall made from Ten-thousand-year-old Coiled Dragon Wood?

Even his boss, Vice Sect Hierarch Shi, didn’t have such fine material.

"Are you also here to change the door?"

In the Extreme Northern Lands where pouring water would turn to ice, huge beads of sweat the size of soybeans formed on Huo Huashen’s forehead as his mind raced.

"What I mean is, such a big Martial Hall and you don’t even have a doorman; we are here to apply for the position."

Chapter 1169: 1168 People from the Seek Dao Sect Have Arrived

"You’re all here to apply for the gatekeeper position?"

Old Master Murong looked at the group with suspicion, muttering to himself. He had mentioned something about hiring a gatekeeper, but he hadn’t posted any recruitment notices, so how did people come to apply?

Had one of his disciples leaked the information?

And wasn’t the number of applicants for the gatekeeper position a bit too high?

"With so many of you..."

"We’re here to compete for the job, whoever you prefer is fine!" Huo Huashen quickly said.

"Then it’s you," Old Master Murong pointed at Huo Huashen and said.

He could see through their cultivation levels at a glance. This one named Huo Huashen was at the third level of Qi Refinement, while the rest were only at the second level. It was clear he had to pick Huo Huashen.

"One Spirit Stone per month, is that alright?"

"Yes, yes."

Leaving Old Master Murong’s room, Huo Huashen calmed down and suddenly realized that Lu Yang didn’t know him at all; to Lu Yang, he was just an ordinary Divine Transformation Stage Cultivator, not a disciple of the Jiuyou Sect.

However, if he didn’t continue the act and aroused Lu Yang’s suspicion, he and his fellow brothers would be doomed.

Huo Huashen no longer worried about the Ghost Killing Alliance leader’s matter; with Lu Yang here, he had surely dealt with the Ghost Kill Alliance Leader already, and if not now, he could handle it later.

Lu Yang was currently in the Void Refinement Stage, his realm fluctuating continuously, likely in a low state at the moment, his voice not yet revealing their cultivation levels.

If he were to discover this, and asked how someone of the Divine Transformation Stage had come to be a gatekeeper, how would he respond?

If his response was poor, it could easily arouse suspicion and lead to the belief that he had ulterior motives.

Huo Huashen’s mind raced, and he quickly concocted an excuse.

When the time came, he would say he admired Young Hero Lu’s reputation and that with Young Hero Lu’s unstable Void Refinement realm, he might run into trouble, so he had come to shield Young Hero Lu from some of that trouble.

If Lu Yang never asked, he would take the initiative to explain before leaving, Huo Huashen decided.

"Boss, why don’t we take up the gatekeeper’s job for you?" The juniors were touched to see Huo Huashen bravely facing Lu Yang.

A boss of the Divine Transformation Stage, how could he be a gatekeeper? It was too shameful to speak of, and how would he stand firm in the Sect afterward?

"It’s not about you replacing me; you all will become gatekeepers," Huo Huashen said.

"Ah? All of us?"

"I’ll be the gatekeeper in the open, and you will be the gatekeepers in the shadows, secretly protecting the martial arts hall."

Huo Huashen stated his plan, so when Young Hero Lu discovered his true cultivation level, he could mention his juniors to him.

...

"Daoist Lu, this new gatekeeper seems quite extraordinary," Xue Shilou said suspiciously, looking at Huo Huashen standing at the entrance.

And the Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators who kept circling the martial arts hall.

"Perhaps they’ve recognized my identity and have come to volunteer as Protectors," Lu Yang said with a smile, "It’s fine, we don’t need to pay attention to them."

"I see," Xue Shilou sighed, understanding the gap in reputation; people were volunteering to be Daoist Lu’s Protectors.

If it were him reaching the Void Refinement Stage, he feared that his enemies would queue up at his door.

...

"So this is Muxue City, finally arrived."

A man leading a horse and wearing a broad hat entered Muxue City, stretched, glad to have reached his destination.

Passersby frequently turned to look at the man.

The man in the broad hat, dressed in bright red robes, was particularly eye-catching against the endless white snow of the Extreme Northern Lands; anyone passing by would turn for another look.

As the man walked into Muxue City and took off his hat,

the passersby who had been strolling lazily stopped in their tracks, looking at the man in disbelief.

"It’s Meng Jingzhou!"

"So many people recognize me?"

Meng Jingzhou couldn’t help being pleased. He hadn’t expected his reputation to have spread far and wide, even to the Extreme Northern Lands.

Not only did they know of his reputation, but they even knew what he looked like.

The Extreme Northern Lands were a good place for him, where the year-round chill could effectively suppress his restless hot blood.

Then, he saw the haphazardly posted missing person notices.

Meng Jingzhou, bounty: one billion Spirit Stones.

"Lu Yang, your granddad!"

He didn’t need to guess who was behind this trick; there was no one else but Lu Yang.

It was surely that bastard Lu Yang’s plan to reveal his identity in Muxue City and protect his own life by masquerading as him.

"Meng Shiyi is here!" someone shouted, and the people of Muxue City became excited.

"Where? Where?"

The windows snapped open, as countless people craned their necks, looking down the street in search of Meng Jingzhou.

Even if they couldn’t capture him, just to see what the young hero looked like would be good enough, and of course, if they could catch him and deliver him to the Seek Dao Sect for the reward, that would be even better.

Seeing this, Meng Jingzhou dared not stay any longer. He grabbed Old Ma and bolted.

His realm’s fluctuation was now at the Golden Core Stage, so evading mortals was not a problem, but now it wasn’t just mortals looking for him; cultivators were also rushing over in groups.

One billion Spirit Stones was a number that would make even those in the Body Integration Realm tempted.

"The one leading the horse is Meng Jingzhou!"

Meng Jingzhou hastily told Old Ma to leave the place quickly.

"The one holding the bamboo hat is Meng Jingzhou!"

Meng Jingzhou swiftly threw away the bamboo hat.
