"The one in red clothes is Meng Jingzhou!"

Meng Jingzhou quickly took off his red clothes.

"The male is Meng Jingzhou!"

Meng Jingzhou hastily...

"Hey, wait a minute, which bastard just shouted that!" Meng Jingzhou suddenly realized something was amiss and became furious.

Before long, he found Lu Yang, who was hiding among the crowd and posing as an ordinary citizen.

Before Meng Jingzhou could lose his temper, a large group of cultivators surrounded him. He made a snap decision, skillfully put on a mask to change his face, and dived into the crowd.

"Where’d he go?"

"How did he vanish?"

Meng Jingzhou blended into the crowd and pointed in a random direction: "He seemed to have gone that way!"

Upon hearing this, the crowd surged in that direction, and soon the street was emptied of almost everyone, leaving only Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

"Was it you, you bastard, who put up the missing person posters!" Meng Jingzhou asked through gritted teeth, having received a welcome worth one billion Spirit Stones upon just arriving in Muxue City, which was more than anyone could stand.

Lu Yang hooked his arm around Meng Jingzhou’s neck and began to explain with an apologetic smile, "See, I was far away in the Extreme Northern Lands and couldn’t return home, so I could only draw a few portraits of a good brother to remember the sect, you know."

Meng Jingzhou knocked Lu Yang on the shoulder, speaking without good humor, "Get lost."

"By the way, how did you end up here?"

Meng Jingzhou rolled his eyes, "It’s all because of you."

"Me?" Lu Yang was puzzled. What did this have to do with him?

"I was originally in my family’s home in Imperial City, having a comfortable life.

Then you came to the Extreme Northern Lands to cultivate, and Eldest Senior Sister sent a letter to our ancestors saying it wasn’t good for me to stay in the sect all the time, that it wasn’t conducive to the Void Refinement Stage cultivation, and told me to join you in the Extreme Northern Lands to cultivate myself."

"Hey, hey, what do you mean I came to the Extreme Northern Lands to cultivate? You can’t just say whatever you want. I came to the Extreme Northern Lands to experience the mortal world, not to cultivate through combat."

Chapter 1170: 169 Meng Jingzhou: Which Great Power could this Sect Leader of the Tiger Roar Sect be?

"Ever since I arrived in the Extreme Northern Lands, I’ve been hiding my Cultivation Level and identity, teaching Fist at a Martial Arts Hall, living in a precise routine from sunrise to sunset."

Meng Jingzhou felt a surge of joy hearing Old Lu say this, indicating it was safe here, "So, since you came to the Extreme Northern Lands, you haven’t engaged in any combat?"

"Ah, I can’t say that I haven’t fought at all; there was one fight."

"Four Great Demon Kings and countless Demon Beasts forming a beast tide."

"...Are you sure you came to the Extreme Northern Lands to retreat from worldly life?"

"That’s not important, let’s go, let me take you to our Martial Arts Hall. Encountering the Great Demon King and the beast tide was an accident; the Martial Arts Hall is where I truly retreat from worldly life."

"I heard you were at the Martial Arts Hall." Meng Jingzhou stated, citing Elder Brother Dai had said Old Lu had conveyed messages about dying, stating he was at the Martial Arts Hall in Muxue City of the Extreme Northern Lands.

"Our Martial Arts Hall is called Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall, a part of the Tiger Roar Sect."

Lu Yang led Meng Jingzhou to the Martial Arts Hall where the guard, Little Huo, was diligently guarding the gate, not letting outsiders enter.

"Old Meng, let me introduce you, this is our Martial Arts Hall’s guard, Little Huo."

Last night, Huo Huashen had arranged the defensive layout of Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall overnight, exerting considerable effort, now looking somewhat listless.

Hearing Lu Yang shout "Old Meng," he immediately perked up.

The only person Lu Yang would call Old Meng was none other than the eldest son of the Meng Family, Meng Jingzhou!

At this moment, Meng Jingzhou’s Realm showed fluctuations to the Body Integration Realm, seeing through Huo Huashen’s Cultivation Level with some astonishment.

A guard at the Divinity Transformation Stage?

Upon pushing the door, a Ten-thousand-year-old Coiled Dragon Wood?

"This is Snow Daoist from Xue Shilou."

"Snow Daoist, this is Meng Jingzhou, you should have heard of him."

Xue Shilou was slightly stunned, then gave a serious bow, "I have seen Fellow Daoist Meng."

Meng Jingzhou returned the bow, increasingly astonished, a teacher at the Peak of Divinity Transformation?

A small Martial Arts Hall hides dragons and crouching tigers, then who might the Sect Leader of Tiger Roar Sect, such a Great Power, be?

"This is Murong You, the Sect Leader of Tiger Roar Sect."

Meng Jingzhou stared at Murong You for a long time, stunned, at the Golden Core Stage?

"Is your Cultivation Level correct?"

Murong You oh-ed, stood up revealing a smile full of interest, pacing around Meng Jingzhou, sizing him up.

"Little Lu, your friend is quite remarkable, being able to tell that I’ve hidden my Cultivation Level."

Murong You stood still in front of them, his expression calm but with a hint of pride: "Indeed, although I’ve always told others that I’m at Great Perfection of the Foundation Establishment Stage, in reality, I had cultivated to the Golden Core Early Stage long ago."

"I didn’t expect that you could sense even this bit of Golden Core energy I leaked!"

Both Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou showed astonished expressions simultaneously, greatly pleasing Murong You.

"Little Meng, I see you’re also at the Foundation Establishment Early Stage. How about becoming a teacher here for a while?" Before Lu Yang brought it up, Murong You voluntarily mentioned it.

Murong You, sharp and experienced, could guess quite a lot without explicit explanations, presumably, Little Meng and Snow Daoist were here accompanying memory-impaired Little Lu to slowly recover his memories.

"Ah? Me, a teacher?"

Lu Yang directly responded for Meng Jingzhou: "Murong You has great insight; I was just about to bring it up. The students are improving quickly in their Fist, and it’s difficult for me to teach them all by myself, Old Meng helping me would relieve some of the pressure."

"My brother’s Fist technique is quite remarkable; ever since he started, he has never met his match."

"Very good. In that case, Instructor Meng, you will receive the same monthly payment as Instructor Xue, which is five spirit stones per month."

"...Okay."

After Lu Yang and another left, a glint of intelligence flashed in Murong You’s eyes.

"Little Lu said he had profound fortune, and it seems to be true. In less than two months of being here, the martial arts hall has gained three Foundation Establishment Stage Cultivators and a third-level Qi Refinement doorman, and even the doors have been replaced."

Meng Jingzhou considered, feeling that five spirit stones a month was quite good. Previously, he had earned money by selling blood, which was somewhat shameful to mention, but this time was different. He was earning money through his skills.

"Old Meng, I need to discuss something with you."

"What is it?"

"I have a student who performs well academically, but he suffers from a cold body. Could you give him a drop of your blood to help warm him up and dispel the cold?"

"..."

So the young master needs to sell his blood for a monthly allowance of five spirit stones?

While they were talking in the courtyard, a snowy-white figure leapt over the wall, shaking its head and its mane flickering.

"Elder Brother Ma!"

Old Ma was pure white, blending into the snowy landscape, and glanced at Lu Yang and the other with pride, but said nothing.

At this moment, loud voices came from outside the wall, followed by Huo Huashen rushing over.

"Two teachers, I heard a horse jumped into our martial arts hall?" Huo Huashen had heard it from the guards patrolling the perimeter and rushed over to check.

It was reported that the white horse was agile, and twenty Nascent Soul Stage cultivators couldn’t stop it from jumping the wall.

"It’s nothing; the horse came here with Old Meng and will be staying at the martial arts hall for a while."

Huo Huashen looked around the courtyard and volunteered, "I see our martial arts hall has no stable. Should I build one? Don’t worry, teachers, it’ll be very comfortable!"

"That would be troublesome."

"No trouble at all, it’s my duty."

After Lu Yang and another left, Huo Huashen whistled, and a group of people in black carrying bricks and wood climbed over the wall, shouting ’Boss’.

"You all know what to do?"

"Yes, build the best stable in the shortest time!"

"Right, remember, don’t get caught!"

"Yes!"

Old Ma scraped the frozen ground with its hooves, revealing the ice-crystal-covered wild grass, took a bite, and shook its tail.

The grass from the Extreme Northern Lands really did taste different.

When class began, Lu Yang introduced Master Meng, the boxing master. During the break, Meng Jingzhou pulled Lu Yang aside.

"Are you saying that this Gong Che person has a cold qi in his body and needs my blood to remove it?"

"Right, any problem?"

"Damn it, although the kid had a cold qi before, now he practices Tiger Roar Fist every day, and the cold qi is almost gone. In a few more days, it’ll be completely expelled!"

"It’s just removing it one day earlier."

"And what’s with these students of yours? One has a hint of flood dragon blood awakening in his human bloodline, another is outright a cultivator, and another has scary high spiritual power and always talks to himself."

"You don’t get it. Though we often say the ’general public,’ meaning that ordinary people are very ordinary, in reality, how can people be exactly the same? These students are just ordinary people with some quirks; don’t overthink it."

Meng Jingzhou was momentarily dazed, feeling there was some truth in Old Lu’s words: "Are these your insights from transcending the mundane world?"

"That’s just a line I made up on the spot to fool you."

Chapter 1171: 1170 What Kind of Door is This?!

Today was a rare day off, with no need to attend private school or practice fist techniques at the Martial Arts Hall. Gong Che and Shi Yi had arranged to go out and stroll through the streets.

"You just call me out to hang out, but what exactly are we going to do?"

Shi Yi rolled his eyes, feeling that Gong Che had been unusually excited lately. Not only was he active during lessons, but he couldn’t sit still during his days off either, waking him up early in the morning.

Ever since his mother returned from the Ice Flood Dragon Clan, his father and mother had been inseparable and saccharine, leaving him feeling somewhat superfluous.

Perhaps that was why, under the guise of "friends are hard to come by, you should go out and play," his parents had shooed him out of the house.

"Why not think about it on the way? After all, you have nothing else to do at home, right?"

"How about this, I’ll treat you to lunch today!" Gong Che said generously.

"What’s the good news that’s put you in such a good mood?" Shi Yi asked, looking at Gong Che with suspicion.

Gong Che had been waiting for Shi Yi to ask this question, and now it had finally come.

"I can finally live past sixteen."

"What do you mean by that?"

"I’m just telling you this—you better not tell anyone else."

"Go on."

Gong Che spoke seriously, "Actually, I’m an orphan who was picked up. I was born with a cold qi in my body, meaning I wouldn’t live past sixteen. I learned all of this by overhearing my parents talking when I went to the bathroom at night."

"They thought I didn’t know, but actually, I knew everything."

Shi Yi was stunned for a moment, not expecting Gong Che to have such a tragic past. "So what about you now..."

"To live past sixteen, I must get rid of the cold qi in my body. My parents tried all kinds of methods to no avail, except for Tiger Roar Fist, which had only a slight effect, but it couldn’t change my fate."

"Until recently, when Teacher Lu started teaching us Tiger Roar Fist, the cold qi in my body began to truly dissipate, and just five days ago, it completely vanished!"

"Does that mean you can keep on living now?" Shi Yi finally understood why Gong Che had been so happy lately. Knowing this, he too was happy for Gong Che.

Shi Yi felt that Gong Che confiding his secret was a sign of trust and that he too should share his own secret.

"Actually, my veins carry the bloodline of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan..."

Shi Yi revealed everything he knew.

He rolled up his sleeves to show the ice crystal-like Flood Dragon scales on his arm.

"No wonder I could never beat you no matter how much I practiced, so you’ve awakened your bloodline." Gong Che suddenly realized why he’d been surpassed. Initially, he was the number one in the fist technique class; then, after Teacher Lu arrived, Shi Yi surpassed him. After learning from Teacher Lu, he surpassed Shi Yi again, but then somehow, Shi Yi overtook him once more.

"But why did your mom tell you to thank Teacher Lu and even send a banner of appreciation?"

Shi Yi shook his head, "I don’t know, my mom didn’t explain."

"Hey boss, give us two baked sweet potatoes, he’s paying." As they passed by a baked sweet potato stall, Shi Yi spoke.

With baked sweet potatoes in hand, and unstoppable smiles on their faces, Shi Yi continued the previous conversation.

"Enough about me, what about you? Have you ever thought about finding your biological parents?"

Gong Che took a bite of his baked sweet potato and mumbled unclearly, "At first, when I found out I was adopted, I thought about looking for my biological parents. But then I realized it’s better to treasure what I have now than to chase parents without any leads."

"Perhaps when I meet my biological parents, I’ll ask them why they abandoned me in the snow and left me to fend for myself."

Suddenly, the two halted their steps as a youth who bore a seven-point resemblance to Gong Che appeared before them.

The youth’s gloomy eyes were filled with a thick murderous intent, and he flashed a sinister smile.

"Wu Ma, I didn’t expect you to still be alive."

Gong Che was taken aback. Wu Ma? Is that me?

The youth stepped closer, "The most failed experiment, abandoned by mom and dad for not being able to endure the Mystic Heavenly Cold Qi, not only survived until now but also dissipated the cold qi within?"

"I thought I had killed Zi Shu, Chou Niu, Mao Tu, and possessed the qualification to cultivate the Xuantian Scripture. But to think, you’re still alive!"

"The Xuantian Scripture is a supreme cultivation technique, with strict conditions for practice. Only those who endured the Mystic Heavenly Cold Qi in their youth are qualified to practice it, and it can only be cultivated when there is one eligible person."

"Now that both of us are qualified, I can’t cultivate it while you’re alive!"

"Remember my name before you die. I am Yin Hu!"

Yin Hu made a move as if to pounce, but Shi Yi quickly stepped forward and pinned the leaping Yin Hu to the ground.

Yin Hu made several attempts to rise, only to find that Shi Yi’s strength was astonishing, and he couldn’t get up at all!

Who was this person?!

"Your brother?" Shi Yi asked Gong Che, who looked utterly bewildered.

"I don’t know him, but listening to all his muttering just now, he doesn’t sound like a good person."

Yin Hu raised his head, staring fixedly at Gong Che. Rather than tiger eyes, they seemed more like viper slits. "Wu Ma, if you dare, let him go, and let’s see who the stronger successor really is!"

After some thought, Gong Che said, "Shi Yi, let him go."

"Okay."

Yin Hu shook free from his restraints and lunged ferociously at Gong Che with claws outstretched, like a tiger descending the mountain, fierce and bloodthirsty!

Roar—

A youthful tiger’s roar erupted, and with two punches, Gong Che left Yin Hu battered and unable to rise from the ground.

Yin Hu’s pupils shook with disbelief; how was the other person more like a tiger than him?

"Is that all?"

Gong Che was stunned; after all the bluster, this guy didn’t put up a fight at all.

However, Gong Che did not let down his guard. He remembered that Teacher Lu had mentioned in class that some opponents, seemingly easy to handle and taken down in one move, might actually be hiding terrifying power within their bodies and rise up to continue fighting.

Gong Che and Shi Yi warily watched Yin Hu lying on the ground, motionless.

Just when the two thought the matter was about to end, a man and a woman with an extraordinary demeanor approached them; one could tell at a glance they were cultivators.

Seeing the man and woman, Yin Hu mustered his last bit of strength to get up from the ground, clutching at the woman’s leg and begging pitifully, "Dad, Mom, please give me another chance, I’ll definitely defeat him!"

The woman looked at Yin Hu with disgust, kicked him away, and then immediately put on a tearful face, looking at Gong Che with pity.

"Child, Mom has finally found you!"

"Stay away!" Gong Che shouted, linking Yin Hu’s nonsense and the woman’s reaction, he roughly guessed the identity of the couple.

They were his own biological parents, and they had given birth to him merely to choose the strongest child to cultivate the inscrutable "Xuantian Scripture"!

"Child, what’s wrong with you, we are your biological parents," the woman called out.

"Why did you abandon me in the first place!"

"Child, you’ve become so strong exactly because Dad and Mom abandoned you, which allowed you to seize the opportunity and achieve today’s success, defeating your worthless brother."

"Perhaps what Dad and Mom did back then was ill-considered, but after all, we are your biological parents, as the saying goes, blood is thicker than water. Is there anything we can’t sit down and talk about?" The woman had a bewitching ability to speak that made one involuntarily believe her.

"Come, child, come to Mom," the woman opened her arms.

Gong Che didn’t even think about it and, grabbing Shi Yi, turned and ran.

"Run!"

"Trying to run?" The man flicked his sleeve, transforming it into an invisible giant hand that reached for Gong Che and Shi Yi.

Unexpectedly, Shi Yi’s body emitted the sound of a Flood Dragon, blocking the invisible hand.

"Pursue!"

The man and woman said through clenched teeth. Although they were Nascent Soul Stage powerhouses, here they were in Muxue City, where too much fanfare could draw the attention of the law enforcers. They could only chase after Gong Che and Shi Yi by navigating through the crowd with their exquisite steps.

"Where do we go? Should we go to my house? We might have a way there," Shi Yi suggested.

"No way, your house is too far, we won’t make it. Let’s go to the Martial Arts Hall!"

After several narrow escapes and nearly being caught, they finally made it to the Martial Arts Hall, taking advantage of their familiarity with the terrain and racing against the clock.

"We’ve made it!"

The two ran into the Martial Arts Hall in a frenzy and slammed the door shut with a clang.

"How naive, do you think you’re safe just because you ran into the Martial Arts Hall?"

The man and woman, seeing Gong Che and Shi Yi enter the Martial Arts Hall, laughed instead. This place was under the Sect’s control, and they could brazenly attack under the pretext of challenging the hall, unafraid of law enforcers.

They had investigated the Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall beforehand and found that its strongest member was only at the Early Golden Core Stage.

"Heavenly Demon Hand!"

"Fallen Heavenly Fire!"

The man and woman unleashed their respective secret techniques, striking towards the Martial Arts Hall doors.

Their eyes widened in disbelief, as if they had seen a ghost—it felt not like hitting a door, but a towering mountain!

Pfft—

Their attacks were all reflected back, shaking them to their cores and causing them to vomit blood without cease.

What kind of door was this?!

Wasn’t it said that the Sect Leader of the Tiger Roar Sect was only at the Early Golden Core Stage? Even selling every Golden Core cultivator in the house wouldn’t be enough to afford this door!

The Martial Arts Hall doors slowly opened, and three people emerged.

Gong Che and Shi Yi hid behind Huo Huashen, pointing at the man and woman lying on the ground.

"Uncle Huo, it’s them who wanted to take us away!"

Huo Huashen’s brow furrowed: "How dare they, messing with our Martial Arts Hall’s people, are they tired of living!"

The man and the woman couldn’t stop trembling; that pressure, was it, was it the Divinity Transformation Stage?!

How was this possible, wasn’t he just a gatekeeper of the Martial Arts Hall?

Faced with the Divinity Transformation Stage, the man and the woman dared not hold back any longer. They bit their tongues and used a secret technique to transform into a mist of blood to escape.

Huo Huashen snorted coldly and commanded twenty Nascent Soul Stage cultivators with his Spiritual Sense.

"I’ve already left a mark on them; go and capture them."

"Yes!"

Huo Huashen then turned to a smiling face, comforting the terrified Gong Che and Shi Yi: "It’s okay now, I’ve scared the bad guys away."

Following that, Huo Huashen escorted the two of them back to their respective homes and returned to sit on a small stool in front of the Martial Arts Hall door.

Soon, the twenty Nascent Soul Stage cultivators brought back two remnant souls, with one reporting to Huo Huashen.

"Boss, we’ve already soul-searched the people we captured, and it seems they are from the Ruthless Sect."

"The Ruthless Sect?"

Huo Huashen growled; when he had wiped out the Nine Ghosts Alliance before, he told Young Master Lu that the Nine Ghosts Alliance was a subordinate force of the Ruthless Sect.

How could it be that there really were forces from the Ruthless Sect in the Extreme Northern Lands?

Chapter 1172: 1171: Snow Emperor Bloodline

"Huo Huashen pondered, ’Since the blame has been shifted onto the Ruthless Sect, why not just eradicate the Ruthless Sect altogether to ensure there are no witnesses left?’ Who would have thought that there really were members of the Ruthless Sect in the Extreme Northern Lands?"

"How significant is the Ruthless Sect’s influence in the Extreme Northern Lands? Are there any high-ranking members present, and what is the highest Cultivation Level among the cultivators?"

Huo Huashen asked, as this was the crux of the issue. After several rounds of suppression by Great Xia, both Vice Sect Hierarchs of the Ruthless Sect had been captured, and almost ninety percent of the upper echelon had been apprehended.

So the Ruthless Sect in the Extreme Northern Lands was either a minor power or... the Sect Master of the Ruthless Sect was here!

The Nascent Soul younger brother shook his head: "It’s not clear whether any high-ranking members are present, but one thing is certain—we still have adherents in the Divinity Transformation Stage."

"Okay, I’ve got it," Huo Huashen said, having made up his mind to report to Boss Shi Huagu and let him decide how to proceed.

"There’s one more thing." The Nascent Soul brother produced a book of Scriptures.

"This is a Cultivation Technique called the ’Xuantian Scripture,’ scavenged from that couple’s residence. According to the couple, it’s a Cultivation Technique of the Unification Level."

The Xuantian Scripture had an ancient design and did not seem like a technique from the era of Great Xia.

"A Unification Level Cultivation Technique? Let me see." Huo Huashen examined the text word by word, making calculations with his fingers, and pondering over each sentence’s implied meaning—profound insights were contained in every single character.

"The requirements for Cultivation are harsh, and it’s a unique technique that can only be cultivated by one person. The design is truly ingenious."

"But how can one only have a slim chance of progressing to the Unification Level after Cultivation to the highest realm? The techniques I cultivate are stronger than this."

...

"The Ruthless Sect in the Extreme Northern Lands?" Mu Baiyi mused as he rubbed his chin upon hearing the news reported by Shi Huagu.

How could the Ruthless Sect be so reckless? I say they’re in the Extreme Northern Lands, and there they actually are?

If Young Master Lu runs into the Ruthless Sect and they claim to have no knowledge whatsoever of the Nine Ghosts Alliance, wouldn’t it be awkward when he and I meet again?

"Shall I personally make a trip to the Extreme Northern Lands?" Shi Huagu volunteered.

Mu Baiyi glanced at Shi Huagu. If you truly wished to venture into the Extreme Northern Lands alone, would you have bothered to report this intelligence to me?

It’s nothing more than the fear that the Ruthless Sect’s devotees aren’t the only ones in the Extreme Northern Lands, but also a Sect Master of unknown identity, and you want to drag me along as well.

Although I am a Pass Tribulation Cultivator, I am only in the Early Tribulation Stage after all. The Ruthless Sect has two Vice Sect Hierarchs, one in the Early Tribulation Stage and the other in the Middle Tribulation Stage, while the Sect Master is definitely in the Late Tribulation Stage; I would certainly be no match against a veteran in the Late Tribulation Stage.

With that thought, Mu Baiyi stood up and ordered Shi Huagu, "Contact the Yaoyang Sect and have them join us to the Extreme Northern Lands."

Chi Sect Leader of the Yaoyang Sect has also advanced to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, and the Yaoyang Sect originally had an old Pass Tribulation Mid-Stage cultivator.

...

On a rare day off, three teachers went on an outing outside of Muxue City, set up a barbecue grill, and Lu Yang took over the cooking.

"Here, try the roasted rabbit."

"This rabbit was caught by me personally after a significant effort during a hunting trip; it almost escaped."

Xue Shilou was holding an Ice Sword, which he had found accidentally formed from an icicle under the eaves of the Martial Arts Hall; it greatly resembled a real sword, and Xue Shilou cherished it since finding it.

Although he could conjure a real Ice Sword using magic skills, he preferred the naturally formed Ice Sword, a product of Mother Nature’s marvelous craftsmanship.

Leaving aside Xue Shilou, even Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, who didn’t practice Swordsmanship, liked it and wanted it—though Xue Shilou wouldn’t give it up.

"Then I’ll trouble Daoist Lu," Xue Shilou said as he placed his Ice Sword into the ice box before storing the box back into his Storage Ring and started tasting the roasted rabbit.

Meng Jingzhou no longer stood on ceremony with Lu Yang and started eating immediately, "This tastes really good, better than any roasted rabbit I’ve had before."

Lu Yang boasted with pride, "That’s because it’s a roasted rabbit from the Extreme Northern Lands. Where have you been eating before?"

"In the Extreme Northern Lands, where it’s cold all year, Demon Beasts have thick fat. When I roast them with my Three Flavored True Fire, the fat melts away. How can rabbits from other places compare?"

"That’s true too."

The three of them chatted and laughed around the fire, from Xue Shilou’s confusion in learning swordsmanship, to the Cultivation anecdotes Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou had experienced, to the local customs of the Extreme Northern Lands.

They didn’t feel cold; the fire was mainly for maintaining the atmosphere.

"Speaking of which, what exactly is this Snow Emperor Bloodline?" Lu Yang asked out of curiosity.

Lu Yang remembered that during the Sword Inquiry Ceremony, Xue Shilou had cured his split personality and had initially awakened the Snow Emperor Bloodline. It was fully awakened in the dungeons of the Laurel Fairy Palace.

Xue Shilou had a Senior Brother named Xuanyuan Tianhen, and judging by his reaction, the Snow Emperor Bloodline must be something extraordinary.

"What is the Snow Emperor Bloodline?" Meng Jingzhou also hadn’t heard of it.

Xue Shilou explained with a smile, "The Snow Emperor is a legend of our Extreme Northern Lands, or rather, an ancient belief. All people from the Extreme Northern Lands are descendants of the Snow Emperor. This legend is little known, so it’s normal for you two Daoist friends to be unaware."

Lu Yang remembered that Fairy Eternity had never mentioned the Snow Emperor. It seems that the Snow Emperor wasn’t as ancient as Fairies.

"In the Extreme Northern Lands, it’s said that awakening the Snow Emperor Bloodline means that you’ve been recognized by the Snow Emperor, and you’re the one with the strongest talent in the Extreme Northern Lands."

"Actually, that’s not accurate. That’s something I learned from my bloodline memories after I awakened the Snow Emperor Bloodline."

"To be precise, awakening the Snow Emperor Bloodline means you’re qualified to be the Snow Emperor’s heir."

"The Snow Emperor is not a specific individual, but a title. In fact, there have already been several generations of Snow Emperors."

"And if I want to become the next Snow Emperor, I have to pass three trials." As Xue Shilou spoke, he took out three thawed pears from a basin next to the barbecue grill.

"Try these, they’re a specialty of our Extreme Northern Lands. You probably haven’t tasted them before."

Lu Yang took a bite of the frozen pear, and the juiciness was so astonishing that it nearly dribbled out of his mouth.

As Lu Yang and another person munched on the frozen pears, they asked, "Which three trials do you have to pass?"

"The first trial is the test of one’s Dao Heart. Your Dao Heart remains unshattered despite facing setbacks, proving your resolve in seeking the Dao."

"I passed that test when I first arrived at the Martial Arts Hall."

"Ah?" Lu Yang didn’t quite understand.

"That’s when I had just arrived at the Martial Arts Hall, you, Daoist Lu Yang, told me you had broken through to the Void Refinement Stage and that your realm could fluctuate to the Transcendence Tribulation Stage. Ever since the Sword Inquiry Ceremony, I’ve been cultivating with you as my goal. When I heard this news, my Dao Heart was greatly shaken."

Lu Yang understood, "So passing the first trial means you didn’t lose heart, continuing to take me as your goal in cultivation?"

Xue Shilou waved his hand, "Ah no, what I realized is that it’s good to be the number one in swordsmanship talent in the Extreme Northern Lands. There’s no need to compete with you for the title of the world’s number one swordsmanship talent."

"..."

"What about your second trial?"

"The second trial requires me to become the unbeatable presence within my own realm in the Extreme Northern Lands."

"I see that Brother Xue’s swordsmanship cultivation has achieved results, allowing you to fight across levels. In the Extreme Northern Lands, aren’t you already invincible within your own realm?" Lu Yang continued to inquire.

Xue Shilou shook his head, "No, at the Spirit Transformation Stage there is a Demon Cultivator known as the Carefree Guest who has committed all kinds of evil acts. I have fought with him and unfortunately was defeated. Only by defeating him can I pass the second trial, and that is one of the reasons I came to Muxue City to contemplate swordsmanship."

Chapter 1173: 1172 Unlucky Thief

"Talking about his defeat to the Carefree Guest, Xue Shilou didn’t feel the slightest bit ashamed; on the contrary, he seemed somewhat excited.

"My Rake Skill comprehension is nearly complete, and once I’ve fully grasped the profound mysteries of the swordsmanship within the Rake Skill, the Carefree Guest will no longer be my match."

"Once I’ve thoroughly comprehended the mysteries within the Shovel Skill, I can become even stronger!"

Ever since Xue Shilou had grasped the swordsmanship contained within the Rake Skill, he felt his long-dormant heart of the Sword Dao stirring to life, his insight into swordsmanship growing day by day.

"All this is thanks to Daoist Lu’s guidance."

"Old Lu’s guidance?" Meng Jingzhou looked at Lu Yang with a suspicious gaze, feeling that something was off. What did the Rake Skill have to do with swordsmanship?

Lu Yang’s expression remained unchanged: "What are you looking at me for? You don’t understand matters of swordsmanship."

Then, Lu Yang quickly changed the topic: "Snow Daoist, continue, what is the third trial?"

"The third trial is to create a move unique to oneself, and it can’t be some trivial life skill meant to pad the numbers."

"According to the memories of my bloodline, this is to test my creativity; I can’t merely inherit from the past, I must surpass my predecessors."

"This third trial is quite difficult," said Lu Yang, counting the moves he knew... weren’t they all unique to himself?

Xue Shilou took it easy: "We’ll cross that bridge when we come to it; such things can’t be forced."

"If I can pass the three trials, I can not only become the Snow Emperor’s heir, but I can also have any wish granted by the current Snow Emperor."

"Now that is a good deal."

"Alright, we’ve about finished eating; it’s time to head back." Lu Yang stood up, patted the snow off his backside, and gathered his things to return to the Martial Arts Hall.

...

In the Extreme Northern Lands, the temperature difference between day and night is vast, with daytime so cold that people are reluctant to reach out their hands, and nights even colder.

As a result, Muxue City had no nightlife. When night fell, every household turned off their lights and went to sleep, leaving the whole city eerily quiet.

A roar erupted, and the entire city could hear Vice City Lord Ma’s furious shouting.

"Who is the scoundrel who dares to steal into my house? You’re courting death!"

A black figure jumped between rooftops, appearing and disappearing, as if he didn’t exist in this space, his movements extremely cunning.

Accompanied by Vice City Lord Ma’s lion-like roar, a bright Sword Qi slashed down, striking the continuously leaping figure.

How could Vice City Lord Ma not be furious; he had recently gained insight into the Sword Dao and had chosen to seclude himself from the outside world. Little did he expect someone to mistake him for being seriously injured and healing, to have the audacity to steal into his house—an outright provocation.

The thief clutched his right arm, a look of resentment on his face. He thought he could evade the sword and leave gracefully, but it turned out that Vice City Lord Ma was not only uninjured but had also improved his swordsmanship, resulting in the thief taking a hit.

"Ma, you wait for me! I’ll not only steal your things, but I’ll also take your daughter away!"

Vice City Lord Ma trembled with anger, ordering a citywide manhunt. In no time, Muxue City was as bright as daytime, lit up throughout.

The thief took an elixir to temporarily stop his wound from worsening. With Muxue City on full alert, an escape was currently out of the question. He needed to find a place to rest first; he would find a way to flee Muxue City later.

He jumped into a courtyard and discovered a beautiful stable where a snow-white steed was swishing its tail and munching grass.

"Is this a Dragon Horse?!"

The thief’s heart leapt with joy; to think that a Dragon Horse from the East Sea would appear in the Extreme Northern Lands, and by the looks of it, a Pure Blood Dragon Horse at that, invaluable.

"If I’m not mistaken, this is the Tiger Roar Sect?"

"It seems the Tiger Roar Sect has struck it rich; I wonder where they got this Dragon Horse from."

"Looks like the cultivation level of the Dragon Horse isn’t too high; otherwise, it would have transformed by now. It’s just perfect for me!"

The thief approached the Dragon Horse with a grin on his face, thinking that getting a Pure Blood Dragon Horse in Muxue City made getting stabbed by that sword worth it.

The Dragon Horse glanced at the thief and continued to eat grass.

With his hands forming a spell-sealing position, the thief whispered an unknown magic skill, trying to control the Dragon Horse with the Beast Taming Technique.

The Beast Taming Mark landed on the Dragon Horse, which had not resisted from the beginning to the end.

"Success."

However, just as the Beast Taming Mark touched the Dragon Horse, the thief’s face drastically changed; there was something strange about this Dragon Horse!

The thief suffered a backlash from the Beast Taming Technique, and his vitality plummeted. He staggered back two steps with a pale face and depleted Qi and Blood.

The previous sword wound that had stopped bleeding intensified, and under the dual burden, his right hand became completely useless!

"What’s that noise?"

Huo Huashen, upon hearing the faint commotion in the backyard, ran over anxiously.

The thief saw that there was no time to leave the Tiger Roar Sect and resorted to using a secret technique to conceal his aura and form.

This was his unique secret technique; whether using the naked eye or Spiritual Sense to search, one couldn’t see him.

"Is there no one?"

Huo Huashen was puzzled; as someone at the Divinity Transformation Stage, he shouldn’t be experiencing auditory hallucinations.

But even when he used his Spiritual Sense, he couldn’t detect anyone—there was only Old Ma grazing in the stables.

"Let’s test it." Huo Huashen flicked his sleeve, releasing a Blood Demon Saber and hacking out countless waves of Sword Qi.

The thief widened his eyes in disbelief; the other party was a Sword Cultivator at the Divinity Transformation Stage?!

If he had not been injured, he wouldn’t have cared about someone at the Divinity Transformation Stage, but with his serious injuries, setting aside the outcome of a fight, it would certainly draw Vice City Lord Ma’s attention.

The Sword Qi slashed into him, and he bit down on his clothes, making no sound, dare not let Huo Huashen discover him.

"So there really is no one."

Huo Huashen let Sword Qi flood the entire backyard and still couldn’t find anyone. He could only conclude that he heard things because he was too excited about being on guard duty.

After Huo Huashen left, the thief showed himself, kneeling on the ground with one hand propping up his body, gasping for breath, sweat continuously beading on his forehead, his consciousness blurry and confused.

This won’t do; he had to find a place to rest, to leave this place. There was something strange about this place; the Tiger Roar Sect was far from simple...

The thief approached a room in the courtyard, outside of which hung a small wooden sword.

As he approached the room, the small wooden sword emitted Sword Qi and immediately repelled him.

At the spot where he landed, there lay a rake infused day and night with endless Sword Qi by Xue Shilou.

The rake teeth were exceptionally sharp, and as the thief collided headfirst with them, several bloody holes were punctured into his head. He struggled on the ground for a moment and soon lay immovable.

Lu Yang scratched his head as he opened the door: "Eh, why did my small wooden sword get triggered?"

He was currently at the Void Refinement Stage, a dangerous time; should a stranger break into his room, it would be problematic, so he had hung the small wooden sword outside his door to repel any strangers.

Xue Shilou, who lived next door, also came out, even more baffled: "How come my second test was passed?"

Both of them looked simultaneously at the thief on the ground, whose blood kept flowing.

He had lost his right arm, was covered with saber wounds, had a sword injury on his hand, and there were several large holes in his head; it was unclear what he had gone through, but it seemed like he had experienced torturous treatment before death.

"How did he die?"

Chapter 1174: 1173: Killing Heart Ancient Realm

"How did he die?" Xue Shilou wondered. The Carefree Guest was renowned for his steps throughout the Extreme Northern Lands. His "Carefree Steps" were unpredictable and unparalleled in the world, making defeating him nearly impossible, let alone killing him.

Yet, how had the Carefree Guest died right at his doorstep when he hadn’t even grasped the supreme swordsmanship from the Rake Skill?

With the death of the Carefree Guest, the position of the foremost Divine Transformation Stage Cultivator in the Extreme Northern Lands had unexpectedly fallen to him.

The second round of the trial was mysteriously passed.

"I don’t know." Just then, Lu Yang was cultivating in his room and hadn’t noticed any disturbances outside.

Lu Yang turned and asked the Immortal Fairy in his Spiritual Space.

"Fairy, do you know how the Carefree Guest died?"

"He committed suicide," Immortal Fairy stated confidently.

Lu Yang looked at the Carefree Guest’s body, which was covered in wounds, and fell into deep thought. "Snow Daoist, lend a hand, let’s lay the Carefree Guest to rest."

"Ah, oh."

Lu Yang and Xue Shilou carried the Carefree Guest’s body, moving it under the tree near the door of Lu Yang’s room.

Fittingly, just beneath the tree were buried the remains of Luo Shi, a Foundation Establishment Stage killer from the Ghost Killing Alliance. Knowing that his body now had for company the formerly strongest Divine Transformation Stage Carefree Guest would probably make him die happy.

"Old Lu, is something happening outside..."

Meng Jingzhou walked out of his room, watching Lu Yang and Xue Shilou carrying an unrecognizable body, one in front and the other behind.

"I didn’t see anything, I’m going back."

"Go back my ass, come help."

"Ugh."

Meng Jingzhou reluctantly picked up the right arm of the Carefree Guest and placed it back onto his body.

Lu Yang pulled out three shovels, and each of them began to dig a hole.

Meng Jingzhou and Xue Shilou, unaccustomed to such rough work, moved awkwardly. In comparison, Lu Yang worked with practiced ease.

Xue Shilou thoughtfully pondered, could this be the real usage of the shovel? Had he been misunderstanding it all along?

Now that he had passed the second round of the trial, it was time to focus completely on creating his own unique moves.

"I see why the tree at your door grows so well; it’s well-nourished," Meng Jingzhou commented as he dug. The method was unique and not easily imitated.

Upon hearing the report from his subordinate, Vice City Lord Ma’s expression turned cold enough to drip water, frightening his subordinate not to even dare breathe.

"The only Divine Transformation Stage Cultivator who could escape me must be the Carefree Guest!"

"Still no clue to that bandit’s whereabouts, damn it, he got away!"

The Carefree Guest’s final words of threat lingered in his mind. Vice City Lord Ma couldn’t always be on guard against the Carefree Guest; raging, he hit the table, "Find him! Dig three feet into the ground if you have to!"

Muxue City was locked down for ten days, the whole city hunting for the Carefree Guest, but found nothing.

Clearly, the soldiers of Muxue City were not strictly executing Vice City Lord Ma’s order to dig three feet into the ground.

...

Soul Cliff.

A rope was tied around a boulder, dangling down to the bottom of Soul Cliff. Su Yuan held the rope tensely, waiting for any signals from the other end.

Suddenly, the rope shook; Su Yuan snapped to attention and quickly pulled up the rope.

Feeling the vibrations along the rope, Su Yuan sensed that the person on the other end was climbing up rapidly.

A figure flashed by, somersaulting several times in the air before landing steadily.

It was Fang Zhige!

"Three days, and you finally came up. If you hadn’t, I would have had to call on Old Lu for help," Zhige said. "How did it go, any gains?"

The usually solemn Fang Zhige unexpectedly showed a cheerful smile, "I passed!"

"That’s great!"

Hearing Fang Zhige’s response, Su Yuan involuntarily thought of the events that had occurred not long ago.

The voice inside him had informed him that by devouring the soul of the Ghost Kill Alliance Leader, he learned of an Ancient Secret Realm known as the Killing Heart Ancient Realm.

The Killing Heart Ancient Realm was an opportunity that allowed the Ghost Kill Alliance Leader to rise early in his career. He passed the first trial in the realm and acquired the incomplete "Killing Heart Scripture," which transformed him into a feared assassin.

He had fled to Muxue City, also hoping to rise again by overcoming subsequent trials in the realm.

The Killing Heart Ancient Realm was located in the deepest part of Soul Cliff.

However, the only way to enter the Killing Heart Ancient Realm was to harbor a killing intent. The voice had tried several times to tempt Su Yuan into killing, but Su Yuan had firmly refused.

Having no other choice, the voice then had Su Yuan contact Fang Zhige, who possessed the necessary killing intent to qualify for entry into the Killing Heart Ancient Realm, agreeing to split any gains fifty-fifty.

After lengthy discussions and thorough preparations with Su Yuan, Fang Zhige finally decided to venture into Soul Cliff.

The voice recognized that Fang Zhige was a cultivator at the Great Perfection of Qi Refinement. If Fang Zhige betrayed them after gaining some benefits, he could control Su Yuan’s body under the guise of saving him.

Fang Zhige had no intention of betraying, however. As per their agreement, he detailed the gains from the Killing Heart Ancient Realm.

"The Killing Heart Ancient Realm seems to be a relic left by an Ancient Killer of great power, who had no descendants or disciples, hence setting up the secret realm, hoping to pass down her legacy."

"The realm comprises three trials. I was fairly lucky to have passed all three."

Though Fang Zhige spoke lightly, Su Yuan could see the fear in his eyes when he mentioned this, indicating the trials were not easy to pass.

"This is the ’Killing Heart Scripture’ that only I can cultivate. I’m keeping this for myself," Fang Zhige said, pulling out an ancient scripture, also the Cultivation Technique practiced by that Ancient Killer.

"Alright."

"Since the Killing Heart Scripture is mine, here’s sixty percent of the Spirit Stones and the other techniques for you."

"I actually obtained one more thing from the Ancient Realm, something I can’t understand, and it wasn’t mentioned in the transmission of memories."

"What is it?"

Fang Zhige unwrapped the package on his back, revealing a three-foot-long ice box.

Only then did Su Yuan notice that Fang Zhige had a package on his back; he hadn’t carried one when he went down.

"However, from the transmitted memories I received, it seems whatever is inside the ice box was very important to the owner of the Ancient Realm."

"Do you know what this is?" Su Yuan asked the voice inside him.

The voice, unusually silent, didn’t know what was inside and was embarrassed to say so.

"Maybe Old Lu has seen many things; how about we ask him when we get back?"

"Let’s go. There’s a parent-teacher meeting soon; we shouldn’t be late," Fang Zhige said, also trusting Old Lu deeply.

...

Facing the combined forces of the Jiuyou Sect and the Yaoyang Sect, the Ruthless Sect’s disciples were either captured or directly subjected to Soul Searches, with extremely efficient operations.

The three Transcendance Tribulation Stage great powers from both sects sat together, sharing the intelligence gathered.

The Chi Sect Leader of the Yaoyang Sect, grimacing, uncertain whether to cry or laugh, said, "The situation is more tricky than we imagined. I didn’t expect that the Ruthless Sect Leader is really in the Extreme Northern Lands, and it seems he is searching for something."

"Just don’t know where he is now; otherwise, we could take the initiative to strike and perhaps resolve this in one fell swoop."

Mu Baiyi took out a piece of freshly obtained intelligence, showing it to the Yaoyang Sect, "I found out that there’s a famous Divine Transformation Stage individual known as the Carefree Guest in the Extreme Northern Lands, a disciple of the Ruthless Sect Leader."

"And the last place the Carefree Guest was seen was in Muxue City."

Chapter 1175: 1174: The Thing in the Ice Box

"Huo Huashen from our sect leads more than twenty experts at the Nascent Soul Stage who are stationed in Muxue City. He might know the whereabouts of the Carefree Guest," said Mu Baiyi.

...

As the year drew to a close, the Martial Arts Hall announced a holiday break. Before the holiday, Old Lu and two other teachers decided to hold a parent-teacher meeting.

Firstly, it was to summarize the teaching achievements since they had become teachers.

Secondly, it was to have the parents encourage the students to not neglect their Fist skills during the holiday and to remember to practice.

Mr. Murong gladly agreed with the teachers’ ideas and even volunteered to decorate the Martial Arts Hall. The inside and outside of the hall were festively adorned with lights and decorations, creating a very festive atmosphere.

"Parents of Liu Yu, please come in, please come in."

The three teachers stood at the door, warmly welcoming the parents.

"This must be Old Lu. Our little Yu often mentions you, saying how much he likes your Fist teaching style."

"Zhige rarely praises anyone, but when he talks about you, he has nothing but compliments."

"Old Lu, Teacher Meng, Teacher Xue, please take extra care with these two children."

"Certainly, certainly, by the way, where is Zhige?" Lu Yang inquired. Zhige should be living at Liu Yu’s place, so why didn’t he come along?

Liu’s father sighed, "Zhige went out with Su Yuan. He said he would make it back in time for the parent meeting. The kids are grown now, and we can’t control them anymore."

Zhige and Su Yuan?

A flicker of doubt passed through Lu Yang’s mind. The two of them together probably meant it wasn’t as simple as just hanging out.

"Gong Che’s parents, please come inside."

Gong’s father enthusiastically shook Lu Yang’s hand.

"Old Lu, thank you so much. Little Che’s illness has finally healed. We are really grateful to you..."

Mid-sentence, Gong Che’s parents burst into tears, surprised that Gong Che’s coldness had truly vanished without a trace and that he had gained such a robust body.

"It’s nothing, just what I should do," Lu Yang tried to pull his hand free. He wasn’t very good at dealing with overly enthusiastic people.

Gong Che’s parents then turned to thank the gatekeeper, Mr. Huo.

"Just before the meeting, Little Che told us that he had encountered human traffickers, and it was you who scared the traffickers away. Thank you, thank you."

Huo Huashen remained solemn and stood up straight, "These are what I should do!"

Lu Yang continued to greet the parents: "Shi Yi... you have a large family, don’t you?"

Lu Yang saw that behind Shi Yi followed a large group of relatives, including Shi Yi’s parents, Shi Liehu, and Bing Wu, along with people from the Ice Flood Dragon Clan.

It was a massive procession, like sweeping through the streets.

The leader of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan came forward, grabbing Lu Yang’s hand, and burst into tears, "Old Lu, thank you for nurturing Shi Yi."

"I am Shi Yi’s god-grandfather. To be honest, we had high hopes for this child but didn’t know how to nurture him properly. Thanks to Old Lu for your expert guidance."

"I believe Shi Yi will definitely live up to Old Lu’s efforts and will quickly grow with the resources our clan provides!"

The Clan Leader of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan wanted to say more but was pushed aside by Bing Wu, who was in line to express her gratitude.

"Old Lu, thank you for helping our family reunite. You act low-key, Old Lu, and do not want others to know about your presence. Rest assured, I have not told anyone about your identity."

Shi Yi’s large family entered the Martial Arts Hall, and other children and parents gradually arrived, thanking the three teachers for their nurturing. Their children were now strong enough to kill a cow with one punch.

Inside the Martial Arts Hall, parents exchanged words, endlessly praising the three teachers while Mr. Murong, standing aside, couldn’t stop smiling.

"The parent-teacher meeting is about to start. Why haven’t Zhige and Su Yuan arrived yet?" Lu Yang muttered, wondering if the two boys might have run into trouble.

Just then, he saw Zhige and Su Yuan sprinting towards them.

To be precise, Zhige was carrying something unknown on his back and dragging Su Yuan along at an alarming speed.

Fearing that he would miss the parent-teacher meeting, Zhige didn’t bother to hide his true Cultivation Level.

"Hurry up and get in, we’re just waiting on you."

Lu Yang followed the two into the Martial Arts Hall, softly closing the big door, leaving Huo Huashen outside.

Lu Yang smiled warmly at the students and parents, cleared his throat, and spoke in a very gentle tone:

"Hello, everyone! First of all, please allow me to represent all the teachers of our class and express our sincerest gratitude to the parents who took time out of their busy schedules to attend today’s parent-teacher meeting."

"I have the privilege to teach this class by coincidence and by fate, and I believe Teacher Xue and Teacher Meng share the same sentiment."

"In the past period, students have made significant progress in all aspects. This could not have been achieved without each student’s hard work and the support and cooperation of all the parents."

"...With the New Year approaching, I hope students will not just indulge in eating, drinking, and playing, neglecting their martial arts."

"Then, see you all next year."

Lu Yang had the manner of a teacher and spoke methodically, bringing the meeting to a close amidst enthusiastic applause, with some students even being moved to tears.

Once the parent-teacher meeting disbanded, the three teachers gathered together. Meng Jingzhou laughed and punched Lu Yang lightly.

"That sounded quite convincing."

"You’re not wrong, staying in the Extreme Northern Lands is quite comfortable; it’s a great place for renouncing worldly desires."

Meng Jingzhou felt he had managed to calm Old Lu.

You see, before I arrived, look at what kind of life Old Lu was living, fighting the Four Great Demon Kings and battling the beast tide.

Since I arrived in the Extreme Northern Lands almost six months ago, there were no occasions for Old Lu to take action. He would teach during the day and retire peacefully at night.

Once the parent-teacher meeting ended and they officially broke for the holidays, both students and teachers had a holiday of two whole months.

"What do you think, how about we find a place to have some fun after the holiday?"

"That sounds good," Lu Yang and Xue Shilou had no objections.

While the three were speaking, Fang Zhige and Su Yuan came to Lu Yang, holding an ice box.

"Teacher Lu, can you open this ice box?"

Lu Yang was startled. The ice box was carved quite anciently, obviously no ordinary object.

"Where did you find this?"

Su Yuan, with his head lowered, dared not face Teacher Lu. "We picked it up."

"...Tell the truth to your teacher."

"We picked it up from beneath the Killing Heart Secret Realm under Soul Cliff."

Lu Yang tapped them both: "Soul Cliff is not a place you should be going to. What would you have done if something had happened? Even I couldn’t have saved you in time!"

Su Yuan muttered softly, "That voice said it was safe..."

"Listen to it or listen to me?"

"We’ll listen to you, Teacher."

Lu Yang’s expression relaxed slightly as he carefully examined the ice box. His cultivation had reached the Divinity Transformation Stage, yet he couldn’t see through the ice box.

Meng Jingzhou and Xue Shilou shook their heads; they had never seen such an ice box.

"Fairy, you..."

"This looks like Leng Ningxiang’s ice box."

"Who is Leng Ningxiang?"

"Oh, she’s an assassin, always cold-faced and never smiles. She’s quite attractive, ranking at the bottom of the top ten beauties of Ancient Times. Her cultivation technique is the ’Killing Heart Scripture,’ which emphasizes harboring a murderous intent while maintaining a heart as calm as an ancient well. However, she never fully mastered her technique."

Being at the bottom of the top ten beauties of Ancient Times would still mean she was extraordinarily beautiful.

"Why hasn’t her technique been mastered? Is it because she lacks talent?"

"Not really. It’s because the Killing Heart Scripture requires detachment from personal feelings and compassion. However, during one assassination mission, she fell for Yang Kongdong. Although she always denied liking Yang Kongdong, the progress of her cultivation didn’t lie—she liked him."

"Yang Kongdong is the one with the Harem Golden Core?"

"That’s right."

The Immortal Fairy used her Immortal Sense to penetrate the ice box’s layered seals: "Let’s see what’s inside this box... Is that an arm?"

Chapter 1176: 1175: My Things Were Originally Taken by Your Two Schools

"A severed arm?" Lu Yang’s expression gradually became more vivid.

If I remember correctly, Fairy Eternity once mentioned that Yang Kongdong had numerous concubines. In the end, a fire in the harem led to him being dismembered by them.

If Leng Ningxiang was one of the concubines, wouldn’t stealing an arm seem not too difficult?

"This ice box is somewhat peculiar. It’s not safe in your hands. How about I temporarily keep it for safekeeping?" Lu Yang said gently.

The voice inside Su Yuan’s body sneered, already suspecting that this man surnamed Lu was no good—he must have realized it was a treasure and was merely fabricating excuses to claim it.

Su Yuan was really naive. It was obvious the ice box was extraordinary, and he should have hidden it, yet he just brought it out, practically waiting for it to be stolen.

Su Yuan and Fang Zhige didn’t think much about it and simply agreed.

"Then goodbye to Teacher Lu, Teacher Xue, and Teacher Meng. See you next year."

The pair bid farewell, and following the students and parents, they left the martial arts hall, which was now only occupied by the three teachers and Elder Murong.

"What a handsome ice box." Although Xue Shilou didn’t know what was inside the ice box, that didn’t stop him from finding it impressive.

The gorgeous patterns on the ice box seemed to tell an ancient legend, both simple and majestic.

Xue Shilou then took out his own ice box for comparison. His also had intricate patterns, but compared to the one brought by Fang Zhige, it was somewhat lacking.

...

In a secret room, a pair of eyes slowly opened, flashing an unusual light.

"I didn’t expect that before I could find the arm, it would appear on its own in Muxue City?"

"Is this what they call fate?"

A tall figure stood up, revealing a thought-provoking smile.

...

After the parent-teacher meeting, Huo Huashen, who had been tense, yawned and relaxed.

Large snowflakes fell on his head, which he brushed away.

He looked up at the gray sky, "It’s snowing again."

No, no, can’t doze off. Standing outside, he represented the face of the martial arts hall; he needed to maintain his essence, qi, and spirit.

Thinking this, Huo Huashen perked up and stood at attention.

"Little Huo."

Hearing someone calling him, Huo Huashen instantly shivered; the voice sounded a bit familiar.

Then, he saw three people approaching at the street corner.

It was his own Sect Leader, Chi Sect Leader of the Yaoyang Sect, and Grand Elder Liu Zhen.

Why had three esteemed transcendence tribulation figures from two sects all come here?

"Greetings, Sect Leader!" Excited, Huo Huashen was about to kneel but was gently supported by a mana force from Mu Baiyi.

"That’s enough, there are too many prying eyes here; no need for such formalities."

"Yes!"

Twenty Nascent Soul Stage juniors also noticed the commotion and hurried over, not just to let Sect Leader Mu recognize them, but getting familiarized was also beneficial.

Having the Sect Leader personally inspect their work, following their elder brother to the Extreme Northern Lands really had its perks.

Mu Baiyi waved his hand to make the Nascent Soul disciples step back.

"It seems you have been doing well here. I heard that it was you who found the lead on the Ruthless Sect."

"Sect Leader flatters me; it was merely a coincidence."

"Even coincidence is part of fortune. Since you have been in Muxue City this whole time, do you perhaps know the whereabouts of the Carefree Guest?"

"The Carefree Guest?"

Huo Huashen was somewhat puzzled and didn’t know why the Sect Leader would suddenly bring up the Carefree Guest, although the Carefree Guest was the strongest among those in the Divinity Transformation Stage in the Extreme Northern Lands, it hardly seemed worth the personal inquiry of three people from the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

He bowed his head and told the truth.

"Sect Leader, a few days ago, the Carefree Guest indeed appeared in the Extreme Northern Lands and even angered Vice City Lord Ma of Muxue City. However, the Carefree Guest truly has skills. Vice City Lord Ma locked down the city for ten days and dug three feet into the ground, but still couldn’t find the whereabouts of the Carefree Guest."

"The Carefree Guest had once declared that he would seek revenge on Vice City Lord Ma, but so much time has passed and the Carefree Guest hasn’t made a move for revenge. It seems likely that he simply spouted momentary bravado and did not dare to provoke Vice City Lord Ma any further, having already left Muxue City."

"Has he already left?" Mu Baiyi pondered.

This was troublesome. Now, they had this single clue about the Ruthless Sect Leader, and it was cut off.

The two sects had already offended the Ruthless Sect, so they must eradicate it completely, offending them to the death.

They couldn’t rest until the Ruthless Sect Leader was dealt with.

The Jiuyou Sect’s Grand Elder Liu Zhen snorted coldly, "Humph, all for naught, a wasted trip."

Suddenly, Mu Baiyi shuddered, feeling someone release a murderous intent, and looked sharply in that direction before shouting loudly.

"Who is it!"

Through the falling heavy snow, a slender figure approached, a smile of unclear intent hanging on his lips—uncertain whether it was a sneer or a scoff.

"Unexpected, really unexpected, my things ended up in the hands of you two sects."

A chill glinted in the eyes of the slender figure.

His arm was part of him, and as long as the arm came within his sense range, even with it sealed in the treasure box, he could still vaguely sense its location.

Following his perception all the way here, he sensed that his arm was right inside this Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall.

And both Sect Leader Mu, Chi Sect Leader, and Grand Elder Liu Zhen were all here.

How could there be such a coincidence in the world!

Surely, these two sects found his arm and used it as bait to lure him in.

Laughable to the extreme, truly ignorant of the heavens and the earth, to actually dare plot against him.

"City Lord?" Huo Huashen was astonished the moment he saw the slender figure.

The visitor was actually the City Lord of Muxue City!

"City Lord? No, he’s just taken on the appearance of the City Lord of Muxue City."

Mu Baiyi and the other two Transcendance Tribulation Stage individuals walked slowly towards the slender figure, expressions solemn.

"The City Lord of Muxue City is said to be a powerhouse of the Extreme Northern Lands, but he’s only at Integration Peak, and it’s impossible for him to approach us undetected, am I right, Sect Leader Yang?"

"Sect Leader Yang? The Ruthless Sect’s Sect Leader Yang?" Huo Huashen was shocked, so the City Lord of Muxue City was the Ruthless Sect’s Sect Leader Yang?!

"Mu Baiyi, interesting. Among the three sect leaders, I admire Eternity Sect the most, followed by your Jiuyou Sect. After Qin Haoran died, Jiuyou Sect didn’t fall apart under your management but thrived instead."

Sect Leader Yang chuckled lightly, his tone casual as if conversing with a long-lost friend.

Then his voice cooled, the air filling with murderous intent.

"But that’s no reason for you to scheme against me."

"Really thought that because Great Xia put all four sects side by side, you have the status to stand equal with me."

"Arrogant!" Liu Zhen couldn’t hold himself back, taking the initiative to attack first. Of the three Transcendance Tribulation Stage members from both sects, only he was a veteran at Middle Tribulation Stage.

"Great Sun Crossing the Sky!"

A burning sun ignited atop Liu Zhen’s head, the temperature sudden spiked, instantly melting the heavy falling snow.

"Wait, don’t attack, take it to the sky. Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou are still inside!" Mu Baiyi didn’t expect Liu Zhen to be so impatient to fight.

Both Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou were inside the martial arts hall, and if either of them were harmed, they would have to face the relentless pursuit of the Seek Dao Sect and the Meng Family!

However, it was too late for Mu Baiyi’s words as the Great Sun was already taking shape.

"Plucking Plum Immortal Palm."

Sect Leader Yang’s figure blurred, appearing in front of Liu Zhen in an instant and striking his abdomen with a palm, dispersing the Great Sun.

Chapter 1177: 1176: Demonic Sect Civil War

"Tell me where the item was found, and I might spare your lives," Sect Leader Yang said slowly. He had gone through great pains to obtain information that someone near Muxue City had gained an inheritance suspected to be Leng Ningxiang’s, and his own left arm was definitely among Leng Ningxiang’s buried items.

As long as he knew where Leng Ningxiang was buried, he would be able to find his own left arm.

But he hadn’t found it.

The Jiuyou Sect and Yaoyang Sect had only arrived in the Extreme Northern Lands for a short time when they found his left arm. They must know where Leng Ningxiang’s inheritance was.

"What item?" Mu Baiyi and Chi Sect Leader, about to make their move, were confused.

"You’re acting quite convincingly. It seems neither of you wants to live!"

Sect Leader Yang’s face turned cold, truly a case of not shedding tears until one sees the coffin.

Mu Baiyi thought the Yangs were simply madmen. Of course, everyone in the Ruthless Sect was a madman, and Sect Leader Yang, as the head of the Ruthless Sect, spouting madness was nothing out of the ordinary.

He suppressed the urge to curse loudly and struck alongside Chi Sect Leader.

"Let’s attack together!"

"Gold Crow True Flame!" Chi Sect Leader’s eyes shot out countless gold crows made of Gold Crow True Flame, as if the gold crows were reincarnated, lifelike.

The gold crows, fast as lightning, flew towards Sect Leader Yang, who had an incomplete soul and a missing arm, unwilling to face such an extremely yang entity and leapt into the air.

"Abyss Hell—Big Grinding Plate!"

A huge iceberg appeared out of nowhere above Muxue City, causing an uproar among the citizens. What was happening? Why had an iceberg suddenly appeared?!

Countless bronze chains, heated to scalding by flames, emerged from the iceberg, rattling towards Sect Leader Yang as he leapt into the air.

Sect Leader Yang was entangled by the scalding bronze chains and dragged towards the iceberg above Muxue City.

The Big Grinding Plate of Abyss Hell contained the concept of one yin and one yang, the iceberg represented the ultimate yin, and the chains the ultimate yang. When a sinner’s soul was bound by the ultimate yang chains on the ultimate yin iceberg, it was like being crushed by a millstone, only stopping when reduced to dust and bone.

"Golden Cicada shedding skin."

Lu Yang was pondering whether to ask Immortal Fairy to open the ice box.

Before he made up his mind, a thunderous noise came from outside.

Looking out the window after pushing it open, he saw Mu Baiyi and Chi Sect Leader fighting with someone he didn’t recognize.

"Wow, how did they start fighting?"

Meng Jingzhou regretted slapping his thigh; he should never have come to the Extreme Northern Lands.

He hadn’t encountered the four Great Demon Kings, but this time, directly came across four Transcendence Tribulation Stage figures.

Who else’s mundane life has experienced fighting among four Transcendence Tribulation Stage figures above their heads?

In the backyard, Old Ma looked up at the sky, occasionally lowered his head to eat some grass, then continued gazing at the sky.

He had come to the Extreme Northern Lands this time not as a Protector but as an onlooker.

Finally, there was some excitement to watch.

"Why is Mu Baiyi calling him Sect Leader Yang? Could it be that he is Yang Kongdong, the Sect Leader of the Ruthless Sect?" Lu Yang only remembered that the Sect Leader of the Ruthless Sect bore the surname Yang, his real name unknown. According to the testimonies of the two captured Vice Sect Hierarchs, even they weren’t sure if their leader truly bore the surname Yang.

"So it really is Yang Kongdong, huh? I didn’t expect this guy to still be alive, his life really is tough," Immortal Fairy’s voice echoed in Lu Yang’s ears.

"Who would have thought that he didn’t die when he was dismembered back then, but survived the ordeal through a secret technique or perhaps his concubines didn’t actually want to kill him back when they parted his body. Maybe they just wanted a part of his body to stay with them forever?" Immortal Fairy speculated.

"But no matter what, his method is still not as good as my Playing Dead Technique," Immortal Fairy boasted proudly, explaining that practitioners could be dismembered during the Playing Dead Technique but could resurrect exactly as before once the technique was lifted.

Which was unlike Yang Kongdong, who still lacked a piece of his soul—the left arm.

"Does that mean the Sect Leader Yang of the Ruthless Sect is indeed Yang Kongdong?" Lu Yang realized, seeing the deep roots of the Ruthless Sect.

At Su Yuan’s home.

Su Yuan nervously looked at the sky, not expecting that a fight would break out towards the Martial Arts Hall just as he had returned home.

He hoped Lu Yang and the others were safe.

In Su Yuan’s spiritual space, a white figure sealing its own power saw the battle of the three sects through Su Yuan’s eyes. The moment it saw Yang Kongdong, cold sweat broke out and its body trembled continuously as lost memories surged forth, piecing together past experiences.

"I... I remember now; I am the true City Lord of Muxue City!"

It suddenly looked up, anger filling the sky, and roared at Yang Kongdong, "It was you, you killed me and took my place!"

Shi Family.

The Clan Leader of the Ice Flood Dragon Clan, who was reminiscing, stared dumbfoundedly at the sky.

"By the Ancient Ancestor above, how did four Transcendance Tribulation Stage suddenly appear?"

Wasn’t it said that Muxue City didn’t have even one Transcendance Tribulation Stage?

He had just come to attend a parent-teacher meeting, to get acquainted in front of Lu Yang, and afterwards, there was such an exciting show?

In the sky, attacks continuously fell. If any landed within the city, countless would die or be injured. The high officials of Muxue City acted together to block these attacks.

They never thought that their city lord was no longer the same as before.

Muxue City had a City Protection Array, but only the city lord knew how to activate the Array Method, and naturally, Yang Kongdong wouldn’t care about the death of Muxue City’s citizens.

"Not good, two attacks are coming at me at the same time!" Vice City Lord Ma cursed inwardly, unable to withstand two attacks.

Suddenly, a small wooden sword flew by, blocking one of the attacks.

"Elder!" Vice City Lord Ma was very excited and looked for the owner of the small wooden sword, only to find that the greatly respected master of Swordsmanship had always been in Muxue City.

"He’s really troublesome." Yang Kongdong was getting impatient, as he was slightly winning despite being one against three.

But securing a quick victory wasn’t easy.

"Get the arm, complete the soul, and all problems can be solved easily."

Yang Kongdong fought as he moved towards the Martial Arts Hall.

"Not good, stop him!" Mu Baiyi became anxious. Both Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou were in the Martial Arts Hall; these two definitely couldn’t be allowed to come to harm!

And he also faintly felt that a great disaster was imminent. If Sect Leader Yang got close to the Martial Arts Hall, there would no longer be any chance of winning.

Seeing Mu Baiyi’s reaction, Yang Kongdong was certain that Mu Baiyi had found his arm.

"Scorch the heavens, boil the oceans!" Chi Sect Leader and Liu Zhen acted together, flames hot enough to evaporate lakes appearing in their palms with just a wisp.

"Abyss Hell–Avici Blade!"

Mu Baiyi held the Abyss Avici Blade, its blade pitch-black with ghastly figures entwined around it. After it was nurtured for decades in Fengdu, it became his Life-Bound Magical Treasure after he advanced to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

"Plucking Plum Immortal Palm."

Yang Kongdong refused to withstand these attacks directly, repeating his old trick by striking Chi Sect Leader’s abdomen with a palm.

Like a kite with its strings cut, Chi Sect Leader crashed through the ceiling of the Martial Arts Hall and fell in front of Older Teacher Lu, writhing in pain on the ground.

Yang Kongdong’s gaze fell on two ice boxes placed on the podium.

Xue Shilou noticed Yang Kongdong’s gaze and hurriedly guarded his own ice box.

"Found it!"

Yang Kongdong, seeing Xue Shilou’s unusual reaction, reached out with an invisible giant hand and forcibly took the ice box.

"My ice box!" Xue Shilou shouted, but it was to no avail.

Yang Kongdong couldn’t help but touch the ice box, feeling that after so long, his body was finally complete.

The beauties did not kill him after dismembering him back then; instead, each obtained a part of him and buried his body with their dead, unwilling to just be buried like that, he slept using secret techniques, and during the Great Xia period, a junior cultivator awakened his head.

He continuously tempted the junior cultivator to find other parts of his body, aiding his return to his prime.

"Not good, don’t let him get the ice box!" Mu Baiyi shouted, realizing that the ice box contained the "thing" Yang Kongdong mentioned.

"Too late!"

Yang Kongdong crushed the ice box without hesitation, taking out a column of ice shaped like an ice sword.

The fight suddenly stopped, and the three Transcendance Tribulation Stage from both sects looked at Yang Kongdong skeptically. Is this ice sword such a precious thing?

"Where is my arm!"

Chapter 1178: 1177: The Origins of Yang Kongdong’s Various Moves

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou bowed their heads to look at the ice box lying undisturbed on the podium, then simultaneously turned their heads to look at Xue Shilou, who was distressed nearby.

If it weren’t for Xue Shilou protecting his ice box in time, Yang Kongdong wouldn’t have mistaken it for something else.

"Why are you all looking at me? It’s an instinctive reaction to protect one’s belongings when facing danger," Xue Shilou protested his innocence.

"Sect Leader Yang... still has the heart of a child," Mu Baiyi slowly commented, surprised that Sect Leader Yang went through so much trouble just to steal an icicle.

Indeed, everyone in the Ruthless Sect is a bunch of lunatics.

"Fine, fine, fine, dare to trick me!"

Even with his excellent Mind State, Yang Kongdong couldn’t withstand such intense fluctuations in his emotions, and in his uncontrollable anger, he crushed the Ice Sword into pieces of ice, his gaze now fixed on another ice box.

"Don’t give him the opportunity!"

Mu Baiyi sent a message to Chi Sect Leader and Liu Zhen, it seemed that the other ice box was what Yang Kongdong was after!

"Golden Crow Burning Sky!"

Chi Sect Leader soared into the sky, transformed into a body of blazing flames, and from his fiery form split into ten Golden Crows.

Ten suns crossed the horizon, with Golden Crows burning the sky – a supreme magic skill created by the Yaoyang Sect based on the legends of Ancient Times, a skill only the Sect Leader was qualified to learn.

The ten Golden Crows turned into ten beams of golden light, rushing towards Yang Kongdong with an immensely terrifying momentum and oppressive force.

At that moment, the citizens of Muxue City actually felt a heat similar to summer, an experience they had never had before!

"Fusang Tree emerges!" Liu Zhen formed hand signs, conjuring the silhouette of the Fusang Tree from Ancient Times.

With the Fusang Tree’s appearance and the Golden Crows’ cries, this complementing move significantly increased the power of Golden Crow Burning Sky.

"Cold Ice Break."

Yang Kongdong raised his hand, and behind his head appeared a halo of condensed ice, which instantly shattered into ten ice arrows. Drawing his bow, he shot them, each arrow hitting a Golden Crow, causing them to cry out in agony. They reassembled into the form of Chi Sect Leader.

"Fairy, is this an ancient technique?"

"This is the unique skill of the Ice Soul Witch from Ancient Times, Yang Kongdong must have been passed this move by the Ice Soul herself."

"The Plucking Plum Immortal Palm that Yang Kongdong used earlier is one of the three supreme skills of the Heavenly Mountain Sacred Land, a skill that only the Holy Master, Saint Heir, and Heavenly Mountain Saintess are qualified to learn."

"The Heavenly Mountain Saintess of that era was enamored with Yang Kongdong, and against the ancestral teachings, she passed on the technique to him. She was then harshly punished by the Holy Master and confined for a hundred years."

"For this, Yang Kongdong brazenly charged into the Heavenly Mountain Sacred Land, defeated the Holy Master, and abducted the Saintess. It caused quite a commotion back then."

"You see, his footwork is very exquisite, almost like he can move through space. This is the ’Carefree Steps,’ created by Leng Ningxiang based on the Rapid Method of the Kunpeng Clan and combined with assassination techniques. It is a first-rate movement skill, with a speed so fast it’s almost like teleportation."

Lu Yang’s eyelid twitched. This Yang Kongdong certainly had learned a mix of skills.

At this moment, Yang Kongdong treated the two sects’ three Transcendance Tribulation Stage figures as live targets, pulling his bow and shooting arrows. He shot through the Fusang Tree with a single stroke, his Extreme Cold Arrow Intent melting the fiery tree. Liu Zhen, caught off guard, clutched his chest and vomited blood.

Yang Kongdong kept shooting cold arrows. Liu Zhen was hit by three arrows; although they weren’t fatal, they greatly hindered his movements, preventing him from executing half his skills.

When Yang Kongdong released the fourth arrow, Liu Zhen, seeing no way to avoid it, gritted his teeth and bit down on the arrow, his teeth aching from the cold, distorting his features into a grimace.

Watching from below, Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou felt a chill themselves.

A pitch-black ghost saber struck, with all manner of demons and ghosts rampant, interrupting Yang Kongdong’s fifth arrow. It was Mu Baiyi, wielding the Avici Saber Technique.

On another side, Chi Sect Leader evolved a golden sun imprint with his hands, launching it at Yang Kongdong. The terrifying might burst forth like a torrential flood, causing the entire space to tremble. Any Integration Period Cultivator touched by it would meet their end.

Yang Kongdong’s right hand flicked, and the Ice Soul Bow and Arrow transformed into a great spear, simultaneously blocking the attack of two people.

Liu Zhen believed in an eye for an eye—he had been beaten so badly, he was determined to tear off a piece of flesh from Yang Kongdong, even with his seriously injured body.
