Hearing Immortal Jiuchong’s analysis, The Controller smiled subtly and began blatantly using his Dao Fruit skills. In an instant, all the Immortal Fire and Profound Ice flying towards him were spiritually awakened and turned against Immortal Jiuchong.

"Using my own moves against me?"

Immortal Jiuchong had a thought flash through his mind, conjuring the same number of Immortal Fire and Profound Ice to collide with those controlled by The Controller, causing the earth and mountains to shake within a ten-thousand-mile radius.

"Managed to block it? Not bad," The Controller said breezily, now holding the upper hand.

Immortal Jiuchong could control Immortal Fire and even manifest the Sun, but whether it was Immortal Fire or the Sun, they were tangible, and anything tangible was within The Controller’s control. How could Immortal Jiuchong make a move?

Suddenly, Immortal Jiuchong was struck by an unknown attack. The strike was powerful, sending him flying, crashing through over a dozen mountains before coming to a halt.

Where did that attack come from?!

Immortal Jiuchong quickly realized that Spiritual Energy also fell under The Controller’s control—it was an attack from the Spiritual Energy!

With that thought, Immortal Jiuchong took a deep breath, drawing the Spiritual Energy from within a thousand miles and refining it inside his body.

Immortal Jiuchong guessed that The Controller could only control non-living things; if he could control living things, he would have controlled Jiuchong’s body long ago, not just the Immortal Fire and Spiritual Energy.

So, by absorbing and refining all the Spiritual Energy, making it a part of his body, The Controller could no longer control it!

The Controller raised an eyebrow, surprised that Immortal Jiuchong, though he didn’t figure out the correct Dao Fruit, had made the right choice.

"How will you handle this now!"

With a thought from The Controller’s Immortal Sense, he enlightened the mountains surrounding Muxue City, which awoke like a giant sleeping on the ground.

The Snow Mountain Giant rose, towering ten thousand feet tall.

Immediately after, the earth swelled, forming layers of cages trapping Immortal Jiuchong.

The earth had also been enlightened by The Controller, used at his command!

Everything under heaven and earth was within The Controller’s scope of control!

The battlefield shifted outside the city, and those daring cultivators in the city left Muxue City en masse to follow and watch the battle.

They watched in awe as one snow mountain after another, under The Controller’s summons, stood like people upon the earth.

Yun Zhi, looking down at the quaking, awakening snow mountains beneath her feet, shook her head helplessly, packed up the Emperor’s Chair, and jumped down from the mountain to find a spot unaffected by the tumult to watch the battle.

"Earth Snow Mountains? Merely mundane objects daring to trap an immortal!" Immortal Jiuchong bellowed, his body shuddering as he broke free like a dragon escaping its chains, shattering the earthly shackles and knocking over a Snow Mountain Giant with a punch.

The Snow Mountain Giant, headless, fell to the ground, causing it to shake.

"Mundane objects, you say? Then take a look at these!"

As Immortal Jiuchong smashed the head of a Snow Mountain Giant, a metallic, lustrous humanoid figure flew out from within, slamming Jiuchong into the ground!

Boom—

The heavens shattered, the earth exploded, and a large blast erupted underground. The humanoid figure was sent flying by a punch from Immortal Jiuchong, who then burst forth from the soil.

Yet, after taking a heavy punch from Immortal Jiuchong, the humanoid figure remained intact, only bearing a fist imprint.

"Immortal Gold?" Immortal Jiuchong frowned.

This was not a magic artifact crafted from Immortal Gold; The Controller didn’t have the assets of a Yingtian Immortal; it was merely raw Immortal Gold. However, this piece of Immortal Gold, enlightened by The Controller, could be referred to as a living Immortal Gold entity, terribly hard.

"Always able to surprise me with something unexpected," Immortal Jiuchong sneered.

"But you surely don’t think my Yin-Yang Dao Fruit can only control Immortal Fire and Profound Ice, do you?"

Chapter 1188: 1178: The True Abilities of the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit

"Blustering!"

The Controller remained steadfast, unshaken by the verbal provocation of his opponent. Countless Snow Mountain Giants rose from the ground, kneeling before him in submission, and then the earth gave birth to numerous agile stone pillars, as flexible as the tentacles of an octopus.

Immortal Jiuchong’s fist knocked down a Snow Mountain Giant, but immediately afterward, a being made of Immortal Gold, hiding inside the giant’s head, launched a sneak attack on Immortal Jiuchong.

The Snow Mountain Giants were merely a distraction, the real trick lay in the beings made of Immortal Gold hidden inside!

These pieces of Immortal Gold were unrefined and lacked the might of Immortal Artifacts, but Immortal Gold itself is renowned for its strength. The Yingtian Immortal even used Immortal Gold to forge his body, demonstrating how terrifyingly hard Immortal Gold could be.

Immortal Jiuchong’s fist left an imprint on the face of the Immortal Gold being, yet it caused no substantial damage!

Immortal Jiuchong intended to leap into the air, but from the ground, a segment of a tree branch, acting like a tentacle, sprung up and entwined around his ankle. This was a heavenly tree from countless Suiyues ago, frozen within the permafrost, now brought to life by the command of The Controller.

Beneath the lands of the Extreme Northern Lands lay buried countless corpses, all within the range of The Controller’s animating powers.

Though Immortal Jiuchong immediately broke free from the grasp of the tree branch tentacle, it provided an opportunity for the beings of Immortal Gold.

Three to five Immortal Gold beings swarmed forward, pinning down Immortal Jiuchong’s limbs, with one sitting atop Jiuchong and punching his face repeatedly, each blow strong enough to smash a Pass Tribulation Cultivator to death.

Immortal Jiuchong instantly transformed into a being of flame, corporeal yet intangible, passing through the beings of Immortal Gold.

"Refine!"

Immortal Jiuchong bellowed, and his Immortal Fire enveloped the Immortal Gold beings. His Immortal Fire was most suited for refining Immortal Gold; in the Ancient Times, even the Yingtian Immortal borrowed his fire to forge Immortal Artifacts.

In an instant, the Immortal Gold beings were melted into a liquid mass.

Whoosh—

A blob of liquid flew out from the flames toward Immortal Jiuchong.

Even when transformed into liquid, the Immortal Gold beings remained under The Controller’s animation, still obeying his commands to attack Immortal Jiuchong by all means necessary.

Immortal Jiuchong rapidly retreated, knowing that if the liquid Immortal Gold beings surrounded and then solidified around him, he would be trapped in an impenetrable cage of Immortal Gold, which would be quite troublesome.

Simultaneously, he extinguished his own Immortal Fire used for refining the Immortal Gold beings before The Controller could commandeer it.

The Controller clicked his tongue. Indeed, as an Ancient True Immortal, Jiuchong’s reaction was impressively swift.

The Immortal Gold beings, now liquid, took on various shapes, extending indefinitely and forming an exceedingly thin layer that adhered to the bodiless Snow Mountain Giants.

The Immortal Gold solidified into a layer of armor for the Snow Mountain Giants, granting them the strength to battle Immortal Jiuchong.

One by one, the Snow Mountain Giants pounded their chests, resembling headless great apes.

The Controller then procured Mystic Ice, which Immortal Jiuchong had manifested during their earlier confrontation but had not yet been fully consumed.

The Mystic Ice, too, was alive, and at The Controller’s bidding, it morphed into ice spears, becoming weapons for the Snow Mountain Giants.

Now armed with armor and weapons, the Snow Mountain Giants surged forward, attacking Immortal Jiuchong, their Mystic Ice Spears posing a real threat to Jiuchong in his fiery form.

A Mystic Ice Spear pierced Immortal Jiuchong’s body, momentarily giving him a corporeal form, and seizing the opportunity, countless Snow Mountain Giants pinned him down and hammered him into the ground, where a vortex appeared, swallowing Immortal Jiuchong whole.

Then, the earth heaved and cracked, fissures emanating light, followed by a surge of fire that blasted apart the numerous Snow Mountain Giants, akin to a volcanic eruption—a doomsday scene never to be witnessed in the Extreme Northern Lands.

Faced with the wrath of Immortal Jiuchong, The Controller remained undeterred, continuing to order the Snow Mountain Giants to assault.

Immortal Jiuchong hovered in the air, consumed by towering anger.

Suddenly, an illusory chessboard appeared on the ground, immensely vast, with each Snow Mountain Giant positioned within a square.

What is this about!

The Controller became alert, for there was no mention in the ancient texts of Immortal Jiuchong possessing such a technique!

"All things in this world can be divided into black and white."

"The Snow Mountain belongs to the Yin Attribute."

Immortal Jiuchong’s voice boomed, as if speaking to himself. Aside from Fairy Eternity and Eldest Senior Sister, no one understood what he meant.

As Immortal Jiuchong’s words echoed, a very inconspicuous black chess piece appeared above the heads of the Snow Mountain Giants.

Immortal Jiuchong’s index finger and middle finger were stacked together, pinching a white chess piece.

The white piece fell, overlapping with the black piece above the head of the Snow Mountain Giant, where black and white collided, mutually dissolving each other.

The bi-colored chess pieces disappeared, and astonishingly, the Snow Mountain Giant also vanished into thin air.

"In this world where there is Yin, there must be Yang. Yin and Yang generate each other, and they also conquer each other."

The Controller stumbled backward, finally understanding what Immortal Jiuchong had done.

Yin and Yang generate and overcome each other. The Snow Mountain Giant was a creature of Yin attributes. When it came into contact with an equal amount of Yang attributes, Yin and Yang collided, and both would be annihilated!

"No, the Snow Mountain Giant is a creature of the Five Elements. How could it be divided by Yin and Yang?" The Controller still had some confusion.

Immortal Jiuchong burst into laughter, taking steps closer to The Controller.

"The Dao Fruit represents the rules. Before the emergence of the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit, it wasn’t clear whether the things in this world were categorized according to the Five Elements or Yin and Yang."

"But now that my Yin-Yang Dao Fruit has appeared, everything in this world is classified by Yin and Yang. The division by the Five Elements is merely a more detailed classification based on the division of Yin and Yang!"

In other words, these are the rules set by the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit!

Immortal Jiuchong realized that The Controller did not control the Dao Fruit of authority; he couldn’t possibly manipulate so many Snow Mountain Giants for precise attacks. Instead, he possessed Dao Fruits related to imparting life or awakening spirits.

But in his presence, it didn’t matter.

The Controller’s face turned pale. Originally, he could animate all things, even Immortal Fire and Profound Ice. He thought he could restrain Immortal Jiuchong,

However, the reality was starkly different. Immortal Jiuchong prescribed the rules for Yin and Yang, allowing annihilation of Yin and Yang, which was actually what suppressed The Controller!

"I want to see how much you can annihilate!"

The Controller activated the Dao Fruit of awakening spirits. The earth shook, people of Muxue City stumbled left and right, thinking it was an earthquake.

Those high-level cultivators turned pale; this was definitely not as simple as an earthquake!

The entire Extreme Northern Lands were in turmoil, with the first change appearing around The Controller.

Beneath his feet, the ground rose, not becoming giants or such, but transformed into an earthen sphere covered with snow and ice.

Muxue City was also on this earthen sphere!

The sphere was incredibly large; standing upon it, one could not even feel its curvature!

The people of Muxue City did not understand the meaning of this earthen sphere, and even the high-level cultivators had a vague concept of it. Lu Yang, however, couldn’t be clearer.

Nowadays, all six continents were forged from star planets. The Controller made the earth come alive, reverting it to its original, primitive planetary form!

The Controller could use these star planets to strike at Immortal Jiuchong at any time.

"So what if they are star planets!" scoffed Immortal Jiuchong. In the Ancient Times, battles would easily shatter stars; what threat could these few planets pose?

He could even use Yin and Yang to counter each other and make these planets disappear.

"No, there are people on the planets!" Immortal Jiuchong’s true personality cried out. The Controller was threatening to use the lives of the Extreme Northern Lands.

"So what!" the other personality of Immortal Jiuchong was unmoved, which was exactly what his true personality feared.

"Let me control the body!" The true personality of Immortal Jiuchong wrestled for control of the body.

The Controller sneered, eager to see what else Immortal Jiuchong could do.

Immortal Jiuchong took a couple of deep breaths; it had been a long time since he had used this move.

"Extreme Yin Cold!"

Immortal Jiuchong opened his Yin Domain. The already cold temperatures of the Extreme Northern Lands fell further and further, dropping to a point that had never been seen before in history.

Absolute Zero!

It was as if the world froze in that moment. All living beings in the Extreme Northern Lands were immobilized, the land itself no longer transforming into a planet, the entire realm of the Extreme Northern Lands was enveloped by absolute zero. Under absolute zero, no object could move, resulting in an effect akin to a halt in time!

"See what I mean, what did I tell you? Controlling Immortal Fire and such, these are merely superficial abilities of the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit. These are the true powers."

Chapter 1189: 1188: Hush Money

Immortal Jiuchong’s technique wasn’t a true halting of time, he could only cause an area to enter Absolute Zero, where time in the Extreme Northern Lands comes to a stop while the rest of the continent continues as normal.

The range of the Time Immortal’s time stop encompassed the whole world.

Seeing the situation take a turn for the worse, The Controller turned and ran.

All his methods had been countered by Immortal Jiuchong, the battle was going poorly, and if not now, then when?

"Not good, he’s trying to escape!" Lu Yang shouted. A battle between Immortals, even if one could determine the victor, it was hard to seal one’s fate; if one wanted to flee, the other could hardly stop them.

However, Lu Yang quickly relaxed, although Eldest Senior Sister had not made a move, she must have arrived by now.

With Eldest Senior Sister here, Immortal Jiuchong was sure not to escape.

"Why call that Miss Yun for help? We, the Five Ancient Immortals, are enough to catch someone." Fairy Eternity would rather ask the Four Ancient Immortals for help than seek Miss Yun’s assistance.

"Fairy, are you sure Immortal Jiuchong can catch someone?"

"Of course, Immortal Jiuchong alone can’t manage it, but I can call for help."

"Little Yang, go out and shout ’Yingtian Immortal is petty’ a couple of times, and Yingtian Immortal will come over."

"Yingtian Immortal has always been like this; he could sense anyone who cursed him, but then Ashy Bean was around, Yingtian Immortal didn’t dare to tune in, but now that Ashy Bean’s gone, his senses have restored."

"...Fairy, wouldn’t it still be better to ask Eldest Senior Sister to help catch the person?" After some deliberation, Lu Yang felt that catching a Daqian Immortal wasn’t worth risking his own small life.

"Oh, what are you afraid of, with me protecting you, nothing will go wrong!" Fairy Eternity held her small chin up and gave her chest a confident pat.

A subject must obey when the emperor commands his death; without any other choice, Lu Yang left the spiritual world and whispered a sentence.

"Yingtian Immortal is petty..."

Crack—

Absolute silence reigned in the Absolute Zero domain of the Extreme Northern Lands, the sudden sound of thunder was terribly conspicuous.

Thunder tore through the void, and Yingtian Immortal, constructed of lightning, appeared in front of Lu Yang, his expression somewhat unfriendly.

"Kid, was it you who called me petty?"

"This..." Lu Yang’s forehead broke out in sweat, stammering. Yingtian Immortal had arrived too quickly; wasn’t this very pettiness?

Fairy Eternity stepped forward to back up Lu Yang, protecting him as though she were shielding a small chick: "It was I who cursed, what of it?"

Yingtian Immortal’s expression shifted then settled into a smile, he chuckled sheepishly: "I was wondering who was reminding me to be careful, and it turns out to be you, Fairy Eternity."

It was only now that Yingtian Immortal noticed something amiss, this was the domain of Absolute Zero, also detecting the chase high in the sky, he noticed the dueling sides.

"Immortal Jiuchong and The Controller?"

At the same time, he looked at Lu Yang in astonishment; how come you’re at the scene again, kid?

"You first help Immortal Jiuchong." Fairy Eternity urged.

Yingtian Immortal nodded and flew towards the sky.

"Ying Tian?"

Immortal Jiuchong wasn’t surprised by the arrival of Yingtian Immortal; that thunderclap made it obvious someone had cursed Yingtian Immortal, announcing the arrival of his Thunder Clone.

"Another one?!" The Controller couldn’t help but glance back while frantically escaping, almost cursing out loud.

Those Four Ancient Immortals they couldn’t find no matter how hard they looked before, how come two have suddenly turned up in such a short span?

Was what Yang Kongdong said true, that this place had clues to the Four Ancient Immortals? But these clues were just too many.

"Let’s join forces and catch him."

"Alright!"

The two had known each other since Ancient Times, and although both had suspected each other of being body-snatched by some mastermind behind the scenes, with no contact for three hundred thousand years, they set aside their past grievances in the face of a common enemy and joined forces to capture the foe.

Ying Tian Immortal’s Thunder Clone was unbelievably fast, intercepting The Controller in the blink of an eye.

The Controller tried to escape in a different direction but was intercepted by Immortal Jiuchong.

"Thunder Tribulation..." Ying Tian Immortal was about to habitually summon the Thunder Tribulation to deal with The Controller when Immortal Jiuchong stopped him.

"Don’t use Thunder Tribulation, his Dao Fruit ability is Enlightenment!"

Ying Tian Immortal decisively abandoned the summoning of Thunder Tribulation, and the two tacitly used their physical bodies to fight.

The Controller had managed to fight Immortal Jiuchong to a standstill only because of the added strength from his bone armor, but now with Ying Tian Immortal joining in, there was no chance for resistance.

Before long, Immortal Jiuchong and Ying Tian Immortal were dragging The Controller back to Muxue City, his many ornaments having fallen off during the fight, lost somewhere, now resembling a plucked rooster.

The Controller did not understand why the two were taking him back to Muxue City.

"Done!"

The two threw The Controller to the ground, and Immortal Fairy emerged from Lu Yang’s body, boasting triumphantly.

"How about that, now you know how formidable we Five Ancient Immortals are, right?"

Then she bragged to Lu Yang, "See, what did I tell you? We didn’t even need Miss Yun to lift a finger to deal with this ’Death Life’ or whatever!"

"It’s The Controller," Lu Yang corrected her.

The Controller stared in alarm at Immortal Fairy, not understanding what she meant by the "Five Ancient Immortals."

Wasn’t it the Four Ancient Immortals?

"So that’s it, I didn’t realize the Tribulation Dao Fruit could be used like this." A familiar voice came from behind Ying Tian Immortal, frightening Ying Tian Immortal so much that he almost dissipated his Thunder Clone.

"Eldest Senior Sister!"

"You did well," said Yun Zhi, nodding.

No one knew that what Yun Zhi truly appreciated wasn’t the capturing of The Controller but Lu Yang’s ability to solve Yang Kongdong on his own.

From initially needing her help when facing a Transcendance Tribulation Stage enemy, to now being able to stand on his own, resolving a Semi-Immortal by himself, was indeed remarkable progress.

The Controller looked at Yun Zhi in disbelief, finally understanding why the Emperor had stepped forward only to retreat immediately.

So this terrifying existence that could even chill the Emperor’s spine was also in the Extreme Northern Lands.

"Let’s get this clear, I am the one who caught this person!" Immortal Fairy quickly spoke up as Yun Zhi seemed about to take The Controller away.

"... Fairy, can this capture really be counted as yours?" Lu Yang couldn’t help but tease.

Immortal Fairy declared righteously, "Whoever is caught through my connections is considered a capture by me."

"What exactly do you want, Senior Fairy? Do you want to become Sect Leader?" Yun Zhi was curious too, not understanding why Immortal Fairy put so much emphasis on this.

Capturing an Immortal to tally Contribution Points, then exchanging them for a Sect Leader voucher, would probably be a process calculated in tens of thousands of years.

Immortal Fairy waved her hand dismissively, "I don’t want to be any Sect Leader. This is hush money."

Immortal Fairy’s words left everyone present scratching their heads.

"Hush money?"

Yun Zhi repeated the words and chuckled inside, guessing Immortal Fairy’s intention—she probably didn’t want people to know she could restore her Immortal strength at any time.

Or perhaps, was it a thank-you gift for not telling Junior Brother about this? Was Senior Fairy too embarrassed to say it out loud?

Imagining Senior Fairy’s internal struggling and reluctance brought an unusual smile to Yun Zhi’s face. She shook her head with a laugh, "Then I’ll accept this hush money."

"No laughing allowed!" Immortal Fairy became furious and stomped her foot in annoyance.

Immortal Jiuchong, Ying Tian Immortal, Yun Zhi’s Junior Brother, the Immortal Lineage’s Second Boss, a High Minister and the Emperor’s Master of the Great Bean Dynasty, Young Sect Master of the Heavenly Court Sect, Speechless Daoist’s disciple, and a peerless Sword Cultivator all exchanged looks, utterly confused by the situation.

Chapter 1190: 189 The Second Battle between Immortal Jiuchong and The Controller

"Jiuchong, what are your plans? Are you still planning to stay inside this white jade token?"

After successfully sending The Controller away as hush money, Immortal Fairy Eternity was in high spirits.

"Has Jiuchong been staying in the wordless white jade token all these years?"

At this moment, the Extreme Northern Lands were still in the Absolute Zero Domain, and everyone was frozen like ice sculptures, motionless, even the Snow Emperor was no exception, frozen solid. Yingtian Immortal curiously observed the white jade token hanging on the Snow Emperor’s chest, feeling as if their fates were somewhat similar.

Both were forced to hide in the Small World space after the fall of the Fire Dynasty.

"Now that Silent Extermination Immortal is gone, there’s no need for Jiuchong to stay here, right?"

However, Immortal Jiuchong shook his head, which surprised Yingtian Immortal, "I still can’t leave this place. Before you came, Yingtian, it was my other personality that was fighting The Controller."

"Another personality?"

Immortal Jiuchong sighed, "It’s embarrassing to say, do you remember Joy Venerate?"

"That Joy Venerate with the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo?"

"Yes, him. I fought him when I was a Semi-Immortal. At that time, he used the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo to bring out my ’Sadness,’ and under the influence of ’Sadness,’ I also became ’Angry.’ In an outburst of emotion, I killed Joy Venerate in one fell swoop."

"But that battle also left me a lingering problem. The true power of the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo lies in leaving a shadow in the bottom of your heart. Even if the holder of the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo disappears, this shadow will always exist because the issue lies with ’me,’ not with the ’Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo.’"

"At first, I didn’t know of this shadow’s existence. It has never appeared in the Ancient Times."

"After Immortal became assassinated, that shadow appeared. Sadness and Anger took over my body. It’s just that everyone had similar emotions back then, and none of you noticed anything strange about me."

"After that battle ended, the shadow disappeared again, and I thought that was the end of it."

"However, when Silent Extermination Immortal destroyed the Fire Dynasty, facing the scenes of the dynasty’s doomsday, the shadow appeared for the second time."

"It was then that the shadow became a personality, beginning to fight with me for control of the body."

"I was worried that his emergence would cause widespread devastation, so I have been staying in the sparsely populated Extreme Northern Lands."

"That personality only knows battle, is extremely belligerent, and doesn’t care about the lives of others. If it weren’t for me stopping him just now, I’m afraid he might have obliterated all the stars in the Extreme Northern Lands, and the entire region would have perished."

Yingtian Immortal hissed, this indeed was a big problem. If something like this happened, I’m afraid Yun Zhi would take you down before you could defeat The Controller.

"Extremely belligerent?" Yingtian Immortal stroked his chin, thinking of a way to help his good brother, "Has that personality ever fought Silent Extermination Immortal?"

Immortal Jiuchong sighed, "He fought during the time of the Fire Dynasty. He nearly became the dominant personality, and I would have become the subordinate one. Knowing that he couldn’t defeat Silent Extermination Immortal, he voluntarily gave up control of the body to me. That’s how I have managed to suppress him for the past three hundred thousand years."

"If he loves fighting so much, could he defeat Daoist Yun Zhi?" Yingtian Immortal was curious.

As soon as Yingtian Immortal spoke, the normally amiable expressions of Immortal Jiuchong changed, becoming coarse and angry, and he looked at Yingtian Immortal with a cold sneer, "Ying Tian, after three hundred thousand years, I didn’t expect you to ask such a foolish question!"

The belligerent and angry personality once again took control of Immortal Jiuchong’s body!

Yingtian Immortal seemed to answer his own question, as if introducing an opponent to his good brother, "Although Daoist Yun Zhi hasn’t been cultivating for a long time, she holds the position of Eldest Senior Sister among the younger generation in the Sect and her master has just reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage. She hasn’t learned much from her master, and I’ve exchanged a few moves with her myself, still manageable to fight."

"Oh? It seems this young girl really has something!" Immortal Jiuchong perked up at this, as his fight with The Controller hadn’t been satisfying, and he wanted to continue the fight.

With that said, Immortal Jiuchong immediately took up a stance, causing Yun Zhi’s brow to furrow, "Are you going to fight me here?"

Immortal Jiuchong cursed loudly, "You little brat, rambling on and nitpicking. If it’s a fight you want, just fight. Why pick the place...!"

Bang—

Before Immortal Jiuchong could react, he felt a punch in his abdomen and was sent flying backward in the sky.

Yun Zhi grabbed The Controller, her launch like a cannonball, creating a sonic boom that shattered the Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall.

Lu Yang shivered at the sight. Immortal Jiuchong’s careless words had clearly enraged Eldest Senior Sister; otherwise, she wouldn’t have caused such a major commotion.

Facing the formidable Immortal Jiuchong with both fear and excitement, she flew backward, exclaiming, "Good, good, good! You actually have some skills. Even though it was a sneak attack, the fact that you managed to send me flying shows you’ve got some ability!"

Immortal Jiuchong hovered mid-air, surrounded by countless flames of Immortal Fire and Profound Ice, like stars orbiting around him, protecting Immortal Jiuchong.

Not to mention the Profound Ice, but these Immortal Fires alone could melt the glaciers in the Extreme Northern Lands and turn them into a vast ocean!

"Go!"

Immortal Jiuchong, like an emperor commanding fire and ice, led the charge against Yun Zhi. The trailing Immortal Fire and Profound Ice painted a brilliant trail, reminiscent of the Flying Sky Map from Ancient Times. Wherever it passed, icebergs melted only to be frozen in that moment!

Confronted with the ferocious charge of Immortal Jiuchong, Yun Zhi glanced at her empty left hand, capable of unleashing the Cloud Falling Palm, and then at her right hand holding The Controller. She decisively charged toward Immortal Jiuchong with The Controller in tow.

Both Immortal Jiuchong and The Controller were shocked.

Originally, Yun Zhi hadn’t planned on bringing The Controller into the fight, but if she left with Immortal Jiuchong, only Junior Brother, the Fairy predecessors, and Yingtian Immortal would be left in Muxue City, making it all too easy for The Controller to escape.

Junior Brother went without saying; he wasn’t a match for The Controller yet.

The Fairy predecessor was still recovering from serious injuries. If forced to suddenly regain her strength, she would likely end up further injured and then squatting in Junior Brother’s Spiritual Space for three hundred years.

Yingtian Immortal had only sent a Thunder Clone, with half the usual power. Single-handedly, he most certainly couldn’t beat The Controller.

The Controller, bombarded by the Immortal Fire and Profound Ice, cried out in pain and then collided head-on with Immortal Jiuchong!

Even Immortal Jiuchong, courageous and battle-skilled as he was, hadn’t expected Yun Zhi’s move and was immediately dazed.

Yun Zhi didn’t care about Immortal Jiuchong’s reaction; she lifted The Controller and struck again!

Immortal Jiuchong crossed his arms in front of his chest to block but didn’t anticipate the great force and heavy momentum behind The Controller’s strike. It exceeded his ability to defend, and once again he was sent flying, piercing through countless stars in the process.

Immortal Jiuchong was fast, but Yun Zhi was faster. She appeared in his path and hit him with The Controller again, not giving him a chance to catch his breath.

Even Immortal Jiuchong, confident in his Immortal Body, couldn’t withstand such high-frequency, intense attacks. He soon swelled up with bruises.

"Huff, huff—"

Facing another attack from Yun Zhi, Immortal Jiuchong split into Yin Body and Yang Body to dodge the strike, then merged back together.

"I didn’t expect you, little girl, to know how to use The Controller as your weapon. Fighting while armed against an unarmed opponent is hardly honorable. If you’re capable, fight me bare-handed!"

If Yun Zhi had been wielding an Immortal Artifact, Immortal Jiuchong could have obliterated it with the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit. But her weapon was The Controller—an Immortal’s Body. No matter how powerful the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit was, it couldn’t annihilate an Immortal outright, so using The Controller as her weapon was quite troublesome for Immortal Jiuchong.

Yun Zhi thought Immortal Jiuchong made sense, so she switched to using her left hand to fight.

"Cloud Falling Palm."

Chapter 1191: 1190: Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall Emerges Anew

"Heh, young people are just too young, so easily baited by provocation!"

Immortal Jiuchong laughed uproariously. Now that Yun Zhi had voluntarily given up the Controller, a weapon she was adept at using, how could she be his opponent!

"Mountains without bounds, sun and moon alternate!"

A majestic scene appeared above Immortal Jiuchong’s head, with mountains, rivers, lakes, and seas all vividly present; even the sun and moon hung high, marking the passage of time.

To the casual observer, it would appear as a piece of space cut from the Central Continent, but upon contact, one would realize it was an endless world evolved from Immortal Fire and Profound Ice by Immortal Jiuchong. Touching it would trigger the interplay of yin and yang, bursting forth with endless Primordial Qi!

This move was one of his trump cards. He had not dared to use it during the fight with the Controller, fearing the Controller might enlighten this endless world.

But fighting against an unarmed Yun Zhi, there was no need for such concerns.

Finally, as an observer, the Controller identified the impressive power of this move. Once it struck the ground, the Extreme Northern Lands would transform into a realm of both fire and ice.

How would Yun Zhi respond?

"Cloud Falling Palm."

The large, fluffy hand seemed like a cloud plucked from the sky, and that cloud hand grasped onto the world of Immortal Fire and Profound Ice.

An explosion was heard within the hand, stained with varying colors of Immortal Fire and Profound Ice, the colors changing continuously as if a dye vat had been overturned.

A tiny bit of the explosion leaked between the fingers, directly piercing through the Extreme Northern Lands, down to the world’s deepest layer.

One could only imagine the true explosive power of the world of Immortal Fire and Profound Ice.

But besides that, no more explosive force leaked out.

Seeing this, Immortal Jiuchong’s expression drastically changed. He also could not comprehend how Yun Zhi’s combat power could have increased without using the Controller as a weapon.

"Cloud Falling Palm."

"Nine Layer Ice Shield!"

Facing the second Cloud Falling Palm, Immortal Jiuchong immediately constructed an absolute defense with Profound Ice. The Nine Layer Ice Shield, his strongest defensive technique, stacked together.

A pleasing cracking sound, like a musical instrument accompanying the battle, resonated as the layers of ice shields were penetrated. The shattered ice cascaded down like a shower, and Immortal Jiuchong, along with the ice shards, became embedded in the snowy ground!

Struggling to climb out of the frozen hole, Immortal Jiuchong then saw the third palm strike bearing down on him.

"This body is returned to you!" Not daring to face Yun Zhi head-on, the belligerent personality returned control of the body to Immortal Jiuchong without a word.

"Why give it back to me at this time!" Immortal Jiuchong was panicked, relinquishing control of his own body. Just what was Yun Zhi’s background, and how exactly had the Yingtian Immortal managed to exchange blows with her!?

"Why do you care so much, just take it when I give it to you!"

"I said I don’t want it!"

The belligerent personality and Immortal Jiuchong pushed against each other, each yielding body control.

The Cloud Falling Palm descended, and peace returned to the world.

The Controller swallowed hard. No wonder the Emperor ran upon seeing Yun Zhi. If the Emperor had been a step slower earlier, they both might have been left behind by Yun Zhi.

"Cloud Falling..."

"Don’t attack, don’t attack, it’s me!" Seeing Yun Zhi ready to continue the fight, Immortal Jiuchong hastily raised his hands to surrender.

"Are you back to normal?"

"I guess you could say that." Immortal Jiuchong replied cautiously, observing Yun Zhi’s expression, "The belligerent personality and I have agreed that he won’t come out without my permission."

"That’s good."

...

Lu Yang flew above Muxue City to observe the battle.

Yun Zhi had kicked Immortal Jiuchong so far away at the start of the fight that Lu Yang couldn’t see the specifics of their exchange, only distant clouds falling, as if heaven and earth were collapsing.

Before long, Yun Zhi, carrying the Controller, returned to Muxue City with Immortal Jiuchong in tow.

"How did the fight go?" Yingtian Immortal asked eagerly, feigning ignorance.

Immortal Jiuchong glared at Yingtian Immortal.

Yun Zhi landed with a frown. When she took off, the commotion was too big, and Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall turned into ruins. Only the two main doors remained intact, at Huo Huashen’s place.

"I’ll fix it, I’ll fix it!" declared the combative personality, volunteering to rectify the mistake after getting a nod from Immortal Jiuchong.

Yun Zhi silently observed the combative personality, who became extremely uneasy under her gaze. Only when Yun Zhi uttered a word of approval did he feel like he had been granted amnesty.

"Let me see what material this martial arts hall is made of... Are the doors made from Ten-thousand-year-old Coiled Dragon Wood?"

The combative Immortal Jiuchong paused for a moment, surprised that the martial arts hall had such high-quality doors.

Other areas were shattered by Daoist Yun Zhi, the spirituality of the Heaven and Earth Treasures vibrated into dispersal, so it was impossible to tell what materials were used elsewhere.

If the doors are made from Ten-thousand-year-old Coiled Dragon Wood, wouldn’t the materials used for the rest of the building be similarly valuable?

Over the past thirty thousand years, the Snow Emperor’s rule spanned six generations, accumulating countless treasures. Immortal Jiuchong took out various precious Heaven and Earth Treasures from the Snow Emperor’s wordless white jade token and clanged away as he began construction.

Yingtian Immortal meticulously listed the construction materials: beams made of Thunder Tribulation Punishment Wood, roofing tiles fired from Soil of Rest, bricks refined from Five-colored Soil...

Soon, Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall was fitted with the main doors and restored to its original state.

Meanwhile, electric arcs flickered continuously around Yingtian Immortal as he departed with a flourish, "The time for this Thunder Tribulation clone is almost up. Immortal Jiuchong, let’s catch up another time!"

"Alright."

Both the combative personality and Immortal Jiuchong responded in unison.

"You release the Absolute Zero Domain; you restore the Extreme Northern Lands." Yun Zhi said. The Controller had the ability to revert the Extreme Northern Lands back to their stellar appearance and could change it back again; it wasn’t a difficult task.

After witnessing Yun Zhi’s power, both Immortal Jiuchong and The Controller were very compliant.

The Absolute Zero Domain quickly dissipated, and the stars suspended in the sky gradually unfolded into the shape of a continent, though the process was imperceptibly slow.

The citizens of Muxue City were utterly confused. Just a moment ago, an incredible battle raged in the sky, but now, in the blink of an eye, both sides of the fight had disappeared?

"Was I dreaming?"

"No way, we can’t all be having the same dream."

"Then what’s the explanation for this?"

"I don’t know."

Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall was bustling with activity, with everyone sporting a variety of expressions.

Uncle Murong was shocked at the sight of Immortal Jiuchong and The Controller appearing in the martial arts hall. Weren’t these two fighting to the death just moments ago? How did they end up here?

Huo Huashen was first puzzled about who had taken his doors, then he noticed the presence of Immortal Jiuchong and The Controller, showing the same expression as Uncle Murong.

Young Master Meng didn’t find it strange at all to see Immortal Jiuchong and The Controller here after seeing Eldest Senior Sister.

The Snow Emperor wore an expression of an elderly retiree.

"Little Snow, I am so grateful for your company over these eighty thousand years, but I no longer have a reason to stay in the white jade token."

With the issues of Silent Extermination Immortal and the dual personalities resolved, Immortal Jiuchong had no more worries.

"There is no longer any need for a Snow Emperor."

Xue Shilou looked defeated as if a young person had just lost their job.

He had just awakened his bloodline and passed three trials to become the heir to the Snow Emperor.

What do you mean there’s no more Snow Emperor?

Uncle Murong noticed the martial arts hall’s roof, clearly remembering that a hole had been punched through it during the battle, but now it wasn’t there.

And the martial arts hall itself.

"I feel like the martial arts hall is different from before."

Chapter 1192: 1191: Elder Brother Dai, We Have Brought Back the Concerned Party (Seeking Monthly Votes)

Tiger Roar Martial Arts Hall was built by Uncle Murong himself; he was intimately familiar with every brick and tile.

Even though the Martial Arts Hall looked exactly the same as before, something just didn’t feel right.

Never mind, he thought, Uncle Murong had reached the Golden Core Stage thanks to his carefree nature—if something didn’t make sense, he wouldn’t dwell on it. There was no point in torturing oneself.

"Uncle, Old Meng and I have been talking, and we think it’s about time we left," Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou came to bid farewell to Uncle Murong.

Although they would have liked to continue teaching at the hall, too much had happened in Muxue City, and it was only proper to return to their sect to report. Moreover, only these brothers fully knew the details of the incidents.

Besides, now that the people of Muxue City were aware of the brothers’ identities, staying would only bring trouble to Uncle Murong.

"Ah, you’re leaving?" Uncle Murong said, feeling melancholy. He knew about Lu Yang and his companion’s real identities, but what left the deepest impression on him were the instructors Little Lu and Little Meng.

"We’ll come back to visit you."

Uncle Murong shook his head with a wry smile, "What’s there to visit me for? You two are busy people. It’s the thought that counts."

He could already imagine the students’ disappointed faces after the New Year when they found out their teachers were gone. He would have to find a way to console them then.

Finding a teacher on the same level would be difficult, but if no one could beat him, he could teach too.

What Uncle Murong didn’t know was that Lu Yang, Meng Jingzhou, and Xue Shilou had entered the students’ dreams at night, bidding farewell to each one, telling them to keep training hard, and that what their instructors had taught was enough to last them a lifetime.

The students cried in their sleep and woke up with puffy eyes, realizing it hadn’t been a dream.

The four youngsters—Gong Che, Shi Yi, Fang Zhige, and Su Yuan—were the most mature. When they learned that Mr. Lu was actually Lu Yang, they had anticipated this outcome, yet when it came to pass, they still found it difficult to accept.

"The Snow Emperor is no longer with us, but you can still be considered my successor. Will you follow me in cultivation?" The Snow Emperor asked earnestly. It had been eighty thousand years since he found someone to pass on his mantle. Now, with Xue Shilou as a promising disciple, it was time to nurture him well.

"I am willing!" Xue Shilou kowtowed three times and offered the Snow Emperor tea, bringing laughter to the venerable man.

Lu Yang remembered something and headed to the backyard. He found Old Ma standing straight, packing away dried herbs. Once they left the Extreme Northern Lands, they wouldn’t be able to find such herbs again.

It wasn’t Old Ma that Lu Yang was reminded of, but rather the tree by his room’s door.

Under the tree, he found the body of Yang Kongdong, which was being gnawed—or perhaps digested—by the tree’s roots.

Lu Yang was startled. When had the tree become so malevolent?

He had only fed it the corpses of a Foundation Establishment Stage Luo Shi and a Divinity Transformation Stage Carefree Guest.

"The lingering obsession of Leng Ningxiang must have attached to the tree after it dissipated," Fairy Eternity analyzed. "In a way, she and Yang Kongdong are together forever now."

"It might seem sinister, but there shouldn’t be any issues once Yang Kongdong’s body is consumed."

The more professional Controller also came to analyze, "This tree could be considered as having gained enlightenment, or one might say it’s a form of reincarnation, unrelated to its past life."

"When the tree truly develops spiritual wisdom, it will be a completely new being."

Lu Yang roughly understood, but he was more concerned about another issue: "Is there a danger to the Martial Arts Hall?"

The Controller shook his head, "No danger. If the hall takes good care of this tree, it can protect the hall until the tree grows legs and transforms."

"That’s good then."

...

"An earthquake in the Extreme Northern Lands?" Dai Bufan couldn’t make heads nor tails of the intelligence just received.

Due to its vastness with sparse population and relative isolation, he seldom paid attention to the happenings in the Extreme Northern Lands.

Even so, he knew that earthquakes had never occurred there before.

"Elder Brother Dai, we’re back!"

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, the two heroes, triumphantly returned, having been rushed back by Old Ma, who pulled both men and a cart full of immortals frantically back to report.

Although their gains from the Extreme Northern Lands were significant, they were still disciples of the Seek Dao Sect, and Lu Yang was a junior brother under Dai Bufan. Any achievements they had made outside had to be reported back to Dai Bufan.

"You two are back from the Extreme Northern Lands?"

Dai Bufan was surprised and, seeing the joyous smiles on Lu Yang and his companion, he was reminded of how Junior Sister Gan Tian looked when she had returned from the Demon Territory, a sign that her cultivation had been fruitful and she had made a big harvest in the Demon Territory.

"It seems like you two have made quite a significant harvest in the Extreme Northern Lands?"

"Elder Brother Dai, you’re as perceptive as a deity!" Lu Yang gave a thumbs-up, genuinely impressed.

"Tell me, what did you two do in the Extreme Northern Lands?"

Listening to others’ worldly experiences could aid one’s own cultivation – a lesson Dai Bufan had learned during his Void Refinement days, and it was still applicable now.

"Elder Brother Dai, as you know, we’ve always advocated for cultivating discreetly and ’Red Dust Mortality,’ so Old Meng and I chose to teach fist techniques to students at a martial arts hall as teachers..."

"The Martial Arts Hall’s master’s cultivation level wasn’t high, just at the early stage of Golden Core. He didn’t know our identities."

"The students were all ordinary people, but of course, there were a few exceptions..."

Dai Bufan nodded frequently as he listened; it sounded credible. The Eldest Senior Sister had said that Junior Brother Lu went to the Extreme Northern Lands to temper himself, but it seems that Eldest Senior Sister could also make mistakes in her judgment – Junior Brother Lu went there to live a mortal life.

"And then the Ruthless Sect’s leader, Yang Kongdong, started fighting with Sect Leader Mu and Chi Sect Leader..."

Dai Bufan was just about to nod again when he abruptly stopped, leaning forward eagerly, staring hard at the two brothers.

"What did you just say?!"

Weren’t they just talking about holding classes? How did it jump to the Ruthless Sect’s leader?

"We didn’t know," Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou responded innocently, as they truly didn’t understand how Yang Kongdong ended up fighting with Mu Baiyi.

"Perhaps it’s an internal strife within the demonic sect?" Meng Jingzhou speculated.

That was precisely the reason they came to report their experience in the Extreme Northern Lands to Dai Bufan. As disciples of the Righteous Path facing a major event like internal conflict within a demonic sect, they couldn’t keep it to themselves.

"Keep going."

"...So Old Lu and I engaged Yang Kongdong in battle, then my cultivation level dropped, and Old Lu, reaching the peak of Pass Tribulation, continued the fight against Yang Kongdong."

"...The Controller from the Great Qian Dynasty started a battle with Immortal Jiuchong that literally shattered heaven and earth."

Dai Bufan pondered, feeling as though he had missed a piece of the story.

How did teaching mortals martial arts escalate to an Immortal Battle?

Others at the Void Refinement Stage either immersed in ’Red Dust Mortality’ or lived by ’War Feeds War,’ yet it seems you two have chosen ’Red Dust Fuels War’?

"Junior Brother Lu defeated Yang Kongdong, but where is Yang Kongdong now?"

When this question was raised, Lu Yang felt a bit embarrassed, "Would you believe me if I said his body was eaten by a tree?"

"..."

Lu Yang clenched his fist, "I knew you wouldn’t believe it, Elder Brother, so we brought the person in question here."

"Let me tell you, it wasn’t easy to invite him, even Eldest Senior Sister had to make a move."

"The person in question?"

Lu Yang turned to open the door, ushering in Immortal Jiuchong.

"This is the Venerable Immortal of Ancient Times, Nine-layered Immortal Venerable!"

Immortal Jiuchong was a very polite person. He knew the Immortal World was vast and customs varied from place to place. Upon arriving at the Seek Dao Sect, he would follow their customs, and if he were to stay awhile, he would need to register.

He strode into the room, revealing a gentle smile, "You may call me Immortal Jiuchong... Eh, why did he faint?"

Chapter 1193: 1192: Venerable Immortal, Can My Spirit Root Still Be Saved?

"Nowadays, young people really can’t handle pressure, they get excited so easily." Immortal Jiuchong shook his head lightly, guessing why Dai Bufan had fainted.

"Back in Ancient Times when we were prominent figures, even though I was low-key, Ying Tian often used us to show off miracles, and I couldn’t decline. Everyone was extremely excited, showing utmost reverence, some even like your Senior Brother here, fainting from the excitement."

Lu Yangxin thought to himself, Are you saying you were low-key, like when you celebrated your own ascension by flaunting heavenly and earthly treasures right after the Immortal Ascension Tribulation ended?

Seeing Dai Bufan, Immortal Jiuchong recalled the glorious days of Ancient Times and couldn’t help showing a hint of a smile.

"Today proves I am fated with you and your Senior Brother, especially since he has Water Spirit Root, I can grant him a bit of fortune."

Immortal Jiuchong reached out and a glimmer of light entered the forehead of the unconscious Dai Bufan.

Water Spirit Root is among the Yin category within the range of the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit.

"When he wakes up, he will handle water attribute magic skills more proficiently."

Meng Jingzhou enviously looked at Elder Brother Dai, wishing for similar good fortune, and when he thought of his own Spirit Root, he immediately pleaded pitifully with Immortal Jiuchong.

"Venerable Immortal, is there any hope for my Spirit Root?"

"Your Spirit Root?"

At first, Immortal Jiuchong didn’t pay much attention, but after observing Meng Jingzhou carefully, he slightly turned his head and hesitated, not knowing exactly how to describe: "Your Spirit Root... is quite extreme."

"If I had known you in Ancient Times, I could have condensed the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit two days earlier."

Immortal Jiuchong was quite regretful. How had such a perfect subject not crossed his path in Ancient Times?

Meng Jingzhou: "..."

"Is there still a chance?" Lu Yang asked on behalf of his good friend.

Immortal Jiuchong pondered, never having encountered information like that of Meng Jingzhou before, "To say there’s a chance, indeed, there is."

"How can it be saved?"

"I can reverse Yin and Yang, transforming your Pure Yang Spirit Root into a Pure Yin Spirit Root..."

Meng Jingzhou was about to say that a Pure Yin Spirit Root wouldn’t be too bad when he heard Immortal Jiuchong continue:

"Since Spirit Root is closely related to the body, once transformed into a Pure Yin Spirit Root, your gender will also change."

"Personality change isn’t unbearable... Gender?"

Meng Jingzhou abruptly realized something was amiss mid-sentence.

"I’ve heard in history of male cultivators with Pure Yin Spirit Root, at most they were effeminate in nature, why does it require a gender change for me!"

"In history, there wasn’t a Pure Yang Spirit Root as extreme as yours, coupled with dual Pure Yang Golden Core and dual Pure Yang Nascent Soul, and the Yang Invigorating Nirvana True Fire, turning you into a Pure Yin Spirit Root would also be unprecedented."

"In our Ancient Times, what did we call this? Oh right, it’s called ’childbearing’."

Meng Jingzhou painfully pondered for a moment, feeling that his Pure Yang Spirit Root was also quite good; at least he wouldn’t need to suppress himself after ascension.

Seeing Dai Bufan would not wake up anytime soon, the three decided to leave the Task Hall. Before leaving, Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou kindly moved the fallen Dai Bufan onto a chair.

In this way, even if someone came looking for Dai Bufan, they would merely assume Dai Bufan had fallen asleep due to the burdens of Sect affairs. They wouldn’t suspect anything else.

When Dai Bufan woke up, he grimaced and rubbed the back of his head. What had happened before?

Oh right, he was listening to reports from Junior Brother Lu and Junior Brother Meng about the Extreme Northern Lands.

"Why do I feel like I faint every time after meeting those two?"

"Junior Brother Lu and Junior Brother Meng are so bizarre; should I go visit the Western Paradise Temple?"

However, Dai Bufan quickly dismissed the thought. Why bother visiting the Western Paradise Temple? Lu Yang had already ascended to immortality in the Buddha Country, which was as familiar to him as his own backyard.

"There’s also the matter of the Ruthless Sect. It’s rather troublesome. Although the Sect Leader had given orders, theoretically, there should be many followers of the Ruthless Sect in the Extreme Northern Lands. That won’t be easy to track down."

As Dai Bufan was about to drink some water to soothe his throat, he noticed the ripples in the teacup. Suddenly, he felt enlightened. The numerous magic skill problems he had faced before suddenly became clear at this moment, and even his cultivation level showed signs of instability, ready to break through at any moment.

"Strange, why has my perception suddenly improved so much?"

Great Xia, Imperial City, Luoshui Guards Headquarters.

Luoshui Guards were specifically responsible for resolving historical issues left by Great Qian, Great Yu, and others, as well as recapturing members of The Three Great Demon Sects.

Recently, they had shifted their focus onto the Ruthless Sect.

The Meng Family had captured many high-ranking members of the Ruthless Sect in the Buddha Country, including a Vice Sect Hierarch. Although these individuals didn’t know the whereabouts of the Sect Leader, the Luoshui Guards had many former constables who were adept at analyzing clues from the slightest hints.

A cross-shaped world map hung on the wall of the Luoshui Guards. Yu Qian, one of the nine authorities in Luoshui Guards and previously known as one of the four great marshals in the Imperial City, pointed confidently at a section of the map and said, "In summary, the Sect Leader of the Ruthless Sect is most likely hiding in the East Sea!"

"Old Yu’s point makes sense. With us in Great Xia searching so thoroughly, Sect Leader Yang definitely wouldn’t dare to hide in our Great Xia. The Buddha Country has already conducted a joint operation without any trace of Sect Leader Yang. Previously, the Demon Territory was chaotic enough for Sect Leader Yang to hide. But now that the Demon Nation is established, with all factions under the unformed rule of the Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit of the Demon Emperor, he has nowhere to hide."

"According to our previous analysis of the Ruthless Sect, they prefer to root themselves in areas teeming with life. The Extreme Northern Lands have few cities and sparse populations, while the East Sea, albeit home to the Dragon Clan, has never had a unified government and is riddled with administrative loopholes. It’s the perfect place for Sect Leader Yang to hide with his remnants!"

The speaker, Luo Changhe, ranked second among the Luoshui Guards’ Nine Palaces, was a powerful old Transcendance Tribulation Stage master, widely known in the city markets for solving major cases like the "Secret Party Rebellion" and "National Treasure Theft" single-handedly.

The other members of the Nine Palaces nodded in agreement, supporting both views. To completely resolve the issue with the Ruthless Sect, they combed through archives tirelessly, staying up all night with dark circles under their eyes, exhausted.

It is known that they were all Body Integration Realm and Transcendance Tribulation Stage masters. To wear them out to such an extent indicated the immense workload.

"His Majesty has arrived—"

Emperor Xia arrived at the Luoshui Guards Headquarters, and the Nine Palaces bowed deeply, "We greet Your Majesty."

They were somewhat puzzled, as His Majesty seldom visited the Luoshui Guards Headquarters. What brought him here today?

Emperor Xia noted the analysis drafts spread across the table. It seemed they were analyzing matters concerning the Ruthless Sect.

"The remnants of the Ruthless Sect are in the Extreme Northern Lands, including Sect Leader Yang. Even the Seek Dao Sect has already clashed with Sect Leader Yang."

"What!"

The Nine Palaces looked at each other, and Luo Changhe couldn’t help but ask, "How is that possible? With all the information we have, we’ve only tentatively concluded that the remnants of the Ruthless Sect are in the East Sea. How did the Seek Dao Sect find Sect Leader Yang!"

Emperor Xia sighed deeply, "They have Lu Yang of the Seek Dao Sect."

Chapter 1194: 1193: Captive Peak Turmoil

Emperor Xia recalled the intelligence reports from the Seek Dao Sect and found them all to be absurd.

The Luoshui Guards of the Nine Palaces were shrewd generals, analyzing the Ruthless Sect’s intelligence day and night for half a year. They had just roughly pinpointed Yang Kongdong’s location when Lu Yang had already eliminated him.

Was establishing the Luoshui Guards superfluous?

...

Seek Dao Sect, the entrance to Captive Peak.

The new prisoner stood at the entrance to Captive Peak, lost in thought. He lamented how his lifelong reputation had come to such a tragic end, imprisoned in jail, truly a pitiful and lamentable outcome.

...

"Old Duan, is the line in my cultivation technique saying that we, as cultivators, should maintain a pure heart and practice abstinence?" Mo Xiaochou, at the mid-stage of Spirit Transformation, asked Duan Qingshui, who had been captured with him by Lu Yang and imprisoned in Captive Peak. Among the powerful figures of Captive Peak, Mo Xiaochou’s cultivation level ranked last, and Duan Qingshui’s was the second to last. The two often exchanged cultivation insights.

During their incarceration at Captive Peak, although unable to practice cultivation, their experience and knowledge had grown exponentially.

Duan Qingshui glanced at the line that puzzled Mo Xiaochou, shook his head gently, and kindly suggested, "Not sure, why don’t you ask Blood Demon Venerable? He wrote that line, right?"

Blood Demon Venerable, the last National Master of the Great Yu Dynasty, in a place like Captive Peak where Transcendance Tribulation Stage cultivators abounded and Body Integration Realm cultivators were nothing more than dogs, could barely be considered an important figure.

Mo Xiaochou indeed had this intention, but then he heard the quiet discussions gradually turn into arguments.

"What’s wrong with speaking a few truths? Why should we be afraid of letting these Great Qian cultivators hear it? You all be the judges, in what way are they from Great Qian better than us from Great Yu?" Master of All Laws bellowed, initially just telling the surrounding prisoners about the decadence of Great Qian, but unexpectedly the Great Qian Dynasty prisoners overheard, and his companions pulled him back from speaking recklessly, provoking Master of All Laws’ dissatisfaction.

"Our Great Qian has far more Semi-Immortals than your Great Yu!" Si Lei God Lord shouted in anger. Master of All Laws looked down on the Great Qian Dynasty, and he looked down on the Great Yu Dynasty too. With the two dynasties’ rivalry transcending time and space, he believed that Great Qian Dynasty would undoubtedly be the ultimate victor.

"Semi-Immortal numbers?" Master of All Laws snorted disdainfully, "Proud of your Dao Fruit Outlines that are cobbled together by the Power of Faith? The kind of watered-down Semi-Immortals like you, I alone could take on two!"

"Arrogant!"

Hearing this, Si Chen God Lord became even angrier than Si Lei God Lord, "You’re just a lucky Semi-Immortal, thanks to your long-lived Immortal body. What’s there to show off about having a natural advantage? I, with my Five Spirits Root, reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, and it is my fortune to have been favored by Lord Controller, who bestowed upon me the Time Dao Fruit Outline."

"If you had only the Five Spirits Root, whether you could reach the Body Integration Realm would be debatable!"

"A Transcendance Tribulation Stage achieved through murder and plunder, how valuable is your success?" Wu Youdao, the Sixth Emperor of Dayu, and the second most talented of all Emperors, scoffed, "The resources spent on you would have been enough for me to raise three Pass Tribulation cultivators!"

"A waste of resources, a heinous crime against nature, no wonder the Great Qian Dynasty declines with each generation, to be replaced by our Great Yu."

Caught in the middle, Mo Xiaochou and Duan Qingshui didn’t dare to interject or even ask their question about cultivation technique at this moment; they were cultivators from Buddha Country, not belonging to one of the two major powers of Captive Peak.

In fact, despite its three hundred thousand years of history, Buddha Country hadn’t even produced an Immortal, at most only Semi-Immortals; they had no place in this debate.

"Just the best among dwarves, reaching the Transcendance Tribulation Stage makes you think you’re something, still presuming to represent Great Yu?" Nightmare Supreme, who ended the Great Qian Dynasty and lost to the combined efforts of Wu Yao and Guan Shanhai, sneered, his experiences leading him to conflict with the Great Yu Dynasty.

"Ha, you wiped out the Great Qian Dynasty and still have the nerve to stand on the side of Great Qian," laughed a Great Yu Pass Tribulation Cultivator, spilling the beans without mercy.

"Even a Pass Tribulation cultivator dares to chime in!" Struck where it hurt, Nightmare Supreme turned red with shame and anger. If they had been outside, a mere glance from him could have plunged the other into an endless nightmare, never to awaken, but within Captive Peak, neither Dao Fruit Outlines nor magic skills could be used.

Thinking of this, Nightmare Supreme angrily threw a slipper through the bars of the cage, striking the opponent’s face and leaving a clear imprint of the shoe.

The Pass Tribulation Cultivator from Great Yu first froze, then flew into a rage. Being at Captive Peak, what did a Semi-Immortal amount to? Besides, he had Guan Shanhai backing him.

"Hardy to hit me, brothers, pummel him!"

Soon enough, slippers went pattering towards Nightmare Supreme.

"Just because you’re from Great Yu, you all gang up? Let’s go together!" shouted Si Lei God Lord, and the Great Qian Cultivators, seeing Nightmare Supreme bullied, knew they couldn’t stand by. Nightmare Supreme had taken those hits while standing up for Great Qian!

Captive Peak descended into chaos, everyone from Semi-Immortals to those in the Body Integration Realm joined in.

Towards the end, the Great Qian side ran out of weapons. Si Lei God Lord had a stroke of genius and began throwing balled-up newspapers at the opposition.

Seek Dao Sect respected rights; they would issue newspapers to the prisoners at Captive Peak each time they were printed, allowing them to keep abreast of the outside world.

The Great Yu Cultivators followed suit, crunching up newspapers into balls, and both sides launched their paper projectiles amidst anger and verbal assaults.

For a moment, the air was filled with flying newspaper balls, as if in a snowball fight.

Suddenly, a newspaper ball hit Guan Shanhai, who was lying on his side resting. Both sides immediately fell silent, daring not to make another move.

They secretly cursed, wondering who was the blind fool that threw the newspaper ball in the direction of the Imperial Teacher, especially since those papers had been printed by Guan Shanhai himself.

Guan Shanhai sat up, his calm eyes blazing with waves of fury as he clenched the newspaper ball and spoke deliberately.

"Is this what newspapers are for?"

Guan Shanhai sat boldly, his tranquil gaze sweeping over the Great Qian and Great Yu Cultivators, and slowly uttered two words:

"Blatant folly."

Both sides trembled, keeping their heads down, afraid to meet Guan Shanhai’s eyes.

"What a laughable question, which is stronger, Great Qian or Great Yu? If Great Qian were powerful, how could they have been replaced by our Great Yu? Is such a foolish question worth discussing?"

The Great Yu side secretly rejoiced, for surely the National Master was standing with them.

But hearing this, the Great Qian side was hot with the urge to contest Guan Shanhai, arguing that Great Qian Cultivators were merely hiding to lessen the impact of the Power of Faith, without which there would have been no stage for Guan Shanhai and Wu Yao.

Especially Si Lei God Lord and The Controller, who had followed Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller in conquering the heavens, and held the deepest affection for Great Qian, they were the most eager to speak these words.

However, in the face of an Immortal, they harbored a deep-rooted fear, a psychological shadow left by Mid-heaven Emperor Lord. In the presence of Guan Shanhai, himself an Immortal, they could never stand tall; they had words, yet dared not speak.

"This is your room now," Yun Zhi’s voice broke the unusual atmosphere at Captive Peak, bringing in a new prisoner.

The Controller looked at his two trusted subordinates, Si Lei God Lord and Si Chen God Lord, not sure what to say.

They must have been hoping that he could rescue them, but he would have to disappoint them, as he himself was trapped and unable to escape.

Si Lei God Lord and Si Chen God Lord, seeing The Controller, had tears in their eyes.

"Lord Controller, we’ve been longing for you for ages, and you’ve finally arrived!"

Can’t you hope for something better for me?

Having Lord Controller’s support is like having a backbone, and Si Lei God Lord finally mustered the courage to retort to Guan Shanhai. He abruptly raised his head, looking down on Guan Shanhai with disdain.

The long-awaited arrival of Lord Controller also reached Captive Peak, and the Great Qian cultivators’ morale soared, staring down the Great Yu Cultivators with straightened spines.

Lord Controller fell into rare contemplation; he had thought about many possible reactions his old subordinates might have upon seeing him, but he never imagined it would be like this.

As if possessed.

Lord Controller gently shook his head; what grant Divine Monarchs or produce outlines of Dao Fruits, those were just old stories, and now they were all trapped. What was the use of arguing about this here?

Guan Shanhai sneered, "Just mass producing outlines of Dao Fruits, and that’s worth mentioning? My Imprinting Dao Fruit can produce outlines of Dao Fruits too, even without the need for the Power of Faith."

"Like it’s some kind of achievement."

"What did you say!" Lord Controller slapped his thigh in anger, pointing at Guan Shanhai. He could say granting Divine Monarchs was an old affair, but others couldn’t say that.

"What, did I speak wrongly?" As an Immortal, Guan Shanhai wasn’t afraid of Lord Controller.

"If the Power of Faith is so great, would you sit idly by in the Fairy Palace while watching Great Qian fall?"

"Founding a nation on the faith of a divine path, I see your Great Qian is nothing more."

Lord Controller spoke mockingly, "And what about your Great Yu? Establishing an Imperial Teacher regime, only the first two were somewhat useful. After that, every Imperial Teacher was entangled in palace intrigues with the Emperor. Was your demise any slower than ours?"

"In the end, our Great Qian had a wise ruler, Gods stood in great numbers, received offerings of incense, each with their own duties, and the nation was strong; the Demon Territory, East Sea, and the world came to pay homage. What right does Great Yu have to compare to us?"

Guan Shanhai slowly rose to his feet, his eyes deepening, as if he was back to the time when he first established the Great Yu Dynasty.

"Utterly ridiculous. Our Great Yu Dynasty ended the chaos of war in the Central Continent, deterred the small factions of the Demon Race from invading, conquered all opponents along the way, and they all willingly submitted to Great Yu. After generations of prosperity, my Great Yu reached its zenith, to the extent that even Great Xia referred to my era as the Great Yu Golden Age!"

"What use are historical evaluations? In the Cultivation World, power is respected."

Lord Controller drew a square map on the ground, representing the Central Continent, then drew a vertical line splitting the Central Continent into two halves, one side Great Qian, the other, Great Yu.

"My Enlightenment Tao Fruit enlightens everything in the world, Emperor Yu’s Devouring Dao Fruit can even consume others’ Dao Fruits. When Emperor Yu and I join forces from this direction offering tribute, can your Great Yu withstand us?"

Guan Shanhai refused to be outdone. His cell was too far from the Controller, making it too difficult to draw a map on the Controller’s chart, so he drew an identical map right in front of himself.

"What’s so difficult about stopping you? Wu Yao’s Swap Dao Fruit can directly swap the Emperor into the cosmos, making him unable to swallow even the northwest wind. Do you think you alone can withstand an attack from both me and Wu Yao?"

"Besides, we have the war generals of Great Yu, each of whom climbed out of a mountain of corpses and a sea of blood. They lead eighteen armies, splitting them into six groups to invade Great Qian en masse. Your so-called divine monarchs would be lucky not to break down in fear when faced with our generals!"

"Break down in fear? Let’s not go too far. On our side, we have Si Lei and Si Chen, who can control hours of Heavenly Tribulation with the immature Dao Fruit. Your eighteen armies would collapse upon a single touch from them!"

"General Yu, the foremost war general of Great Yu, possesses the immature Yin-Yang Dao Fruit, truly cultivated by himself, not some semi-immortals you fabricated in imitation of the Four Ancient Immortals!"

"With General Yu leading the army, defining Yin and Yang, what can you possibly do?"

"The Imperial Teacher speaks well! That’s exactly how we should fight. Let’s see what Great Qian can do about it!" The cultivators of Great Yu grew more excited as they listened and could not help but punch the air in approval.

"What are you being arrogant about? The Emperor has long returned. He has descended directly from the cosmos to your rear in Great Yu, creating a slaughtering spree and forming a pincer attack with Lord Controller!" shouted the cultivators of Great Qian in anger. They had been swallowing their pride at Captive Peak for a long time, and now they finally had a chance to raise their eyebrows and breathe freely; how could they willingly be suppressed by the forces of Great Yu again?

"All Emperor Yu has to do is teleport the Emperor away again when the time comes!"

"How could the Emperor possibly be defeated by the same maneuver again? If he gets teleported again, he could drag Wu Yao with him!"

The two sides argued back and forth, heatedly setting up sand tables on the ground, studying how to defeat each other and boost their nation’s prestige.

The Controller and Guan Shanhai glared at each other disagreeably, rolling up their sleeves and pointing out faults, with spittle flying and faces turning red with restraint. Were it not for the inappropriateness of the setting, they might have stripped to their waists and settled it with a real fight.

Yun Zhi quietly watched from the side; originally, she wanted to interrogate the Controller overnight. Seeing the Controller’s condition now, even if she used the old method and whipped him, she doubted she could extract anything. She decided to wait for them to calm down before torturing.

The Grandmaster of the children listened intently to the tactics and countermeasures used during the fight, jotting down any points of confusion to think through slowly later. This was precious experience that could not be sought elsewhere.

Under the national pincer strategy, Mo Xiaochou and Duan Qingshui felt fortunate to be cultivators of the Buddha Country, uninvolved in such large-scale battles.
