Buddha Country, Western Paradise Temple, Mahavira Hall.

Dai Bufan considered repeatedly and decided to try his luck at the Western Paradise Temple.

After all, Junior Brother Lu only had an Immortal Body in Buddha Country, not truly an Immortal, and Buddhism led by the Worldly Venerable should be able to suppress Junior Brother Lu.

The Mahavira Hall was the biggest reliance of the Western Paradise Temple, not a place just anyone could enter to worship.

Dai Bufan, who had long handled internal and external affairs for the Grand Elder in Seek Dao Sect and was well-regarded externally, ranked second in the sect. His stature naturally qualified him to enter the Mahavira Hall.

Dai Bufan respectfully lit three sticks of incense and kowtowed to Buddha.

"O Great Buddha, I have two junior brothers, one named Lu Yang and the other Meng Jingzhou. I suspect they have been tainted by evil spirits, as every outing results in a major incident, and although they always manage to turn danger into safety, I ultimately worry about them encountering mishaps and dying young."

"Please, Great Buddha, expel the evil spirits from them and keep them safe!"

On the Lotus Platform, Buddha opened his ancient eyes and looked helplessly at the earnestly kowtowing Dai Bufan.

Is this really something I can manage?

Forget lighting three sticks of incense; even if you set the entire Mahavira Hall on fire, I wouldn’t have the power to handle it.

Chapter 1196: 1195: The Second-in-Command Is So Awesome

"Buddha’s blessing!"

Dai Bufan kowtowed three times in a row, a clear sign of his sincerity.

Then he looked up and saw the three sticks of incense, which had already burned out, miraculously come back to life, their ashes reassembling into their original form.

"?"

Dai Bufan emerged from the Mahavira Hall, and the curious Ming Yu Abbot approached to ask, "Amitabha, how effective was Benefactor Dai’s prayer?"

Dai Bufan fell silent for a moment before slowly saying, "Buddha manifested himself."

"Oh?" Ming Yu Abbot’s surprise was genuine, not feigned.

Dai Bufan spread out his hands, revealing three incense sticks, intact and undamaged: "The Buddha gave the incense back to me."

Seek Dao Sect, Sky Gate Mountain.

"Look, don’t the fruits look more appetizing when they’re cut like this?"

"Although only the shape of the fruit has changed and not the flavor, it really reflects the Spirit Chef’s intent."

"Indeed, it does."

Atop Sky Gate Mountain, right beside the Soybean Dynasty Ancestral Temple, Yun Mengmeng and Qinghe were studying fruit plating.

Qinghe taught Yun Mengmeng everything she knew without reservation, and Yun Mengmeng, eager not to disappoint, studied with great diligence, giving her mentor Qinghe a strong sense of accomplishment.

Suddenly, Yun Mengmeng looked up keenly, as if spotting something, causing Qinghe to become curious, "What is it?"

"Second-in-command’s scent!"

With that, Yun Mengmeng dropped everything and dashed straight down to the mid-mountain slope.

Lu Yang, just about to ascend the mountain, looked up to see Yun Mengmeng charging towards him.

He was swiftly knocked over by her.

"Second-in-command, you’re back safe and sound! I was so worried!"

Yun Mengmeng lay atop Lu Yang, her big watery eyes gazing at him: "Tell me, why did you and the Chief Housekeeper just disappear from Buddha Country all of a sudden? We searched high and low and couldn’t find you."

"Later, Xiao Zhi told me you went to the Extreme Northern Lands to train. I said I wanted to go too, but Xiao Zhi wouldn’t let me!"

"Naughty Tao Zi, I was so mad I didn’t speak to her for days," pouted Yun Mengmeng, visibly annoyed.

Immortal Fairy floated out from the Spiritual Space, feigning a cough twice, and said, "Third Boss, the Immortal Lineage has a clear hierarchy. What sort of decorum is this, you hugging Little Yang like that?"

"Ah? Oh." Yun Mengmeng, not quite understanding how her actions related to hierarchy but knowing better than to question, got up from the ground. After all, whatever the Chief said was right, so it was best to listen.

Pink lotus petals fell from the sky in a fluttering cascade, heralding a divine and auspicious welcome—Qinghe’s unique way of greeting the Immortal Fairy.

"You’ve returned, my lord."

"Fairy, speaking of Senior Qinghe, if her true form is a lotus, why is she called Qinghe instead of Qinglian?" Lu Yang whispered.

This question had been in Lu Yang’s mind ever since he had learned about Qinghe’s true form back in Buddha Country, but it was temporarily put aside during his journey to the Extreme Northern Lands. Now that he saw Qinghe, he remembered again.

"As it turns out, that issue was due to me, Immortal," the Fairy admitted.

"Back when Immortal saved Little Lotus, I was quite young and couldn’t tell lotus from water lily, so I thought Qinghe was a lotus and named her Little Lotus. Later, when I figured it out and wanted to change her name, she refused to change it, so she has been called Little Lotus ever since."

"Little Lotus, it’s been so long," Immortal Jiuchong greeted with a smile, having seen many old acquaintances these past few days.

"Big Brother Jiuchong," Little Lotus smiled faintly. After a separation of 300,000 years, it truly had been a long time.

Lu Yang arrived at the peak of Sky Gate Mountain, surprised to find that the Ancestral Temple of the Great Bean Dynasty, originally built in Buddha Country, had been moved to Sky Gate Mountain.

"After the Chief Housekeeper and Second-in-command disappeared, Sister Ling, Sister Lianyi, and Sister Caiwei all wanted to move the temple to their territories, but they couldn’t persuade me and Little Lotus. We staunchly moved the temple back to our Sect!" Yun Mengmeng explained with a giggly smile.

"Second-in-command, you’ve been to the Extreme Northern Lands for such a long time, were there any fun stories?" Yun Mengmeng brought over a plate of fruit, eagerly asking, the shape of the Spirit Fruits made up on the spot.

Lu Yang ate a piece of Spirit Fruit shaped like the Green Peak Sword. He didn’t know what kind of Spirit Fruit it was but it tasted pretty good.

"The Extreme Northern Lands are chilly and rather dull, not much fun there. I did meet a few interesting students, though," Lu Yang gestured dismissively. The Extreme Northern Lands, apart from ice blocks, had nothing more interesting than Buddha Country, so he wouldn’t recommend Yun Mengmeng go there for fun.

"But there are many good people in the Extreme Northern Lands. Our Martial Arts Hall’s door broke twice and was replaced for free."

"There’s also someone called the Carefree Guest in the Extreme Northern Lands, who committed many evils. I guess he thought he had done too much wrong and committed suicide. Guess that makes him half a good person."

"And then the Ruthless Sect came knocking. I have no idea why they would come to me. It’s truly a plague on the pond. What do they want with me, a Red Dust Mortality who teaches?"

"Right, the Ruthless Sect is really hateful!" Yun Mengmeng indignantly spoke up for Lu Yang. Nothing had happened while they were in Buddha Country.

"Even though Yang Kongdong is quite strong, a Semi-Immortal, he is still not as strong as me. I took him down in a few moves," Lu Yang said proudly. Defeating a Semi-Immortal in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage was a first throughout history. Once he returned, he would definitely publicize his heroic deed widely in newspapers.

Interviewing himself wasn’t ideal, it lacked credibility, so he’d have Old Meng from the audience interview him.

"Second-in-command is so impressive, what happened next?" Yun Mengmeng listened, starry-eyed. Every time the Second-in-command did something she couldn’t, proving once again why he was the Second-in-command.

"Then the Main Priest of the Great Qian Dynasty called by Yang Kongdong before he died, luckily Immortal Jiuchong stepped in at the crucial moment, and I was spared."

Immortal Jiuchong revealed a satisfied smile. Little Friend Lu knew how to talk. Although the details weren’t quite right, it was close enough.

"Immortal Jiuchong overcame the Controller and then took the initiative to spar with Eldest Senior Sister, who gladly accepted."

"At the beginning of the fight, even the Martial Arts Hall was damaged. After the battle, Immortal Jiuchong kindly offered to repair the hall, truly a model of virtue."

Immortal Jiuchong listened more comfortably, for amongst the birth conditions of the Five Ancient Immortals, he was indeed the most polite.

Yingtian Immortal had escaped from burial goods, Fairy Eternity was from a small tribe, Time Immortal was an enlightened piece of wood, and Qilin Immortal was a natural spirit. Only he was born in the great tribe of the Lianshan Clan of the Human race.

"Did Xiao Zhi win?" Yun Mengmeng was most concerned about the outcome of the fight.

Lu Yang was about to embellish the result a bit on behalf of Immortal Jiuchong, but he heard Jiuchong smack his thigh, his tone filled with regret, "Oh, if Daoist Yun Zhi and the Controller hadn’t joined forces against me, how could I have... Never mind, let’s not talk about it anymore."

Lu Yang: "..."

You’d better say the same when Eldest Senior Sister comes back.

Little Lotus was quite happy to see Immortal Jiuchong, but then she suddenly recalled a problem.

It seems Yingtian Immortal and Time Immortal were both found by Lu Yang.

Ashy Bean had been searching for 300,000 years and hadn’t found a single one; how did Lu Yang manage to find them all in just a few years?

If Lu Yang had followed Ashy Bean back then, wouldn’t Ashy Bean have achieved Transcendence much sooner?

Thank goodness Lu Yang stayed with the adults.

Wait a second, why was she thinking this way?!

Chapter 1197: 1196 Realm Almost the Same

"Little Lu is back?"

While Lu Yang was vividly describing the culture and people of the Extreme Northern Lands with Yun Mengmeng, a hearty laughter reached his ears.

Lu Yang, hearing this, hurriedly got up to salute, "I’ve seen Ancestor Guiyuan and Vast Sea Ancestral Master."

"What brings the two ancestors to my place?" Lu Yang asked.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord laughed heartily, slapping Lu Yang’s shoulder, his mouth unable to close from so much laughter, "We heard from Xiao Zhi that the leader of the Ruthless Sect, Yang Kongdong, is a Semi-Immortal, and yet you beat him."

"Impressive, really impressive!"

"I had an eye on you, my boy, but I didn’t expect your progress to be this great."

Celestial Master Guiyuan also wore a beaming smile, proud of such strong juniors, which pleased him as an ancestor.

Lu Yang felt somewhat embarrassed facing his elders, "It was all luck."

"Ah, how can defeating a Semi-Immortal be called luck? I’ve sparred with Chasing Moon True Person and know how difficult the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo is to deal with."

"Moreover, even without the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo, Yang Kongdong is genuinely an Ancient Tribulation Peak Cultivator. You defeating him definitely can’t be called luck!"

"Right? Our second-in-command is really powerful," Yun Mengmeng said puffing up her chest, sharing in the glory, more delighted than Lu Yang himself that he had defeated Yang Kongdong.

"Come, let’s have a match," Vast Sea Daoist Lord said eagerly.

Lu Yang never expected the Vast Sea Ancestral Master to make such a request, "Ah, how could I possibly make a move against you, Ancestral Master?"

"It’s not about making a move, just an exchange," Vast Sea Daoist Lord laughed, having heard that Lu Yang had grown so strong, he was itching for a challenge, knowing that in a few years, he might not be able to win.

Celestial Master Guiyuan sided with Vast Sea Daoist Lord, "Exactly, why can’t you spar with an ancestor? Didn’t your Eldest Senior Sister fight both of us?"

"... Alright then."

Having said that, Lu Yang couldn’t refuse.

After all, it was only an exchange, there was no need to find another place, they could just clear a space on the top of Sky Gate Mountain.

For instance, by moving the Soybean Dynasty Ancestor Temple aside.

Lu Yang and Vast Sea Daoist Lord agreed on the boundaries of the fight, to not run outside of the circle, and then they took their positions.

Lu Yang took two deep breaths, drew out the Green Peak Sword, borrowed strength from Buddha Country. In a breath, his realm soared, and when he opened his eyes again, his gaze was sharp like a sword, startling Vast Sea Daoist Lord.

"I’ve long heard that you’re adept at a magic skill that borrows strength, seeing it today really is impressive, you’ve managed to elevate your realm to the peak of the Pass Tribulation Stage in one go," Vast Sea Daoist Lord exclaimed, having never seen such an amazing magic skill.

"You misunderstood, Ancestral Master, usually, Imitation Fist isn’t so potent," Lu Yang earnestly explained.

"What do you mean?"

"Imitation Fist can only borrow the maximum strength of my current realm. For example, if my fluctuation limit in the Void Refinement Stage touches the Tribulation Crossing, I can raise my realm to the Tribulation Crossing when using Imitation Fist."

"If I advance to the Body Integration Realm, then using Imitation Fist can only elevate my realm to the peak of the Integration Period. Pushing further, my body could not withstand it."

"I see," Vast Sea Daoist Lord nodded, "That’s quite impressive too."

"Come, you make the first move."

"I must apologize in advance!"

Facing Vast Sea Daoist Lord, Lu Yang found there was no need to hold back. He immediately executed One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand, spreading his Swordmanship Domain, with Sword Qi of Green Peak Sword filling the heavens and earth.

"Well done!"

Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s eyes lit up, the last time he felt this pressure was from Sword Tower Supreme.

Fighting with a master of Swordmanship was indeed interesting.

Sword Qi continuously burst from the ground, but Vast Sea Daoist Lord always managed to retreat before the Sword Qi emerged. He stopped walking back; retreating further would take him out of the circle.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord somersaulted in the air, treading the void, weaving through the Sword Qi with only a few streams managing to cut his skin.

He approached Lu Yang, his punch soaring like a dragon, resonating with the might of thousands of True Dragons roaring.

This was the True Dragon Fist, which Vast Sea Daoist Lord learned from the Long Clan during his youth while traveling in the East Sea. It was the highest fist technique of the Long Clan that existed even before Ao Ling was born.

Now, with Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s contact with Ao Ya, his understanding of True Dragon Fist had deepened. Few in the history of the Long Clan could reach his level of mastery.

Lu Yang flicked his wrist, transforming the Sword Qi into the likeness of Ao Ling to combat Vast Sea Daoist Lord, then executed Arhat Fist, imitating the Buddhist Dragon Subduing and Tiger Suppressing, momentarily overpowering Vast Sea Daoist Lord causing him to burst into laughter.

Celestial Master Guiyuan watched the battle, nodding approvingly at Lu Yang’s performance.

"Look at my junior, isn’t he doing well?" Celestial Master Guiyuan boasted to the onlookers.

"He is doing quite well." The bystanders nodded. To cultivate swordsmanship to such a realm, comparable even to Liu Ningxuan, creator of the Silent Destruction Sword Technique, it’s no wonder he defeated Yang Kongdong.

"My friend, you seem unfamiliar." Celestial Master Guiyuan had been focusing on Lu Yang and only now noticed a stranger on Sky Gate Mountain.

Celestial Master Guiyuan’s eyes were exceptionally discerning, instantly recognizing the stranger’s extraordinary aura as nearly equal to his own in terms of realm.

"Are you here visiting our sect?"

"I suppose so, an old friend told me Seek Dao Sect is pretty good, so I came to see for myself. It’s indeed an eye-opener, and I even get to meet an old friend after a long time," the stranger politely replied.

"Then you might stay for a while. We at Seek Dao Sect always welcome friends. If you encounter any difficulties here, feel free to seek me out."

"May I ask your esteemed name?" The stranger courteously clasped his fist, surprised by the claim, thinking Yun Zhi was the speaker for Seek Dao Sect.

"I am Celestial Master Guiyuan, who has condensed the Guiyi Tao Fruit outline and studied the Unity Theory extensively."

"We are quite similar then; my research involves all things being composed of Yin and Yang attributes, interdependent and counteracting each other, which naturally includes the theory of Yin and Yang unity."

"Oh? Then we indeed share a bond, and we must definitely converse more in the future." Celestial Master Guiyuan took another long look at the stranger, who studied the rare Unity Theory.

"Most certainly." The stranger smiled broadly with his hands behind his back.

"Speaking of which, I have a friend called Mister Tiankui, founder of the Five Elements Sect, who believes all things are composed of the Five Elements attributes, interacting in generating and overcoming cycles, and has condensed the Five Elements Dao Fruit outline."

"I’ve discussed Unity Theory with him a few times and gained some insights. If you’re interested, I could introduce you; maybe you two will also find common topics."

"The founder of the Five Elements Sect? That sounds rather interesting," the stranger smiled intriguingly, fascinated that some believed all things were constituted by Five Elements attributes.

"By the way, we’ve been talking for so long, but I still don’t know your name?"

"I am Immortal Jiuchong, having condensed the Yin-Yang Dao Fruit."

Chapter 1198: 1197: The Fat Water Does Not Flow to Outsiders’ Fields

Five Elements Sect, a place of Enlightenment.

Mister Tiankui stood facing the intricate chessboard, lost in deep contemplation. The more he studied the black and white pieces, the more he sensed the endless mysteries and wonders of the world embodied within the game.

"I can’t figure it out... Achoo!"

Suddenly, Mister Tiankui sneezed.

"Strange, is an old friend thinking of me?"

"Could it really be because I’m so well-liked?"

...

"I had heard long ago that Mister Tiankui founded the Five Elements Sect, advocating that all things are comprised of the Five Elements. I thought about whether I should meet him," Immortal Jiuchong, who had heard of Mister Tiankui over a hundred thousand years ago in the Extreme Northern Lands, said with a smile. It would be rude to just approach him without an introduction, and it would be much more convenient with Celestial Master Guiyuan there to introduce him.

Celestial Master Guiyuan quietly stepped back and said, "Well, should I first get him some insurance? I heard it’s popular among young people these days."

He felt that Mister Tiankui might not be very keen on communicating with Immortal Jiuchong.

"It’s just a joke, Celestial Master Guiyuan. I am a scholar, and a gentleman does not resort to violence."

Meanwhile, Yun Mengmeng clenched her fist tightly, cheering for Lu Yang silently as she worried that a loud voice might distract the Second-in-command during the battle.

Qinghe bit on the tip of her brush, pondering how to write the history of the Great Bean Dynasty, particularly the significant events that occurred when the adults were in the Extreme Northern Lands, which were indeed vivid Chapters in the dynasty’s history.

She was currently writing about Emperor Dou commanding Lu Yang to battle the Controller.

"Maybe I should title this part ’The Two Emperors Battle the Controller?’"

"No, that makes the Controller seem too strong."

"It’s a pity. If only the adult could regain his Immortal Body and defeat the Controller straightforwardly, this history would be much easier to write."

Seeing Yun Mengmeng quietly cheering, Qinghe suggested, "Actually, Mengmeng, you could prepare some Spirit Fruit or juice it."

When Yun Mengmeng looked puzzled, Qinghe further explained, "After Lu Zhuguo finishes fighting, he’ll surely be hungry and thirsty. The most needed thing will be food and drink, and your preparation would come in handy."

These were insights Qinghe had gained while serving Immortal Fairy Ao Ling in ancient times.

"I see!" With the explanation made clear, Yun Mengmeng hastened to prepare.

The battle between Lu Yang and Vast Sea Daoist Lord entered a tense stage. The True Dragon Fist was no match for Ao Ling Sword Qi and Arhat Fist, and he was forced to retreat step by step. The Vast Sea Daoist Lord used other magic skills that Lu Yang also managed to break through. Finally, in desperation, he shamelessly activated the Embryonic Spatial Dao Fruit.

He set up a spatial barrier around him, which absorbed any approaching Sword Qi, letting it fly out from another end of the barrier, ensuring the Sword Qi never harmed him.

However, in this state, the Vast Sea Daoist Lord couldn’t harm Lu Yang either.

The Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s forte was absorbing attacks and transferring them back to the attacker, but since he was absorbing Lu Yang’s Sword Qi, it was impossible for Lu Yang’s own Sword Qi to harm him.

Activating the Dao Fruit Embryo exerted a minimal strain on mana, and Lu Yang’s strength originated from the Buddha Country Immortal Body. If they were to continue in this stalemate, they could fight for three days and nights without ending.

Faced with this situation, Lu Yang came up with a plan.

Activating the Dao Fruit Embryo required the Vast Sea Daoist Lord to keep his focus on the Dao Fruit Embryo. Lu Yang could use the Extinguish Immortal Sword Array to force the Vast Sea Daoist Lord to kneel, causing him to be distracted and lose focus, which would allow Lu Yang an opportunity to strike.

Reluctantly, Lu Yang raised his hand; such a tactic could only be imagined and dismissed, as this was not a fight to the death. How could he make the Vast Sea Daoist Lord kneel before him?

"I surrender."

Vast Sea Daoist Lord helplessly looked at Lu Yang and the endless Sword Qi surrounding him, "Don’t just say you surrender, withdraw the Sword Qi as well!"

Vast Sea Daoist Lord was forced to use the Embryonic Spatial Dao Fruit, otherwise, he would have been pierced into a hornet’s nest by Lu Yang long ago.

Once Lu Yang dispersed the Sword Qi, Vast Sea Daoist Lord dared to stop using the Embryonic Spatial Dao Fruit and landed.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord was thankful he had chosen to spar with Lu Yang now, for in another two years, he truly would have had no chance of winning.

"Little Lu, you are very impressive. If I were only at the Transcendence Tribulation Stage, I definitely couldn’t have beaten you."

When Vast Sea Daoist Lord was revitalizing the Seek Dao Sect, he too conquered the world with his fists. Every household in the Transcendence Tribulation Stage had fought him. This isn’t modesty but a fact; he was truly an expert at that level.

It’s just that he still fell short compared to Lu Yang.

Lu Yang’s swordsmanship, magic skills, and combat experience were all sufficient. What he lacked now was realm, Vast Sea Daoist Lord thought secretly.

"Second-in-command fought really well." Yun Mengmeng brought over a tray, which not only had Spirit Fruit and juice but also thoughtfully included a warm towel for Lu Yang to freshen up with.

Lu Yang released the Imitation Fist, took a deep breath, and felt utterly exhausted.

Sparring with Vast Sea Daoist Lord was not an easy task; it posed a huge strain on Spiritual Power. Once he released the Imitation Fist, fatigue surged through him, and he nearly couldn’t even stand.

Fortunately, there was the Spirit Fruit prepared by Yun Mengmeng.

"Delicious." Lu Yang ate two slices of Spirit Fruit shaped like the Green Peak Sword. He didn’t know what variety this Spirit Fruit was, but its effects were immediate and significantly rejuvenated his spirit.

"What kind of Spirit Fruit is this?" Lu Yang had wanted to inquire about the origin of the Spirit Fruit before the fight.

"This is the Immortal Spirit Fruit from our Immortal Lineage, it restores energy and physical strength when eaten." Now, Yun Mengmeng had mastered the cultivation of Immortal Spirit Fruit.

"..."

Lu Yang suddenly lost his appetite for the Spirit Fruit; he remembered that Immortal Spirit Fruit could explode.

But indeed, it was quite delicious.

He decided to keep eating.

After Celestial Master Guiyuan was forced to take the Immortal Jiuchong to the Five Elements Sect, and Vast Sea Daoist Lord returned to the Dragon Palace of the East Sea, Lu Yang regained some strength and got up from the ground.

"It’s time to get to business."

...

Meng Jingzhou gritted his teeth as he stared at his good friend, "So, you fought with the Vast Sea Ancestral Master and then ran straight here?"

"You didn’t rest at all, did you?"

"Old Meng, you know that only you can handle the interview," Lu Yang earnestly persuaded Meng Jingzhou for his own fame.

"Just look at the people involved; it can’t be the Eldest Senior Sister or the Immortal Jiuchong who interview me."

"Senior Ma is low-key and wouldn’t interview me either."

"The Snow Emperor and Xue Shilou are in the Extreme Northern Lands; no one can find them, and the Snow Emperor has already retired."

"The guard Little Huo isn’t at the required level."

"So counting up and down, you’re the only one left."

"Besides, talents like us shouldn’t be wasted; my victory over Yang Kongdong is a historically significant event. The newspapers will be preserved, and future generations reading them will find not only my name but yours too!"

Meng Jingzhou considered whether to use his financial resources to simply buy up all the copies of this newspaper issue.

Chapter 1199: 1198: Printing Newspapers? I Can Help Too

Just as Lu Yang had said, other than Meng Jingzhou, there was no more suitable person for the interview. Seeing Lu Yang shamelessly pleading, Meng Jingzhou had no choice but to agree.

Lu Yang didn’t even have to bother about the headline; Meng Jingzhou had already thought of one for him: "Young Hero Lu Red Dust Mortality Slays City Lord."

"So, Fellow Daoist Lu Yang, could you tell how you fought against Master Yang Kongdong of the Ruthless Sect?" Meng Jingzhou quickly assumed the role of the interviewer and commenced the interview about Lu Yang’s experience at that time.

"It was all coincidental. At the time, I was undergoing Red Dust Mortality in Muxue City, situated in the Extreme Northern Lands. Little did I know, Yang Kongdong had already possessed the City Lord of Muxue City. To prevent Yang Kongdong from slaughtering the people of Muxue City, I was forced to stand up. Fortunately, during the battle, my realm fluctuated to the Peak of Tribulation Crossing, which gave me the strength to fight Yang Kongdong. After a fierce battle, I managed to slay him."

As Lu Yang spoke, he gestured to recreate the scene as best as he could. This was a grand event, and he wanted to describe it properly.

Meng Jingzhou nodded while jotting down Lu Yang’s words: Lu Yang purely attributed his victory over Yang Kongdong to coincidence.

"Yang Kongdong was a formidable enemy; he had condensed an outline of the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit, which could alter people’s emotions and thus affect the outcome of the battle. There was no direct way to counteract such an outline of Dao Fruit; one could only endure it. Luckily, I possessed a Buddha Heart, which protected me from the influence of the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Outline."

Meng Jingzhou once again wrote down Lu Yang’s words: Lu Yang was dedicated to Buddha, unswayed by emotions.

"...No one expected that Yang Kongdong’s true identity was that of a Semi-Immortal from Ancient Times, bearing the inheritance of various ancient great forces. During his cultivation, he made countless beauties his confidants. Unfortunately, in the end, these beloved beauties fought over Yang Kongdong, and tore him apart, leading to his slumber from Ancient Times until today."

Meng Jingzhou noted: Too many beloved confidantes led to Yang Kongdong’s gruesome dismemberment. This made Meng reflect on whether having too many beauties is truly not a good thing. It is often said that beauties are a source of calamity; as a cultivator, one should minimize contact with the opposite sex, focus on cultivation, and prevent such tragedies from happening again...

"Have you got everything down?"

"I’ve got everything."

"Let me see."

Lu Yang craned his neck to look, feeling as if Meng had finished writing too quickly after his long speech.

"There’s nothing worth seeing here," said Meng Jingzhou, quickly hiding the interview draft behind him.

Lu Yang instinctively sensed something was amiss and circled around to Meng Jingzhou’s back: "I’ll just have a quick look."

"Hey, Old Lu, why do you just grab it like that?"

Then Lu Yang saw Meng Jingzhou’s interview notes.

"..."

Both of them fell silent simultaneously.

"Is this how you do interviews!?"

"I just condensed it."

Lu Yang felt fortunate that he had seen Meng’s draft beforehand; it would have been disastrous to publish it in the newspaper.

Behind the scenes, Meng Jingzhou secretly sighed; indeed, he couldn’t get away with any tricks in front of Old Lu.

Under Lu Yang’s supervision, a correct and objective interview draft emerged, and after adding some fun facts and trivia from the Cultivation World, this issue of Cultivation Monthly was ready to be printed.

When Lu Yang brought the finalized Cultivation Monthly to Captive Peak, he heard a passionate discussion inside.

"I’m telling you, our Almighty Emperor’s Devouring Dao Fruit can devour all things, and the more he devours, the stronger he becomes. If he wishes, he can directly devour our world and become an unprecedentedly powerful being, with just a blink of his eye turning Great Yu into ashes!"

"Then, not to mention Great Yu, even Great Xia wouldn’t stand a chance against our Emperor. Even the Four Ancient Immortals would tremble before him!"

"Devouring everything counts for nothing; our Emperor Yu can swap Dao Fruits for anything and even swap cultivation levels," a voice boomed. "No matter how powerful the Emperor becomes, he can take the Emperor’s cultivation level and assimilate it into himself. What good is the Emperor’s strength then, when in the end, it only benefits our Emperor Yu!"

"What of the Four Ancient Immortals, Great Xia’s Twin Immortals, and Yun Zhi of the Seek Dao Sect? Faced with our Emperor Yu, they only have escape as an option!"

Lu Yang stood silently at the doorway, pondering whether life at Captive Peak was too dull, as the prisoners had begun to talk nonsense.

"Cough."

Lu Yang coughed dryly, and the once heated arguments and mutual finger-pointing and cursing that filled Captive Peak came to an abrupt halt. Only the echoing footsteps of Lu Yang could be heard throughout the silent space.

The prisoners nervously watched Lu Yang, fearing he might bring back another new prisoner.

Fortunately, this time Lu Yang had only come to find Guan Shanhai.

"Here’s the latest issue of the newspaper, make more copies," Lu Yang handed several newspapers to Guan Shanhai.

"Alright."

Seeing this, the Great Yu Cultivators looked provokingly at the Great Qian Cultivators, their spirits high. See, even Lu Yang has to seek help from our Imperial Teacher, do you Great Qian have this capability?

"Wait a moment." The always silent Controller suddenly spoke, his voice deep as though he had made some sort of decision.

Lu Yang looked puzzled at the Controller, wondering what was going on.

"Printing newspapers, I can help too."

The Controller had known about the existence of newspapers since his time in the Fairy Palace, but he didn’t know Guan Shanhai was the one who printed them.

The blatant arrogance of the Great Yu power was one reason why Lu Yang had asked Guan Shanhai to print newspapers.

"I see your newspapers have an Array Method for broadcasting; it must be intended for those who can’t read. It’s a good idea, but it’s not very handy for the common folks."

Lu Yang nodded, gesturing for the Controller to continue.

"My Enlightenment Tao Fruit can enlighten newspapers. They won’t need the broadcasting Array Method to produce sound, and after my enlightenment, the newspapers will possess a degree of spiritual wisdom. Should a commoner want to hear a specific part of the newspaper, they merely need to speak to it, and the paper will recite it automatically!"

"If commoners wish to learn to read, the enlightened newspapers can also teach them how!"

Lu Yang hissed in astonishment, seeing this as a potential idea. If feasible, the literacy rate in Great Xia, or perhaps the entire world, would dramatically increase.

Lu Yang took out a copy of the newspaper Guan Shanhai had printed and let the Controller enlighten it. Indeed, as the Controller described, it became more user-friendly.

Moreover, Enlightenment was an ability of the Controller’s Dao Fruit, which even Guan Shanhai couldn’t replicate.

In other words, to enlighten all the newspapers, the power of the Controller would be necessary.

Guan Shanhai remained silent throughout, merely looking at the Controller through the bars of the cage.

Truly, a formidable foe, the Main Priest of the Great Qian Dynasty!

"Grandmaster, do you think life on Captive Peak might be a bit monotonous? Should we allow the prisoners some space for free activity?" Lu Yang quietly inquired of the young Grandmaster, the master of Captive Peak.

The young Grandmaster too felt that continuing this way was not ideal and nodded, following Lu Yang’s thought:

"Do you mean to suggest we allocate a section of land for the Controller and Guan Shanhai to lead their respective forces against each other in real combat, to see whether Great Yu or Great Qian is stronger?"

"...Grandmaster, you’re being a bit extreme."

Chapter 1200: 1199: Something in the Sun

Letting the Controller and Guan Shanhai truly fight would definitely be unacceptable, the influence of a battle between two Immortals can be inferred from the fight between Immortal Jiuchong and the Controller, where the Extreme Northern Lands almost reverted to a sky full of stars.

The same goes for the Controller and Guan Shanhai. If these two Immortals were to fight, the entire Seek Dao Sect would be affected... oh, that’s not right, before it could be affected, Yun Zhi would have sent them back.

However, suppressing the cultivation levels of prisoners and allowing them to move freely is a viable idea.

Take, for example, the Restraining Light Treasure Box of the Laurel Fairy Palace. Any cultivator within the box has their realm suppressed to the same level, and Fairy Eternity once possessed herself, using the Restraining Light Treasure Box to challenge the Sect Leaders of the Five Great Immortal Sects.

To suppress an Immortal, the Restraining Light Treasure Box definitely doesn’t have such power, but it shouldn’t be a problem for Yingtian Immortal to use the principle of the Restraining Light Treasure Box to refine a higher-level Immortal Artifact.

After all, he is the ancestor of refining; he cannot possibly be incapable of refining anything.

"However, in order to refine an Immortal Artifact that can suppress an Immortal’s realm, quite a bit of Immortal Gold will likely be needed," Lu Yang pondered. Although he was not the Sect Leader, as a member of the Seek Dao Sect, he had to consider the interests of the Sect.

Is the cost of transforming Captive Peak a bit too much?

As Lu Yang left Captive Peak, he happen to encounter Yun Zhi walking this way.

"Eldest Senior Sister, do you have business at Captive Peak?"

"I came to find you," Yun Zhi said. "The Controller has given the method to unseal the Sun."

After learning of the method to unseal the Sun, Yun Zhi wanted to take Lu Yang for a spin on the Sun, she came out from Captive Peak, went to Sky Gate Mountain, heard that Lu Yang was looking for Meng Jingzhou, found Meng Jingzhou, then heard Lu Yang went to Captive Peak to print newspapers.

After going around in circles, the two finally met at Captive Peak.

"Can the Sun’s seal finally be broken?" Lu Yang’s spirit was lifted. The Sun had two seals in total, the first laid by the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, the second by Emperor Wu Yao of Yu. With Guan Shanhai’s capture, breaking Wu Yao’s seal would cause the power of the Sun and Lunar to surge.

The most notable example of the effect on Lu Yang’s side was Meng Jingzhou, whose Pure Yang Constitution nearly broke due to the surge of the Sun’s power.

To break the seal laid by the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and liberate the power of Sun and Lunar, cultivators practicing the Yin Yang Technique welcomed a springtime of opportunity.

Of course, if the seal could not be broken, asking Immortal Jiuchong to increase the firepower of the Sun could also lead to a springtime for cultivators practicing the Yin Yang Technique.

"Shall we go?"

"I shall!" Lu Yang nodded eagerly. There was no reason not to go.

Yun Zhi nodded, flew ahead, and then, as if she remembered something, turned her head and said, "Also, Junior Brother, don’t always run around aimlessly."

"Ah? Oh." Lu Yang didn’t quite understand, was Eldest Senior Sister telling him not to walk around on the Sun at will?

Lu Yang performed the Tofu Venerable Celestial Form to elevate his realm to the peak of the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, where he could freely roam the universe, no longer needing Yun Zhi’s assistance.

Then Yun Zhi noticed that Lu Yang’s flight speed could not keep up with hers, so she simply formed a hand seal to drag Lu Yang along faster.

The two arrived at the Sun, and the power of the Supreme Yang hit them in the face. Lu Yang, who would once get scorched approaching the Sun, now feared not the Supreme Yang power on the surface of the Sun.

When Lu Yang wanted to get even closer, golden patterns emerged, circling the surface of the Sun, thwarting Lu Yang’s intentions.

Countless magnificent runes flickered like stars, gathered at Yun Zhi’s pale fingertips, the golden bell bracelet on her wrist jingling. Yun Zhi raised her hand, touching the golden pattern on the surface of the Sun.

Crack—

The golden pattern shattered, and the power of the Supreme Yang surged wildly, almost causing Lu Yang to tumble over.

Meanwhile, every cultivator in the Cultivation World felt the changes emanating from the Sun, with those who practiced Yin Yang Techniques experiencing the most pronounced sensations. Quite a few cultivators seized the opportunity to break through their bottlenecks.

But none of this was of concern to Yun Zhi.

"It’s time."

Yun Zhi spoke indifferently as she drew a vertical line in the air, slicing open a door on the surface of the Sun and revealing the landscape of Sun Cave Heaven.

Inside Sun Cave Heaven, majestic palaces stood tall, surrounded by an aura of immortality, glittering with gold and jade splendor as if all the marvelous treasures of the world had been used to decorate the palace. The number of Heaven and Earth Treasures within could be described as vast beyond measure, making Great Xia Palace seem insignificant by comparison.

"This is Great Qian’s Fairy Palace!"

Lu Yang suddenly realized. This was the very Fairy Palace spoken of by the cultivators of Great Qian, but they had been bound by a Restriction by the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, unable to disclose the location of the Fairy Palace!

Turns out, the Fairy Palace had been right above their heads all along!

This wasn’t just darkness beneath the lamp; it was like hiding inside the lamp itself.

"Yes, this is Great Qian’s Fairy Palace," Yun Zhi was not surprised, as The Controller had already made everything clear.

Seeing Lu Yang on alert, Yun Zhi shook her head, signaling him to lower his guard: "The Emperor Lord left long ago. When The Controller was battling with Immortal Jiuchong, he had already sensed my presence. Knowing his character, his first reaction would have been to retreat to the Fairy Palace and lead his followers to escape. Perhaps in their haste, they failed to take the Fairy Palace with them."

"There’s no one left in the Fairy Palace now."

Upon hearing this, Lu Yang felt a sense of regret but then realized that the Fairy Palace itself was a treasure.

Worried about the lack of Immortal Gold for the renovation of Captive Peak? Well, wasn’t that problem now solved?

The brother and sister entered the Fairy Palace, which was equally grand inside. Murals depicted various bizarre events: colossal giants plowing the earth with divine oxen, sages creating paintings with a flick of their brushes, adorning the night sky with stars, divine beings descending to eliminate demons and bring peace to the world, Emperor Yu of Great Yu seated at the center of heaven and earth, upholding the Sun and Moon...

As strangers entered the Fairy Palace, divine beings and demon beasts crawled out of the murals, baring teeth and claws ready to attack the newcomers.

This was the ability of The Controller’s Enlightenment Tao Fruit. Even an Immortal would suffer severe injuries if caught off guard by a single strike from the murals.

Yun Zhi, without even a glance, snapped her fingers, and the divine beings and demon beasts dissolved like wet paintings, blurring and fading until they vanished like a receding tide.

All this was but a trivial act for Yun Zhi, and she didn’t take it to heart as she explained the origin of the Fairy Palace.

"Initially, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller constructed the Fairy Palace within the Sun to mitigate the influence of the Power of Faith, and since the Sun was at the center of the world, it also served to search for the Four Ancient Immortals."

"To prevent anyone from entering the Sun, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord set up a seal."

"After Great Qian fell and Great Yu rose, Emperor Wu Yao of Yu, unaware of the purpose of the seal on the Sun, disliked the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s monopolization of the Sun and set his own seal, effectively trapping both the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller within the Sun."

"In fact, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord was not adept at Sealing Skills; his capability to set the seal relied on the accumulated Power of Faith."

"Violently breaking the seal would reveal the existence of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller, which was not conducive to reducing the influence of the Power of Faith."

"To escape the Sun, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller spent a hundred thousand years studying the seal set by Wu Yao and finally found a flaw. They could leave the Sun without breaking the seal."

Fairy Eternity understood and summarized with her hands in her sleeves: "So what you’re saying is that Emperor Yu and The Controller, these two fools, were locked inside their own home by Wu Yao, and it took them a hundred thousand years to jimmy the lock open?"

Lu Yang: "..."

The enmity between Emperor Yu and Wu Yao was not small indeed.
